पूर्णैं वर्षसहर्ये तु परितुँये महेश्वरः ।

वरेण छंदयामास काव्यं प्रति मनोहरः ॥ 35 ॥

In this way one thousand years passed duly, the God Mahādeva was very pleased and addressed Śukrācārya that He had come to give him boons that he desired.

ईश्व उवाच

यच्छ किंचिदपि ब्रह्मान्विधते घपुनन्दन ।

प्रतिपश्यसि यासर्वे तच्च वाच्यं न कस्यचित् ॥ 36 ॥

सर्वाधिभावकत्वेन भविष्यिन र संशयः ।

अवध्यः सर्वभृतानां प्रजेशश्र द्विजोन्म ॥ 37 ॥

The God Mahādeva said: Whatever there exists in this universe, whatever you see with your eyes and whatever can not be described in words, you will be the lord of all these and the conqueror of all. There is no doubt in this. You can not be killed by any creature; you will be the ruler of all beings and will be reckoned as the best of the Brāhmanas.

व्यास उवाच

एवं दत्ता वराऊघुभुतवैवांतराधीयत ।

काव्यसामय संवैश्व जवन्नी वाक्यमवबीत् ॥ 38 ॥

Vyāsa said: The God Mahādeva disappeared then and there, after granting him the boons. Then Śukrācārya saw Jayanti and said to her: O lady of beautiful hips! Who and whose daughter art thou? What is the desire in your mind? What for you have come here? O one of beautiful thighs! what is your duty?

कासिस कल्यासि सुश्रीणि बूहि किं ते चिकीक्षित् ।

किमर्थमिहु संप्राप्ता कार्य बद वरोके मे ॥ 39 ॥

किं बांछिस कांप्यष्ठ दुकंचे चेसुलोचने ।

प्रौतिसीय त्वलृतेनोषा वरं वरय सुहते ॥ 40 ॥

O beautiful eyed! I am very pleased with all that you have done for me. What do you want? O one

Book IV Chapter XII

of good vows! Ask boons from me; I will grant them even if they be difficult.

ततः सा तु पुर्न प्रह जयन्ती मुदितान्ना ।

चिकीर्षितं मे भगवंतनसा ज्ञानुमर्हसि ॥ 4 1 ॥

On hearing this, Jaiyanti's face became bright with joy; she said: whatever I desire, you know that already by force of your Tapasyā.

व्यास उवाच

ज्ञातं मया तथापि तवं बृहि यन्मनसेसितम् ।

कंरोपि सर्वेथा भर्त्र प्रीतो5स्म परिचर्यय ॥ 42 ॥

The Kārya said: Your mental desires I know; still mention them yourself particularly; I will do good to you in any way you like; I am pleased with your services.

जंदेल्यावर

गऊकस्याहं सुता ब्रह्मियात तुभ्यं समपिता ।

जयंती नामतश्राहं जयंतावरजा मुने ॥ 4 3 ॥

सकामास्मि तस्य विश्वेभीविधोबीछितंकुभेयुभा ।

रंस्ये तस्या महाभाग धर्मतः प्रीतिपुर्वकम् ॥ 4 4 ॥

Jayanti said: O Brāhmīn! I am the daughter of Indra and I am the younger of my brother Jayanta. Father has given me over to you; I now like to live with you. Kindly fulfil my desires now. O blessed one! You better take me and enjoy me according to Dharma. This is my desire.

गृक्र उवाच

मया सह त्वं सुश्रीण दूरा वर्षाणि भामिनि ।

सर्वभूतिरूपया च रमवेह यदृष्ठया ॥ 4 5 ॥

Šukrācārya said: O thou of large and handsome hips! Better enjoy with me for ten years, according to your liking, without being visible to any body.

व्यास उवाच

एवमुक्ता गृहं गत्वा जयंल्या: पाणिगमुढ़त् ।

तया सहासर्वेह्या द्रग वर्षिणि भागंशः ॥ 46 ॥

अजुष्यः सर्वभृतानां मायया संवृताः प्रपुः ।

दैत्यात्मतागानं क्रुवा कृतार्थ मान्सर्युतम् ॥ 47 ॥

अभिजमगुरुं तस्य मुदिताते दिवूक्ष्वतः ।

नाप्यवत्रममाणों ते जयेत्या सह संयुतम् ॥ 48 ॥

Vyāśya said: Thus saying, Šukrācārya went to his home and, according to religious observances, married Jayanti and lived with her for ten years under the shade of Māyā and unobserved by any body. On the other hand, the Daityas, hearing that Šukrācārya returned home successful getting his desired Mantra, were very glad and went to his house to pay their respects to him. But Šukra was then living with Jayanti; hence the Asuras could not see him.

तदा विमनसः सर्वे जाता भग्नोट्यामृतः ते ।

चिंतयारतिद्वीनाश्य वीक्षमाणाः पुनः पुनः ॥ 49 ॥

Then they got very much dejected, sorry and were drooped in spirits; they searched for again and again.

अर्द्धवा तं तु संवृतं प्रतिजनमुवंशगतम् ।

स्वगृहान्दैत्यवयासेते चिताविषाय भयानुतः ॥ 50 ॥

Not being able to see Šukra under the shade of Māyā, the Daityas were very sorry and got afraid and then returned to their own residences.

यमाणं तथा नात्वा शकः प्रोक्षितं गुकम् ।

ब्रह्माति महाभागं चिकं कर्तैल्यमतः परम् ॥ 51 ॥

On the other hand, the Devas, knowing that Šukra was holding intercourses with Jayanti, Indra, the God of Devas, spoke to the Deva Guru Brhaşpati. O Guru! Advise us what to do under the present circumstances and relieve us from the impending difficulties.

गच्छधा दानवान्बह्मभायया त्वं प्रतोभय ।

अस्माक कुरु कार्य त्वं बुद्धा संहित्य मान्दे ॥ 52 ॥

O Brāhmaṇa! Better go to day to the Dānavas and do that by which our honour may be maintained. You better put the Dānavas under some charm by your magical spell and think and do good to us.

तच्छूला वचनं काव्यं सम्भाणं सुरंसृन्ति ।

जाला तद्धुपास्थाय दैत्यान्याति यद्य गुरुः ॥ 53 ॥

On hearing Indra's words and knowing that Śukra is now enjoying with Jayanti, Brhaṣpati went to the Dānavas, putting on the appearance of Śukrācārya.

गत्या त्रातीथकत्वाउसौ दानवास्मुपाक्रवत् ।

आगातालेउपुगा: सर्वे द्वशु: काव्यमप्रतः ॥ 54 ॥

Going to the Demons, he called on then very politely and sweetly. The Asuras said that Śukrācārya had come in presence of them.

प्रणाय संशिता: सर्वे काव्यं मत्वाजित्मोहिताः ।

न विदुर्ते गुरोगियां काव्यरूपविषाथविनीम् ॥ 55 ॥

The Demons were very glad and bowed down before Śukrācārya and could not make out that he was the false Śukrācārya, under the Māgic charm of Brhaspati.

तानुवाच गुरः काव्यरूप: प्रच्छनमायया ।

स्वागतं मय याज्ञानां प्राणोर्भं चै तिहान ये ॥ 56 ॥

अहं वो बोधियधामि विश्वां प्राणातामाययां ।

तपसा तोषितः शंभुर्यामकल्याणहेते ॥ 57 ॥

Then the false Śukrācārya asked the welfare of the Daityas? and said: I went so long for your good; I performed severe tapasyā and satisfied the God Śambhu and acquired from him the Mantra, the

sacred knowledge, and that I will frankly explain to you.

तच्छूचा प्रीतमनसो जाताते दानवोत्तभाः ।

कृतकार्य गृहं मत्वा जडयुले विमोहिताः ॥ 58 ॥

On hearing this, the Demons were very pleased and, knowing that the Guru had been successful, were filled with joy.

प्रणेपुले मुदा युक्ता निरातांका गतत्यक्षाः ।

देवेध्यश्र भयं चत्वन्ता तस्युः सर्वे निरपायाः ॥ 59 ॥

इति शीमदेवीभागवते महापुराणे चतुर्बेकन्चे

द्वादशोध्याः ॥ 12 ॥

They saluted him with joy and began to live quietly and freely and without pain, having got rid of fear the Devas.

Here ends the Twelth Chapter in the Fourth Book of Śrimaddevibhagāvatam, the Mahāpurānam of 18,000 verses by Maharṣī Veda Vyāsa on Bhargu's curse and the dialogue between Śukrācāryā and the Daityas.

Šrimaddevibhāgavatam

CHAPTER XIII

On Cheating the Daityas

राजोवाच

किं कृतं गुर्गणां पशुब्राजूरूषण वर्तां ।

छनेनेव हि दैत्यानां पौरोहित्येन धीमता ॥ १ ॥

The king said: What did the intelligent Brhaṣpati do after he had assumed falsely the appearance of Śukrācārya, and lived there as the spiritual guide of the Demons.

गुरुः सुराणामिनि सर्वविद्यानिशितथा ।

सुतांगीरस एवासी स कबं छलकूम्पिनः ॥ 2 ॥

O Muni! Brhapsati is the Guru of the Devas; he also devotes his time in studying the Vedas; and is the ocean of all knowledge; he is the son of the Maharsi Angirā and he is himself a Muni. Endorsed with all these good qualifications, how could he deceive the Demons.

धर्मशक्षेपु सर्वेषु सत्यधर्मय कारणम् ।

कथितं मुनिभियैन परमात्यासि लभ्यते ॥ 3 ॥

वाय्यसितसथा मिथ्या वक्ता चेहानवान्यति ।

कः सत्यक्ता संसारे भविष्यति गृहाश्रामी ॥ 4 ॥

In all the religious Sāstras, Truth is declared to be the essence of Dharma; and the Supreme Self is attained through Truth, so the wise sages say. How can we expect an ordinary householder to be true when such a man as Brhaspati takes recourse to falsehood with the Demons.

आहाराद्धिक भोज्यं बहाण्डविभवेउपि न ।

तत्वं मुनयो मिथ्या प्रवर्तैने कर्थ मुने ॥ ५ ॥

If one acquires, as one's wealth, the whole Universe, still one does not require anything more than what is required in feeding one's belly; how is it that Brhaspati could speak falsehood merely for the sake of his belly?

शब्दप्रमाणमुच्छेदे शिषाधावे गत न किम् ।

छलकमप्रमुत्ते वारक्षितारांत गृती कथम् ॥ 6 ॥

O Muni! The words sung by the ancient virtuous and respectable sages were true and had their corresponding objects by those words; now they

Book IV Chapter XIII

employed the term Śiṣṭa meaning that there were virtuous, respectable persons as denoted by them. When Brhaṣpati can even commit such condemnable deceitful acts and speak falsehood, we can expect no virtuous respectable persons in the world. Where then do you find the Śiṣṭa persons, denoted by the word Śiṣṭa, sung by the ancient sages? The word Śiṣṭa is now meaningless!

देवा: सत्त्वसमुद्धुता राजसा मानवा: स्मृता: ।

तिर्वास्तमासा: प्रोक्ता उत्सत्ती भूनिषि: क्रिल १ ७ ॥

The Devas are sprung from the Sāttvic qualities, men from Rajisaci qualities and birds, etc. from the Tāmasic qualities.

अमराणां गुरु: साक्षात्म्यायावाटी स्वयं यदि ।

तदा कः सत्यवक्ता स्याध्वासस्तामास: पुनः ॥ 8 ॥

When the Guru of the Immortals, the incarnate of Sāttvik qualities, can become a liar, how can one expect those who are Rājasik Tāmasik to follow rigorously the truth?

व्य स्थितितस्य धर्मस्य सन्देड़ोयं मामात्नः ।

का गति: सर्वेन्तूनां पिथापुते जातयते ॥ 9 ॥

Oh! This Trilokī is all pierced with falschool! Where is the Religion! And what will be the ultimate goal of all these creatures!

हरिबंहा रणीकान्तस्वाधाउ्ये सुरस्वभा: ।

सर्वे छलिधी दह्म मनुयाणां च का कथा ॥ 10 ॥

When Bhagavān Hari, Brahmā, Indra and the best of the Devas when all can betake to protest, fraud and trickery and show cleverness in them; what to speak of men!

कामाबोधासिन्ताप लोधीपहत्तचेतस: ।

छल दक्षा: सुरा: सर्वे मुनयशु तपोधना: ॥ 11 ॥

वसिष्ठो बामदेवश्व शिक्षापित्रो गुरुरूषना ।

एते पारपततः काउत्र गतिभर्मेय मानव ॥ 12 ॥

O Giver of honour! When all the Devas, Vasistha, Vāmadeva, Visvāmitra, Bhraspati and other ascetic Munis get themselves overpowered by lust and anger, when their intelligence gets destroyed by covetuousness and avarice, when they are addicted to vices and are expert in fraud, pretext

and trickery, then what fate, alas! can you expect of Dharma and what help is there of any religious persons?

इन्ट्रोसिनशून्ट्रमा वेधा: परदाराभिलम्यता: ।

आर्यत्वं भुवनेचेषु स्थितं कृत्र मुने वद ॥ 13 ॥

Alas! Indra, Agni, Moon, and Brahmā when these get overpowered by the strong influence of lust, are in illicit love with others wives, where is the goodness and virtuous behaviour in this Triloki?

वचनं कस्य मन्त्यमुपदेशप्रियासनच ।

सर्वे लोपाधिभूतास्ते देवाशु मुनयस्तथा ॥ 14 ॥

O Sinless One! To whom, then, can we look upon as our spiritual guide and our advice and law givers when all the Devas and Muni are corrupt with avarice?

व्यास उवाच

किं विश्यु: किं शिवो बहुत मघवा किं बृहस्यति: ।

देहवान् प्रभवत्येव विकारैः संयुतः सदा ॥ 15 ॥

Vyāsa said: O king! Be he Indra, Brhaspati, Brahmā, Viṣṇu or Maheśa whoever is embodied or will put on bodies, he will have to be in touch with the previously mentioned Aharūkāra, and covetuousness and other vices due to name and form.

रागी विध्य: शिको रागी बह्ञापि रागसंयुतः।

“रायाविकमकुत्वं वै न करोति नाराधि ।”

रागवानपि चातुर्गविद्धेड इव लक्ष्यते ॥ १ ६ ॥

O king! Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Maheśa are all attached to sensual objects; and what improper and sinful actions there can be that cannot be committed by persons devoted to sensual objects! सम्प्रावे सुक्टे सेठीप गुणैः सम्वायते किल ।

कारणाद्रहितं कार्यं कवं भवितुर्महैति ॥ 17 ॥

It is through cleverness and fraud that any one devoted to sensual objects can easily make oneself as cleverly free from Māyā; but when difficulty comes, then his trickery gets discovered and the respective qualities hidden in him are brought to bear their respective results. Know, then, the three

qualities to be conjointly the cause of all these actions; as without any cause, no action gets visible.

इद्यादौनां च सर्वेषां गुणा एव हि कारणम् ।

पश्रविश्वसमुद्धता देहासेलां च नाच्यथा ॥ 18 ॥

These discrepancies in the case of Brahmā and others are caused by the three qualities; their bodies are all created from Pradhāna Mahat and the other 25 Tattvas. (essences).

काले मरणधार्मिते सन्देहः को3उ ते नृप ।

परोपदेशे विस्पर्धे शिषा: सर्व पवित्त च ॥ 19 ॥

विलुत्तिहिंशेशेषे। स्ककार्ये समुपस्थिते ।

कामः क्रोभत्धला लोभग्रोहाङ्कुरासस्तः ॥ 20 ॥

O king! Brahmā and others are subject to death; then how can you doubt on other things? In advising others, everybody gives, as it were, good and virtuous advices; but the burden falls upon their own heads; they fall off from their advices and act according to their hidden natures; then they yield to lust, anger, envy, egoism and fascination.

देहावा-कः परित्यक्त्मीशो भवति तान्युनः ।

संसारोउयं महाराज सदैवैर्विविधः स्मृतः ॥ 21 ॥

No one who is embodied can get rid of passions, born of the three qualities. O king! Thus the Triloki goes, is the saying of the Maharsis.

नान्यथा प्रभवत्येव रुभाग्रुभमयः किल ।

कदाचिद्वगाविच्यानुस्तपशृति दाकृणम् ॥ 22 ॥

कदाचिद्विषायान्यान्तिनतीसे सुराधिधः ।

कदाचितु स्मारूरस्त्रिक्तः परमेश्वरः ॥ 23 ॥

रमते किल वैकुणले तद्वासस्तरणो विभुः ।

कदाचिष्ठानवैः सारधे सुखं परमदाकाणम् ॥ 24 ॥

करीत कर्णासिम्युस्तद्वाणपीडिते धृप्यम् ।

कदाचिजयमानोति देवालसोप्रि पराजयम् ॥ 25 ॥

सुखदुःखाधिपृतीउसी भवलेवेव न संशयः ।

गेपे गोते कदाचिष्ठे योगनिद्रासमावृतः ॥ 26 ॥

This Trilokī, auspicious, inauspicious, mixed, never gets any serious change; its nature remains always uniform. See Bhagavān Viṣṇu sometimes practises severe asceticism; Indra, the lord of the Devas some times follows the practices of religious sacrifices. Again you find Viṣṇu Bhagavān, full of

youth, fond of the Līlā, enjoying the company of Ramā in Vaikuntha; some times He is the ocean of mercy, is fighting dreadful battles with the Demons and being severely afflicted with their clusters of arrows; some times he gains victories, some times he gets defeat through the irony of Fate; thus he gets undoubtedly pleasures and pains.

काले जागर्ति विश्रामा स्थायाप्रतिविधितः ।

गर्वें बढ़ा हरिश्छित इन्जाडा वे सुरातथा ॥ 27 ॥

मुनवश्व तिविनारीः स्वायुषो विवरान्ति हि ।

निशावसाने संजाते जनस्थावरजंगम् ॥ 28 ॥

घियते नात्र सन्देहो नृप किश्छलदापि च ।

स्वायुषो-ने पचजाड़ा: क्षयमूच्छिति पार्थिं ॥ 29 ॥

Oking! some time Nārāyaṇa draws all the worlds into his belly and takes his yogic sleep on the thousand headed serpent Śeṣa and again he gets himself awakened by Prakṛti. O king! Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Maheśa, Indra, the Devas, and Munis all of them, live up to the limit of their ordained time and when the time of Pralaya the Universal dissolution, ends, this whole Universe, moving and nonmoving, again comes into existence as before; there is no doubt in this. O king, at the expiry of the ordained time, Brahmā and all others will die, no doubt.

प्रभवन्ति पुनर्विष्णुहराशकाय: सुग: ।

तस्माल्कामादिकान्यावादैह्वान्यतिप्रदते ॥ ३० ॥

नात्र ते विमयम: काव्यं: कदापिचिप पार्थिति ।

संसारोयं तु सन्दिधाः कामक्रोधादिपिनूर ॥ ३१ ॥

Again, in due course, Brahmā, Viṣṇu, and Maheśa and the other Devas come out and assume bodies and get all the passions, lust, etc. as ordained. O King! You need not be astonished; this Trilokī always goes on accompanied by lust, anger, etc.

तुर्वभस्तिद्विनमुक्तः पुरुषः परमार्थवित् ।

यो विवेपौहं संसारे स दारात्र क्रौतलपि ॥ 32 ॥

विमुक्तः सर्वसोपेध्ये विवरत्याविगकतः ।

तस्मादुर्हस्यतेभिरां शगशनातिभिता पुनः ॥ 33 ॥

गुरुभालाभिता भार्यां तथा भातुर्थीवासः ।

एवं संसारचक्रोंस्मव्रगतोपादिविधित्विधृतः ॥ 34 ॥

Book IV Chapter XIII

Persons free from lust, anger and other passions are very rare in this world. He who is afraid of this world does not marry, and thus being free from the attachments to any worldly objects, becomes free and roams fearless. The Moon stole away the wife of Brhaşpati, and Brhaşpati himself stole away the wife of his younger brothers. Thus in this wheel of Sanisāra, all the creatures are ever passionate with attachment avarice, etc.

गत्थस्यं च समास्थाय कर्थं मुक्तो घेवेत्रः ।

तस्मासर्वप्रयतेनं हित्वा संसारसारताम् ॥ 35 ॥

The householder can never expect to obtain freedom. Therefore those who want to be free, should carefully relinquish the idea of the stability of the world and worship the Eternal Mother Full and Sat, Cit and Ānandam.

आत्यायेमहेशानी सचिद्वानन्द्रविणीम् ।

तन्मायागणतश्छं जगदेतच्चाराम् ॥ ३६ ॥

This world, moving and unmoving, O Maheśanī, rolls in madness, overpowered by Her Māyā.

प्रभत्यमन्तवलर्ष मदिरामपतवज्ञ ।

तस्या आगधानेपैव गुणानसर्विन्धयुत च ॥ ३७ ॥

Intelligent persons worshipping Her, trample upon the three qualities and become free. O king! No other Path exists for Freedom.

मुक्ति भजेत मतिमाजान्यः पंधास्थितः परः ।

आग्रायिता महेशगानी न यावकुकृतो कृपण। ३ ३ १ ||

तावद्ध्वेल्पुं कमाकाकोयोर्जित दया युतः ।

करणासागरामेतां पंजतेन्स्मादायया ॥ ३ ९ ॥

Until one gets the Grace from the Maheśānī, one never gets happiness. True mercy is not found anywhere else but from Her. Then one should worship the All merciful, being of pure heart. For Her worship leads to freedom, even in this bodyhood.

यस्यास्तु भजनेनैव जीवन्मुत्कवमरन्तु ।

मानुध्यं तुर्वर्भ प्राय्य सेविता न महेश्वरीं ॥ 40 ॥

He who getting a human frame fails to worship Maheśānī, gets down from the highest rung of the ladder. This is my opinion.

निःशिणकाप्राप्तिता अथ इन्येव विवहें ।

अहूक्राप्तुं विश्वं गुणावसम्वित्यम् ॥ 41 ॥

असत्येनापि सम्वद्धं मृच्यते कायमन्यथा ।

हिला सर्व ततः सर्वे: संसेल्या घुवनेश्धी ॥ 42 ॥

This Universe, composed of the three qualities, is encompassed with Ahankāra and fastened to untruth; therefore freedom can never be expected without the worship of That Potent Goddess. O Muni! O king! Renounce every worldly object and serve the Goddess Bhuvanesvari; this is the highest duty of all.

राजोवाच

किं कृतं गुरुणा तत्र काव्यकपधेण च ।

कदा शुक्र: समायाततन्मे बृहि पितामहं ॥ 43 ॥

The king said: What did, then, the Devaguru do in the disguise of Śukrācārya? And when did the real Śukrācārya come there? O respected Muni! Speak on these points.

व्यास उवाच

भ्युण राजन्यव्ययामि यत्कृतं गुरुणा तदा ।

कृत्वा काव्यस्वरूपं च प्रच्छेने महात्मना ॥ 44 ॥

Vyāsa said: Please hear what the disguised Brhaṣpati in the shape of Śukrācārya did afterwards.

गुरुणा बोधिता दैत्या मत्वा काव्य स्वकं गुरुम् ।

विश्वास परमं कृत्वा बध्वुत्स्नमयास्ताद ॥ 45 ॥

The demons were made to understand clearly by Brhaşpati; and then they took him for Śukrācārya and placed implicit faith on him and began to think of him and him alone.

विद्यार्थे ग्रणत भातु भगुं मत्वाउमोगैहिताः ।

गुरुणा वियतख्याने तोभाको वा न मुवाहि ॥ 46 ॥

The Daityas, enchanted and deceived by the magic of Brhaşpati, took now his refuge for acquiring the knowledge from him, since they mistook him for Śukrācārya. Who is there that is not enchanted by the idea of gaining something?

दूरश्वर्णिके काले सम्पूर्णसमये तदा ।

जर्यवा सह क्रीडित्वा काव्यो याज्ञानचित्यत् ॥ 47 ॥

On the other hand, when the term of ten years was over, Šukrācārya, the real Guru of the Daityas, ceased enjoying Jayanti and began to remember his disciples, the Daityas.

आशया माम मार्गे ते प्रध्यन्तः संस्थिताः किल ।

गतला तान्ये प्रपयेउई घाज्ञानतिनययातुगन् ॥ 4 8 ॥

He now began to think that “my disciples, the Daityas, are expecting every instant my return; and I would now go and see them, bewildered with fear.

मा देवेध्यो भयं तेषां मदूकानां भवेदिति ।

सँत्रिल बुद्धिमास्थाय जयंत्रीं प्रत्युवाच इ ॥ ४९ ॥

देवानेवोघसंवर्ति पुरा मे चाहलोचने ।

समयलेउछ स्यमूर्णं जातोरअं दशाविषिकः ॥ ५० ॥

तस्माद्छामहं देवि रूढुं याज्ञान्सुमध्ये ।

पुरेनेवागमियामि तवान्तिकमनुद्रतः ॥ ५१ ॥

They are my devotees and I ought to do such that they might not be afraid of the Devas." And then he exclaimed to Jayanti, "O beautiful one! Let my sons take the shelter of the Gods; your term of ten years is today over; I now go therefore, to see my disciples; soon I will again come to you.

तथैति तमुवाचाय जयन्ती धार्यितमा ।

यथेहं गच्छ धर्मेन न ते धर्मं विलोपेचे ॥ 52 ॥

"Be it so", replied Jayanti, the best of those who know religion, "you can go where you like; I am not to destroy your Dharma."

तद्युवा वयनं काव्यो जाग्य वरितस्ततः ।

अप्यशूनवानां च पार्थे वाच्यति तदा ॥ 53 ॥

छरूक्षपरं सीयां बोधयन्तं छलेन तान् ।

जैनं धर्मं कृते खेने यज्ञनिद्वपरं तथा ॥ 54 ॥

Hearing these words, Šukrācārya went hurriedly to the Demons and saw the Devaguru Brhaspati sitting before them in the guise of Šukrācārya. He was explaining to him the Jaina doctrines, compiled by himself and finding fault with the act of envy, taking revenge and killing and cursing the sacrifices, etc.

भो देवरिप्वः सत्यं ब्रवीमि भवतां हितम् ।

अहिंसा परमो धर्मों हेतव्या ह्राततायिनः ॥ 55 ॥

He was telling them “O Enemies of Gods! Truly, I am telling you words that will, no doubt, prove good to you. Non-killing is the highest virtue; even the enemies ought never to be killed.

छिडीभोगतरैवैदे दर्शिनं हिंसनं पशोः ।

जिहास्वादपरै: काममहिंदेव परा मता ॥ 56 ॥

It is the Brāhmanas, addicted to enjoyments and pleasures of senses, who want to satisfy their tastes and pleasures that are found in the Vedas injunctions to kill animals; but there is no virtue higher than non-killing animals.

एर्वधिधानि वाक्यानि वेटगाछ्चपाराणि च ।

बुवाणे भूमकाकर्णे विक्सोजसी गुणो: सून: ॥ 57 ॥

चिन्त्यामास मनसा मम ढ्रेष्यो गुरुः किल ।

वझिता: किल धूतेन याज्ञा मे नात्र संशयः ॥ 58 ॥

O king! Sukrācārya was perfectly astonished to hear Brhaşpati, the Guru of the Devas, speaking against the Vedas and began to think that "Brhaşpati is certainly my enemy. My disciples have been duped by this cheat; there is no doubt in this.

प्रियतोषं पायबीजं वै नरक्रहागूर्जितम् ।

गुरुत्यन्ते बृते प्रेरितो येन पायमा ॥ 59 ॥

Fie to Avarice! It is the seed of sin; very strong and the veritable gate to hell; Brhaşpati, even, the Guru of the Devas, is speaking lies, bound under the influence of this heinous avarice!

प्रमाणं चत्वं यस्य तोप्रेप पाखण्डधारकः ।

गुरुः सुराणां सर्वैंधं धर्मशास्त्रवतर्कः ॥ 60 ॥

Oh! What wonder is this that the Guru of the Devas, who is the promulgator of all the religious Śāstras and whose word is accepted as the final decision, is now expounding the doctrines of atheists.

किं किं न तपते लोभामलिनीकृतमानसः ।

अन्योदपि गुरुरूपेवं जातः पारखण्डपिछतः ॥ ६१ ॥

When Brhaşpati can become the expounder of atheistic doctrines, impelled by covetuousness: what to speak of those whose minds are not pure and whose intelligence is not sharp?

Book IV Chapter XIV

<table><tr><td>शैलुपचेघितं सर्वे परिगूहा ब्रिजोत्तमः ।वंचययतिसम्मुजादैन्यान् याज्ञान्माप्यसी ॥ 6 2 ॥इति श्रीमद्वैभागवते महापुराणे चतुर्षकन्येत्रयोद्धारो5यायः ॥ 1 7 3 ॥</td></tr></table>

This Deva Guru, though a Brāhmin, is acting to-day like a rogue, wanting to take away all and

is deceiving my disciples the Daityas, who have been confounded by his magic.

Here ends the Thirteenth Chapter in the Fourth Book of Śrīmaddevibhāgavatam, the Mahapurānam of 18,000 verses on cheating the Daityas by Maharṣi Veda Vyāsa.

Book IV Chapter XIV

CHAPTER XIV

On the Daity's Getting Back their Sukrācārya

व्यास उवाच

इति सिंह्येल मनसा तानुवाच हस्रिवः ।

विश्रिता मत्सक्षरेण दैत्याः किं गुरुणा किल ।। । ॥

Vyāsa said: Thus arguing in his mind, Śukrācārya smilingly said to the Daityas: O Daityas! what for have you all been cheated by Brhaṣpati, the Guru of the Devas, in my guise.

अर्ह काव्यो गुरुकाशं देकाकार्यप्रासधकः ।

अनेन बक्षिता यूर्यं महाज्ञा नात संशयः ॥ २ ॥

I am Śukrācārya; you are my disciples; this man is Brhaṣpati, wanting to serve the Gods. He has cheated you, there is no doubt in this.

भा ब्रद्धयं चोरोस्पायां द्राधिकोरयं महाकृति: ।

अनुपाख्त मां याज्ञास्त्वर्जनैं बृह्मश्यति ॥ ३ ॥

This vain villainous person has assumed my form; do not put the least faith in his words. O Daityas! You are disciples, follow unto me; leave this Brhaşpati, vain and arrogant.

दूर्याकष्टं चबरतस्य दूर्या ती सूर्शी पुनः ।

विस्यमं परंं जगुः काव्योउयिति निश्रिताः ॥ 4 ॥

The Daityas, on hearing his words were struck with wonder at the resemblance of the two personages and came to the conclusion that “the person just come is the real Śukrācārya.

स तान्त्रीय सुसंधानानुक्राविस्युवाच ह ।

गुर्वाँं चंचचायेव मदूधोरं बृह्यस्ति: ॥ 5 ॥

Then Brhaşpati, the false Śukrācārya explained to the Daityas in plain and enchanting words that “the second man just come is the Deva Guru Brhaşpati; he has come here in my guise. His object is to cheat you.

प्राणो वश्यितुं युधान्देवकार्यार्थसिद्धये ।

मा विश्वासं वचस्यय कुठघं दैत्यसत्तमाः ॥ 6 ॥

He has come here to cheat you and serve the purpose of the Devas; do not believe in his words.

प्राणता विश्वा मया शफभोर्याभनधायपाति ताम् ।

देवेथ्यो विजयं नूनं कारिष्यामि न संशयः ॥ 7 ॥

What knowledge I have acquired from the God Śarībhū, I am teaching it to you; I will make you, no doubt, victorious in the battle with the Devas."

इति श्रुत्वा गुरोविंयं काव्यरूपधरस्य ते ।

विश्वांस परमं जगुः काव्योर्यमिति निश्यान् ॥ 8 ॥

Thus the Daityas, hearing the words of the false Śuśārkārya, thought the false one to be real and placed implicit reliance in his words.

काव्येन बहुधा तत्र बोधिताः कित दानवाः ।

बुबुधूनं गुगोर्मायामीहिताः कालपयंयात् ॥ 9 ॥

The real Śukrākārya, then explained them as much as he could; but the Daityas, owing to the magic of the false Śukrārya and to the wonderful effect of time, did not hear his words.

एवं ते निश्छयं कृत्ता ततो भागवमब्रुन्त् ।

अयं गुरुर्नों धर्मातना बुद्धिद्ध हिते तः ॥ 10 ॥

The Daityas thus convinced said to the real Śukrāśraya, “this man before us is our Guru for our welfare enlightenment, he is the foremost religious Śukrāśraya; for ten continuously he is giving us advices. You are not our Guru; you appear fictitious and false; you better leave this place and go away.”

दूण बर्षीण सततमयं नः शारित भागवः ।

गच्छ तर्व कृहको भासि नास्याके गुरुपन्तु ॥ 11 ॥

Šrimaddevibhāgavatam

The Dull brained Daityas repeatedly told Šukrācārya the reproachful words and bowed down to the false Šukrācārya, and, saluting him, gladly welcomed him as their Guru.

इतुसुक्ता भार्ची मुहा निर्भर्य व पुनः पुनः ।

जगुहुस्तं गृहं प्रीत्या प्रगिपत्याचीभवाहा च ॥ १ २ ॥

काव्यस्तु तनयान्दूर्वा चुकोषाय शरणाय च ।

दैत्याविचोधिताभ्यमा गुरुणा चातिविहितान् ॥ १ ३ ॥

यस्मान्यया बोधिता वे गृह्वीयुर्नं च मे वशः ।

तस्मान्नरस्थेना वै परणभवमावस्यय ॥ १ ४ ॥

The real Śukrācārya, seeing the Daityas exceedingly attached to Brhaşpati, the Guru of the Devas, and deceived by his words, cursed them out of anger and said: "As you have not taken my words though I have explained every thing to you, you would lose your knowledge and get defeat.

मद्ववापकर्ते कामं स्वल्ये काले ह्वायाययः ।

तदास्य कपटं सर्वे परित्रां भविष्यति ॥ 15 ॥

As you have shown towards me, you will get the fruits of it at an early date and will then understand the deceitful behaviors of that Deva Guru.

इत्युक्तवासी जगाभागु भागवेः कोधेसंयुतः ।

ब्रह्म्यातिपूर्वे प्राप्य तस्थो तत्र समाहितः ॥ 16 ॥

Vyāsa said: Thus saying Śukrācārya hurriedly went away, in furiated with anger. Bhraspati was glad and remained there with his mind calm.

ततः शप्तानुर्जाला दैत्यांस्ताम्पारीयण है ।

जगमा तरसा तव्कता स्वस्तयं स्व विधाय च ॥ 17 ॥

गत्वोबाच तदा शकं कृतं कार्य मधुवम् ।

शप्ता: गुहैमा ते द्विता मया तवक्ता: पुनः किल ॥ 18 ॥

निराधारा: कृता नृनं वातधं सुरससभा: ।

संग्रामाय महाभाग शापद्धया मया कृताः ॥ 19 ॥

Bhraspati then knowing the Daityas cursed by Śukrācārya, assumed his real appearance, left that place, and hurriedly returned to Indra. He began to say “I have undoubtedly succeeded in my undertaking; the Daityas have been cursed and left by me too. They are now helpless; so, O Good Suras! I have made them cursed, you would better now try to fight with them.”

इति श्रुत्वा गुरोर्वस्यं मघवा मुदामपल्न् ।

जड्धूश सुरा: सर्वे प्रतिपुज्य बृह्मयतिम् ॥ 20 ॥

संग्रामाय मिति चक्रः संविद्वार्य विष्य: पुनः ।

निर्वंगुर्मिततः सर्वे दानवाभिमयुका: सुरा: ॥ 21 ॥

Indra heard their Guru and became very glad; all the other Devas were glad and worshipped him. They held another cabinet, secret council, how to fight with the Daityas; and, then, all uniting together marched out in battle against the Asuras.

सुरान्समुहाताज्ञाला कृतोद्योगान्यहावलान् ।

अन्तहिंतं गुरुं चैव बथयुधिंहतयान्वितः ॥ 22 ॥

Seeing that the powerful Devas marching towards them ready to fight and knowing their false Guru had fled, the Daityas became very anxious.

परस्पमधोचुले मोहितातस्य मायया ।

सम्पसाठो महात्मा च यातोउसी नष्टमानस: ॥ 23 ॥

They told each other: Alas! We were enchanted by the Devaguru; the highscould Śukrācārya angrily left us; now it is our incumbent duty to satisfy him.

वड्यायिता गत पापो गुरुः कपटपिण्डतः ।

भ्रातृस्कीतंभनः प्रायो मलिनोउन्तबिकेः सुचि: ॥ 24 ॥

That vicious, dirty inside and pure outside, hypocrite Pundit Deva Guru, who used to go to his brother's wife, has really cheated and has quitted us.

किं कुंना: क्व च गच्छाम: कर्थ काव्यं प्रक्रोधितम् ।

कुर्विमहि सुहायांष प्रसनं इद्यानसम् ॥ 25 ॥

What are we to do now? where to go? How to satiate now the anger of Śukra so that he might be glad and help us.

इति संचित्त ने सर्वे मितिता भयंकंपिताः ।

प्रहारं पुतः कृत्वा जगमुते भारपं पुः ॥ 26 ॥

Thus pondering over they all unitedly, shuddering with fear, again went to Śukrācārya, keeping Prahlāda in front of them.

प्राणेशुधगृते तस्य मुनिर्मान्यतता ।

भार्यवत्नावाचा रोषसंरक्तलोचनः ॥ 27 ॥

They all bowed down at the feet of their Guru;

Book IV Chapter XIV

Šukra remained silent; then, bursting with anger and with eyes red, told them.

मया प्रबोधिता चूयं मोहिता गुरुभायया ।

न गृहीत चंचो योग्यं तदा याज्या हिते शृणि ॥ 28 ॥

You were all warned by me that you were being cheated by the Māyā of Brhaṣpati; you did not take my word, as worth hearing, though unselfish, pure and leading to your welfare.

तदास्वागीतश्रहं भवद्धिस्तद्वर्श गतेः ।

प्राणं नूनं मदोभनैमावमानजं फलम् ॥ 29 ॥

Rather you were influenced by him and infatuated with vanity, you insulted me; now you will have to bear undoubtedly the effects of that Karma.

तत्र गच्छत सद्भूषा घजासी कपटाकृति: ।

वंक्कः सुराकार्याँनी नाहं तद्विद्ध बझकः ॥ ३० ॥

You are now vitiated from the path of your welfare; go now where that disguised cheat resides for the welfare of the Gods; know me not to be a cheat like him.

व्यास उवाच

एवं बुवंत शुक्र तु वाक्यसन्दिशया गिरा ।

प्रहादस्ते तदोवाच गृहीता चरणों ततः ॥ ३ १ ॥

Vyāsa said: O king! When Šukra finished saying his uncertain words, Prahlāda clasped his feet and began to say thus:

प्रहाद उवाच

भार्यावछ सयायात्याज्यानस्मांतश्वातृन्त ।

त्यन्तु नारीस सर्वेत्र त्विद्धितांसनयार्थ नः ॥ ३२ ॥

Prahlāda said: O our Guru Bhārgava! To-day we have come to you in a very distressed condition! O Omniscient! we are your disciples; we are your good sons; you ought not to quit us.

गते त्वयि तु मन्थर्ष शैतृपेण दुरात्मना ।

त्वेक्षेयम्युरातार्वियं तेन प्रविश्रतः ॥ 33 ॥

On your departure to get the Mantra, that hypocrite, vicious Brhaspati getting the opportunity, assumed your false appearance and cheated us.

अज्ञानकृतदोषेण नैवं कुयिति शान्तिमान् ।

सर्वेत्रसर्वे विज्ञानासि चितं नः प्रवर्ण त्वति ॥ 34 ॥

Peaceful persons do not take any offence committed with ignorance; you know every thing; you know very well that our hearts are devoted to thee and to thee alone. There is no need of telling any thing further to you.

ज्ञाला नतस्पास भावं त्वज कोर्भ महामते ।

ब्रुवर्ति सुनयः सर्वे शणकोया हि साधयः ॥ 35 ॥

O Highly Intelligent One! By your Tapas, you know our inner minds and relinquish your anger. The sages say that the anger of the saints is not lasting.

जर्त स्वभावतः शीर्ते नह्वातापसमागान्ति ।

भवल्युर्ण विश्योगाच्छ शीतत्यमनुगच्छिति ॥ 36 ॥

O Muni! Water is naturally cool; when in contact with fire it gets, hot; but, when the heat is removed, it gets cold shortly after.

कोधश्रशतलयो वै तचक्यः सर्वेधा बुधैः ।

तस्माइत्रोषं परित्यज्य प्रसादं कुक सूत्रत ॥ 37 ॥

O observer of good vows! Anger is like Cāndāla; sages therefore quit it. Our prayer to you is that you leave your anger and be pleased with us.

यदि न त्वासि कोधे त्वनयस्मानुसु: विज्ञान् ।

तथा तत्का महाभाग गायिष्यायो सत्ताल्म ॥ 38 ॥

If you do not quit your anger and if you make us overpowered with grief and sorrow, O blessed one! We, being abandoned by you, will go down to the Pātāla.

व्यास उवाच

प्रहादस्य वचः श्रुत्वा भाग्यो ज्ञानवशूषा ।

विलोक्य सुमना भूत्वा तानुवाच हसविश्च ॥ 39 ॥

Vyāsa said: Bhārgava heard Prahlāda's words and, with his intuitioned eye, came to see the proper state of affairs and was pleased and lovingly said.

न भेतल्यं न गतल्यं दानवा वा सतालम् ।

स्वशिव्यामि वो याज्ञान्यान्यैरितयिः किन्त ॥ 40 ॥ 10

You will not have to fear nor to center into the Pātāla. You are my Yajamānas; I will certainly protect you all by my never failing Mantra power.

हितं सत्यं ब्रवीम्यद्य शृषुण्यं तत् निश्रयम् ।

वचनं मम धर्मज्ञ: श्रुतं यद्बहण: पुरा ॥ 4 1 ॥

O knower of religion! What Brahmā of yore told me, I am now telling you accordingly. Hear my truthful words, leading to your welfare.

अवर्धभ्याविनो भावाः प्रभविति शुभाणुभाः ।

दैवं न चान्यथा कर्तुं क्षमः को3पि धगतते ॥ 4 2 ॥

Whatever is inevitable, doomed to pass, must come to pass, be it auspicious or inauspicious. No one is able in this world to go against the current of Fate.

अष्ट मन्दवला यूयं कालयोगादसंशयम् ।

देवैर्जिताः सकृष्चापि पातालं प्रतिपल्यथा ॥ 4 3 ॥

Under the influence of Time you are now deprived of strength; therefore you will have to suffer defeat at the hands of the Devas and you will have to go once to the Pātāla.

प्राप्त: पर्यविकालो व इति ब्रह्मा उध्यभाषत ।

भुर्फ तारयं भवाद्रिष्ठ पूर्ण सर्वे समुद्धिमत् ॥ 4 4 ॥

युगानि दर्श पूर्णानि देवानात्रक्य मूर्धनि ।

देवयोगाच्छ युधाविभर्षुर्भे जैतलेवमूर्जितम् ॥ 4 5 ॥

Brahmā said: When your time to enjoy the sovereignty of the Triloki had come, you enjoyed the kingdoms of the Triloki with all its wealth and power. You attacked the Devas and, helped by Time, had been able to trample them under your feet and held your sovereignty for full ten yugas and enjoyed the pleasures without any hitch.

सावर्गिके मनी राज्य पुनस्तुभ विध्यति ।

पौरब्रहीलोक्यविराजी राज्य प्राप्यति ते बलि: ॥ 46 ॥

You will regain this kingdom in the Sāvarnika manvantara. Then Bali will come in your family as the grandson of Prahlāda and will conquer the Triokī and will get name and fame throughout his kingdom.

यदा वामनस्लेण हृतं देवेन विज्याना ।

तदैव च भव्यतीयः प्रोक्तो देवेन विज्याना ॥ 47 ॥

हृतं वेन बलै राज्यं देवेनावळशधीसद्धये ।

त्वमित्यो भविता चाठ्ये चिषते साविणीके मन्ती ॥ 48 ॥

When the Lord of Vaikuntaha had incarnated as

Vāmana and stolen away the kingdom of Bali, then the Janārdana Viṣṇu told Bali, the king of the Demons that “I have taken away your kingdom by pretext to serve the purpose of the Gods; you will become Indra, no doubt, in the coming Śavarnika manvantara”.

भार्गव उवाच

इल्युक्तो हरिणा पीजस्वल प्रब्रहा संग्रतम् ।

अदृष्यः सर्वभूतानां गुप्तभूतरित भीतवत् ॥ 49 ॥

Bhārgava said: According to the sayings of Śrī Bhagavān Hari, your grandson Bali is now invisible to all creatures and is now passing away his time, very much terror stricken.

एकता वासवेनासी विलिर्यंभरूपाफक ।

गूच्य गृहे स्थितः कामं भयभीतः शतकातः ॥ 50 ॥

दृष्टि बढुप्रा तेन वासवेन बलिस्तव ।

किमर्थ गार्वैं रूपं कृतत्वान्द्विपृगव ॥ 51 ॥

Being afraid of Indra, he is now staying in a lonely house as an ass. One day Indra on seeing him enquired of him, in various ways, the cause of his assuming that ass-body.

भोक्ता लं सर्वतेकास्य दैत्यानां च प्रगामिता ।

“न लज्जा खरूसंपण तव राक्षसस्ताम ।”

तस्य तद्धचनं श्रुत्वा दैत्याज्ञो बलिस्तदा ॥ 52 ॥

O Lord of the Daityas? You have always enjoyed pleasures of all the world; you are the ruler of the Daityas; you ruled over all the world; do you not feel shame now in thus assuming this ass body. The Lord of the Daityas, hearing him, spoke thus.

प्रोवाच चवनं शकं कोउर शोकः शतकतो ।

यथा विज्यामृदोतजो मल्यकछ्छपतं गते ॥ 53 ॥

तथा3ई खरूर्णे संवितः कालयोगतः ।

यथा तं कमले लीन: संस्थितो बहाहत्यया ॥ 54 ॥

O Indra—‘There is nothing to be sorry in these matters? When the most powerful Viṣṇu can assume fish and tortoise incarnations, then what wonder is there that I by virtue of the force of Time, am now staying as an ass? When you had murdered a Brāhmana, you had hoid yourself

Book IV Chapter XV

in the Mānasa lake in the lotusses; similarly to-day distressed, I am staying here in this ass body.

पीडितश तथा ताश स्थितोई खललपूक् ।

दैवार्थिनस्य किं दुःखं किं सुरं चाकशासन ॥ 55 ॥

कालः क्रातिवे वे नूनं यदिच्छति यथा तथा ।

O Indra! What sorrow or happiness can be to a person who is under the control of Fate. To him everything is alike; for whatever the Time wishes, It can act accordingly."

भानव उवाच

इति ते बलिवेदेशेगे कृत्वा संविद्युतमाम् ॥ ५६ ॥

प्रबोधी प्राप्तुः कामं यथास्थानं च जमतुः ।

Bhārgava said to Prahlāda! Both persons Bali and Indra got enlightenment at the mutual

conversations; and they went away to their places at their own will.

इन्योतनें समाध्याता मया देवबलिष्ठता ।

देवाधीनं जगत्सर्वे सदेवासुरमानुभम् ॥ 57 ॥

श्रिति श्रीमद्दैयिणालो महापुराणे वतुर्बेकन्के

चतुर्देशोउध्याः: 11 14 11

O Lord of the Asuras! I have narrated to you this stroy indicating how powerful is the Time. Know that the Devas and Daityas and all the human beings and this whole universe is under that Great Fate.

Here ends the Fourteenth Chapter in the Fourth Book of Śrimadevībhāgavatam, the Mahā Purānam of 18,000 verses on the Daityas getting back their Śukrācārya, by Maharṣī Veda Vyāsa.

Book IV Chapter XV

CHAPTER XV

On the Truce Between the Daityas and the Devas

व्यास उवाच

इति तस्य वचः शुत्वा भार्गवस्य महात्मनः ।

प्रहादस्तु सुसंह्छो वध्व वृनपन्दन ॥ १ ॥

Vyāsa said: O king Janamejaya! Prahlāda was glad to hear the above words of the high souled Bhārgava.

त्राला देयं बलिष्ठ च प्रहादस्तावुरावा च ।

कृतंपि युद्धे न जयो भविष्यति कदाचन ॥ 2 ॥

Knowing Fate to be the strongest, he addressed the Daityas: Never, in this battle will victory be ours.

तदा ते जयिनः प्रीघृदानैवा मदार्थिततः ।

संग्रामस्तु प्रकर्तेथो देई किं न विदामहे ॥ 3 ॥

निरुद्धामानां देई ति प्रधानमसुराधिप ।

केन दृष्टं कव वा दृष्टं कौदृषंगे केन निर्मितम् ॥ 4 ॥

तस्मादुब्दं करीथ्योगे बलमाथाय साम्यन्तम् ।

भवागे देल्वयंरं लं सर्वज्ञोरि महामते ॥ 5 ॥

Then the victorious Demons, infatuated with pride, told Prahlāda: What is Fate? We do not recognise it. We ought to fight. O Lord of us: Fate reigns over those that are idle, not energetic. Has Fate any shape? Who has created it? Has any body seen Fate? However let us gather strength and

fight. You are very intelligent and all knowing. It is proper that you should take our lead in the fight.

इतुक्तसैतदा राजप्रहादः प्रवलाहिता ।

सेनातीक्ष तदा भूत्वा देवान् युद्धे समाक्रयत् ॥ 6 ॥

O king! When the Daityas spoke thus, Prahlada, the great Destroyer of enemies, became the general and challenged the Devas to fight.

तेक्षि तजासुरान्द्रवहा संग्रामे समुपस्थितान् ।

सर्वे संभृतसंभाग देवास्तानसमयोधयन् ॥ 7 ॥

On seeing the Asuras in the battlefield, the Devas, dressed with arms and weapons, began to fight with them.

संग्रामन्तु तदा घोर: शक्रप्रहादयोरभूत् ।

पूर्णे वर्षशंत तत्र पुनीनां विस्मयावह: ॥ 8 ॥

For full one hundred years the dreadful battle was fought between Indra and Prahlâda; on seeing this, the Munis were astonished.

वर्तमाने महायुढे शुक्रेन प्रतिपालिता: ।

जयमापुस्तदा दैत्याः प्रक्रावप्रमुखा नृप ॥ 9 ॥

O king! In this fearful battle, the Daityas with their general Prahlāda, the followers of Śukrācārya, got the victory.

तदेवे-न्नो भुगोर्वक्यासर्वेदुःखविनाशिनीम् ।

सम्मार मनसा देवीं भुक्तिंदां परमां शिवाम् ॥ १० ॥

Then Indra, advised by their Guru Brhaşpati, began to remember the Goddess of the Universe, the Most High, the Giver of welfare, the Destroyer of all sorrows and calamities, and the Bestower of freedom, worship Her, and sing hymns to Her with great devotion.

इन्ह जवाच

जय देयि महामाये शुल्याधिपी चांबिके ।

शंखचक्रमावयवघडूगहलेउंपययदे ॥ १ १ १ ॥

नमसे घुवेगुओगि शक्तिरूर्गनमकिके ।

दुशतत्वास्थिके मातर्महाविन्दुव्यक्षिपि ॥ १ २ ॥

Indra said! Victory be to the name of the Goddess Mahāmāyā, the Eternal Mother, the Holder of the trident! Holder of conchshell, disc, club, and lotus, the Giver of “no fear.” Salutation to Thee, the Goddess of the Universe; Thou art the Supreme Heroine in everything that relates to force, that is described in the Sakti Darśana Śāstras. Thou art the Ten Tattvas, Thou art the Mother, Thou art the Mahāvidyā (the Supreme Knowledge).

Note: There are many Tattvas; here the ten tattvas are according to the Sakti Darśana. There are many Dharma Śāstras. Here Śakti Śāstra is meant.

The Tattvas are those ultimate substances into which these gross manifestations resolve. The tattvas are Mahābindu, Nāda Śakti, Miśra Bindu, etc.

महाकुष्डलिनीकले सचिवादानन्दलिपि।

प्राणानिहोत्रविद्यो ते नगो दीपिशाखामिके ॥ १३ ॥

O World Mother! Thou art the Mahā Kundalini (the great Serpent Fire); thou art the Everlasting Existence, Intelligence and Bliss; Thou art the Deity of the vital Fire (Prāna); Thou art the Deity of the Agnihotra (maintenance of the Sacred Fire and an oblation to It); Thou art the Holy Flame, burning always in the etherial space in the Heart; Salutation to Thee!

पढ़कोशान्तराते तुच्छे ब्रह्माखलीणि ।

आनन्दकलिके मात: सर्वोपनिषद्धिति ॥ 14 ॥

Thou dwellest within the five Sheaths (the Annamaya, the Prānamaya, the Manomaya, the Vijñānamaya and the Ānandamaya sheaths are the five sheaths here referred to). Thou art the Indweller of the Ānandamaya koṣa, Thou art of the nature of Puccha Brahma, the end of Brahma. Thou art the Deity of all, the Ānanda (bliss) unblown, O Mother! Thou art the Deity of all the Upaniṣadas.

मातः प्रसीद सुमुखी भव हीनसत्त्वं-

स्वायव नो जननि दैन्यपारजितान् वै ।

त्वं देवि नः शरणादा घुवन प्रमाणा

शक्ताउसि दुःखागमनैस्तिलबीयकुंचे ॥ 15 ॥

O Mother! Be pleased unto us; we have become powerless; protect us. O Mother! we are defeated by the Daityas; O Goddess! Thou art endowed with all the powers, Thou art our Sole Refuge in this Universe, in times of distress, and Thou art the Only One, strong and capable to remove all our dangers.

ध्यार्याति येधि सुखिती नितरां भवन्ति

दुःखात्विता विगतगोक्षभायतशास्त्रये ।

मोक्षार्थिनो विगतमानविष्युकसंगाः

संसाराविर्धितजं प्रतरित संतः ॥ 16 ॥

O Goddess! Those who incessantly meditate on Thee are really happy; and those that do not meditate Thee, their fear, and sorrows are never removed; those that want ultimate freedom from bondage and who meditate on Thee always—those pure souls, being free from Ahariñkāra, and free from attachment go, no doubt, beyond this ocean of world.

लं देवि विश्वजनपि प्रशिक्षभावा

संस्थानाथमुदितासंहिरुपतापा ।

संहरुभेतदिखिलं किल कालकपा

को बेत्ति तेउम्य चरितं ननु मन्दबुद्धि: ॥ 17 ॥

O World Mother! Thy prowess is ever manifested whenever protection is demanded; You always come forward and relieve the distressed; You are the great destroyer! Thou art the Time Incarnate of all these worlds; O Mother! We are fools; how can we appreciate your qualities.

Book IV Chapter XV

इहा हर्ष शरिद्धशरो हरीष्ठ इन्जो

यमोक्ष वक्षणोजनियमौरी च ।

जातुं भूमा न मुन्योरीप महानुभावा

यम्या: प्रभावमतुलं निगमागमाधि ॥ 18 ॥

Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Maheśa, I myself, Sun, Yama, Varuna, Fire, Air, the high minded munis, Āgama, Nigama (the Tantras and the Vedas, are quite unable to realise Your unequalled prowess; Salaution to Thy Fect.

धन्यास एव तव भक्तिपरा महानतः

संसारयु:खरितृतः सुखसिन्युमानः ।

ये भक्तिभावरहिता न कदापि

दुःखाधोधि जनिक्षयतरंगमुपे तरिति ॥ 19 ॥

Those are blessed that are devoted to Thee; They are the great souls; they always dive in the Ocean of Bliss, being always free from the fangs of this Samsāra. Those that are not Your devotees, cannot cross this Ocean of Samsāra, where the Birth and Death are the billows.

ये वीर्यमाना: सित्तवामरेशु

क्रीडन्ति धन्या: शिविकाधिकडा:।

मै: पूजिता त्वं किल पूर्वदैते

नानोषहरिति चिन्तयामि ॥ 20 ॥

O Goddess! Those that are always fanned by the white cāmaras and those that travel always in cars, they in their previous births worshipped Thee with various things; therefore they have acquired the effects of their meritorious deeds; this is my opinion.

ये पूर्यमाना वरावणत्या

विलासिनीर्वृद्विलासयुक्ता:।

सामानकैक्षीपनर्तैर्जलित मन्ये

हि तैन्स्व कि पूजितासि || 21 ||

Those that are always worshipped amongst the human beings, those that go on nice elephants, those that are surrounded by pleasures and enjoy the lovely companions of beautiful coquetish women, those that go surrounded by soldiers, O Goddess! I consider they worshipped Thee in their previous births, and they are now enjoying fruits of their past deeds.

व्यास उवाच

एवं स्तुता मघवत्ता देवी विश्वेषृति तदा ।

प्रादुर्बंभृत तत्सा सिंहाकृडा चतुर्भूजा ॥ 22 ॥

गंखचकानदायपात्बिघन्नी चाक्लीचना ।

रक्तबारधा देदी तिव्यमाल्यविष्यपाण ॥ 23 ॥

Vyāsa said: Thus praised by Indra, the Goddess of the Universe with four arms hurriedly appeared there mounted on a lion. Conchshell, disc, club, and lotusses were held by the beautiful eyed Goddess in Her four hands respectively, wearing a red apparel and ornamented with divine garlands.

तानुवाच सुगान्देवी प्रसज्वद्ना नीता ।

भयं रचजुं भो देशः शं विधाये किलतानुपा ॥ 24 ॥

The Goddess being pleased addressed the Devas with sweet words, "Cast off your fear. O Devas! I will see presently all about your welfare."

इत्युत्नवा सा तदा देही सिंहास्काठितिसुन्दी ।

जगाम तरसा तर यत्र दैर्या मद्धान्धितः ॥ 25 ॥

Addressing the Devas thus, the Divine Mother mounted on a lion, went hurriedly to the place where the demons were waiting, infatuated with pride.

प्रहादप्रमुखा: सर्वे दूर्वा देर्वी पुरः त्रिवताम् ।

ऊचु: परमरं भीता: किं कतन्यमितमत्तद ॥ 26 ॥

All the Daityas with their general Prahlāda saw the Goddess before them and were terrified and began to address each other “What are we to do now?”

देवं नारायणं चार संप्राप्ता चधिडका किल ।

महिप्रांतकरी नूनं चण्डपुर्णद्विनारिणी ॥ 27 ॥

निहनिययिति नः सर्वान्विका नास संगयः ।

वक्रकृष्णा यया पूर्वे निहतों मधुकेटभी ॥ 28 ॥

This Candikā Goddess has come here to protect the Devas. She destroyed Mahisāsura and Caṇḍa Munḍa; it was She that killed, in days of yore, Madhu-Kaitabha with evil look.

एवं चिन्तातुान् बीक्ष्य प्रह्मादस्तानुवाच है ।

योद्ब्येन यान्तव्य पलाय्य दान्वोतमाः ॥ 29 ॥

नमुचितानुवाचा पलायनपरान्ह ।

हनिष्यति जगन्माता क्षिपता किल हैतिथि: ॥ 30 ॥

Seeing the demons thus full of sorrowful thoughts, Prahlāda addressed the Daityas: “It is better not to fight but let us fly away all together.” Then the Daitya Namuci told the Daityas ready to fly away “If you fly away, this World Mother will instantly kill you all with weapons in Her hands.

तथा कुभ महाभाग यथा दुःखं न जायते ।

जजामोउदीय पातालं तां स्तुत्वा तदनुज्ञा ॥ ३ १ ॥

Do that by which we can protect us. Let us worship the Goddess of the Universe, and, getting Her permission, we will go this very day to the Patāla."

प्रहाद उवाच

स्तोमि देती महामायां सृष्टिश्यन्तकारिणीम् ।

सर्वेषां जननीं शान्धि भक्तानामभयंकीम् ॥ ३२ ॥

Prahlāda said “I will worship the Goddess Mahāmāyā, the Creatrix, Preservix and Destructrix of the Universe, the World Mother, and the Assurer of safety to Her devotees.”

व्यास उवाच

इत्युक्चा विद्याभनस्तु प्रहादः परमाध्ययित् ।

तुधव जगतां धार्यी कृताझलिपुटसदा ॥ 33 ॥

Vyāsa said: Thus saying, the knower of the highest knowledge, Prahlāda, the devotee of Viṣṇu, began to sing hymns with folded hands in praise of the Goddess, the Upholdress of the Universe.

माला सर्वद्वापाति यस्यां सर्वे चराचरम् ।

सर्वाधिष्ठान्तपारे तस्ये हिंमूर्तेय नमः ॥ 34 ॥

I bow down to Thee, the incarnate of the mantra "Hrim" the Refuge of all, and within Whom this whole Universe, moving and unmoving, is appearing untruly as a snake is mistaken for a garland of flowers.

त्वस्तः सर्वमिदं विश्वं स्थावतं जंगमं तथा ।

जन्ये निविमगाङ्गाते कतारित्वन विनिर्मितः ॥ ३ ५ ॥

O Goddess! All these Universes, moving and unmoving, have sprung from Thee; Brahma, Viṣṇu and others are Creators, Preservers in name only; Thou hast created them all.

नमो देख महामाये सर्वेधा जननी स्मृता ।

को भेदस्त्व देवेषु दैत्येषु स्वकृतेषु च ॥ ३६ ॥

O Mahāmāya! You are the Divine Mother of all! When You have created the Asuras and the Suras, how can you then see any difference between the Devas and the Daityas?

मातुः पुत्रेषु को भेदोउध्यगुंष शुभेषु च ।

तथैव देवेब्यमातु न कर्तैवस्त्वयोर्जना ॥ 37 ॥

As a Mother makes no distinction between her good sons and bad sons, so You are not to make any difference between us and the Devas; this is our prayer to you.

यादुपासताशुग मातः सुताते दानवा: किल ।

यतस्त्वं विश्वजननी पुराणेषु प्रक्रौतिंता ॥ 38 ॥

O Goddess! You have been sung in all the Purāṇas as the World Mother; therefore, O Mother! We are your sons just as the Devas are.

तेत्रि स्वार्थपरा नूनं तबेव वयमयत् ।

नातरं दैत्यसुर्योपैदोर्धं मोहस्पमवः ॥ ३ १ ॥

O Mother! As they have got their interests, so we too have got our interests; therefore there is no difference between the Daityas and the Devas. Therefore if any one makes any difference, it is due to the subtle error.

धन्वरादिघोगेषु वयं सत्ता दिवानिगम ।

तथैव देवा देवेशि को घोदोउसुदेवयो: ॥ 40 ॥

O Goddess! As we are attached to wealth, wives, and other pleasures of the senses, so the gods are; O Goddess! How then can any difference exist between them and us.

तेषि कश्यपदायादा वयं तत्सम्भाव: किल ।

कुतो विरोधसभूतिजिता माततलायुना ॥ 41 ॥

O Mother! They are the sons of Maharşi Kaśyapa; we also are his sons; Therefore you cannot have partiality for them before us.

न तथा विहितं माततलयि सर्वीमुद्धले ।

साम्यतेव त्वया स्थाया देवेच्यमासु चैव हि ॥ 42 ॥

O World Mother! In You no such difference is visible any where. Therefore do You here preserve equality amongst us both.

Book IV Chapter XV

गुणधितिकात्सर्वे समुपयज्ञ: सुरासुरा: ।

गुणाधिता यावेयुने कर्थे देहभृतोजभगः ॥ 4 3 ॥

The Suras and Asuras all have sprung from the permutations and combinations of the 3 qualities! Then how the Devas being embodied can possess more qualities than us.

कामः कोधछ लोगश्र सर्वदेषेरु सचित्यः ।

वर्तने सर्वदा तस्मात्ञोत्रियोरी भवेजनः ॥ 4 4 ॥

Every embodied soul possesses always cupidity, anger, covetuousness; how then can one expect to remain without any quarrels with others.

त्वया मिथो विरोधोउरं कलित्यः किल कौतुकात् ।

मन्यामाहे विषयेने नूनं सुद्दिद्वक्षया ॥ 4 5 ॥

We think that it is all sport with You to see our opinions different, rather contradictory, and it is You who got us involved in quarrels with each other and it is Your pleasure to witness how we fight against each other.

अन्यथा खलु भानुणां विधीयः कीर्णोउनये ।

तं वेत्रेच्छस चामुपडे वीक्षितुं कलहिं किल ॥ 46 ॥

O Sinless one! O Câmunde! Were You not so fond to see our fight, how then, we being brothers are at war against each other. Certainly it is Your Divine Sport.

ज्ञानायि धर्म धर्मे वेधि चाहं शनकतमुम् ।

तथापि कलहोउसाकं भोगार्थ देवि सर्वदा ॥ 47 ॥

O Goddess! I know what is religion, I know who is Indra. It is the very idea to enjoy these sensual pleasures that is the only cause of our incessant quarrels.

एकः कोर्पि न शास्त्रास्ति संसारे तां चित्रास्थिके ।

सूहावतस्तु कः कर्तुं क्षमते वचनं बुधः ॥ 48 ॥

O Mother! You are the Sole Ruler of this Samsāra; no sensible man can carry out the words of a man who yearns for something (i. e. O Mother, You are the only one that is desideriss; so we can obey your words).

देवानुरीरयं सिंभूमिधितः समये क्वचित् ।

विष्णुना विहितो भेदः सुधानलच्छनेवे च ॥ ४ १ ॥

O Mother! Once the Devas and the Asuras

conjointly churned the ocean. At that time Viṣṇu, on the plea of distributing the jewel, and the ambrosial nectar, incurred quarrels amongst them.

त्वयाउसौ कल्पितः शौरि: पालकल्चे जगदगुरुः ।

तेन तक्षसी: स्वयं लोपागृहीतामसनुस्ति ॥ ५० ॥

O Mother! You have made him the Preserver and Controller of the Universe and the Spiritual Guide of the world, And it was He that took away the Goddess Lakṣmī, the beautiful lady amongst the Deva women.

ऐरावतलवेढ़ेण पारिताजोर्ध यामधुक ।

सुरैरूच्छे:शबा: सर्व गृहीतं वैण्यावेच्छ्या ॥ 51 ॥

Indra, the Lord of the Gods, took the elephant named Airāvata, the flower Pārijāta, the Heavenly Cow yielding all desires, and the horse Uchchaishravā. Thus, through the desires and devices of Vishnu, they got all the excellent things.

अनय तादुर्ग कृत्वा जाता देवासुं माधवः ।

"अन्यायिनः सुग नूनं पश्य तं धर्मक्षणम्।"

संस्थापिता: सुग नूनं विष्णुना बहुमानिना ॥ 52 ॥

O! What a wonder is this that the Devas were considered holy persons, after they had committed such unholy acts; no doubt the Devas had done a very heinous crime. O Goddess! You can judge Yourself what is the just and unjust thing in this case.

नूनं दैत्या: पराभूवन्यग्र चंधर्तक्षणम् ।

क्व धर्मः कीर्ण्यो धर्म: क्व कार्य क्व च समुत्ना ॥ 53 ॥

What is Religion? And where is Religion? And what are the acts done by a religious man? What is uprightness, justice, and purity? You better examine which party has observed virtue? Who has shown uprightness justice and purity? To whom victory and defeat are due? You are the only one capable to judge all these things.

कवयासि न कस्याएं सिद्धे मेमापिके मन्त्र।

ताकिका युक्तिवाद्ञा विधिप्रा वेदवादाकः ॥ 54 ॥

उक्ता: सकर्निकं विश्व विवदते जाड़ानकाः ।

Alas! Whom to tell all the conclusions arrived at in the Mīmārīsakas. If any one considers, one

Srimaddevibhāgavatam

will find the world is the field of dissensions and quarrels; the argumentators look to the logical reasoning only; the followers of the Vedas look to the rules and regulations only; these so called men of gross ideas they acknowledge that this world is created and preserved by the One only, and yet they quarrel amongst each other.

कर्ता भवति चेद्यम्बस्मोत चितते किन्ति ॥ 55 ॥

वितोध: कीदुगस्तन चैककर्मणि चै निय: ।

वेरेनेकमाति: कममच्छासेध्चपि तथा पुनः ॥ 56 ॥

नैकवाक्यं चवस्तेषामपि वेदिविदां पुनः ।

If there be One and only One Lord of this wide infinite Sarīsaña, then why would there be difference and quarrels amongst each other? Why is there not seen any agreement in opinion and why do the Sāstras differ and why are there so many differences in the opinions held by the knowers of the Vedas.

यतः स्वार्थपरं सर्वे जनस्थावरजंगमम् ॥ 57 ॥

निःस्पृहः कोर्पि संसारे न भवेवे भविष्यति ।

O Goddess! This whole Universe, moving and unmoving is selfish; hence arise so many differences between several opinions. There was no one unselfish in this world and there would be no unselfish persons born hereafter.

शशिजास्य गुोपोर्षां हेता जात्वा बलादपि ॥ 58 ॥

गौतमस्य तवेज्ञे जानता धर्मनिश्रभ्यम् ।

गुरुणान्तुज्यभावां च भृत्ता भर्षिती बलात् ॥ 59 ॥

ग्रणो भर्षगोत बालः कृतशोधस्वा पुनः ।

Look! The Moon stole away perforce knowingly the wife of Brhaspati; Indra, knowing what is religion stole away the wife of Gautama; Brhaspati enjoyed forcibly the wife of his younger; and also he outraged his elder brother's wife in her pregnant state and cursed the boy in the womb and made him blind.

विष्णुना च शिरिश्छत्रं राहोश्रकेश के बलात् ॥ 6 0 ॥

असपार्थ विभा कामं तदा सच्चवताम्विके ।

पौजो धर्मवतां शूर: सत्यवतारपायण: ॥ 6 1 ॥

यज्ञा द्वापणितः शान्तः सर्वैजः सर्वेपूजकः ।

What more to say than Viṣṇu, all full of Sāttvic

qualities, severed perforce the head of Rāhu. O Mother! Look to the case of my grandson Bali who used to pay due respects to all, who was the foremost amongst the virtuous, observer of rigorous truth, performer of sacrifices, liberal, peaceful, all-knowing.

कृत्यास्त्र धावानं रूचे ह्रौणा छलवेदिता ॥ 62 ॥

वडितोउरी बलि: सर्व हतं राज्यं पुरा किल ।

तथापि देवान्यर्थन्यावदिति मनीपिणः ॥ 63 ॥

वद्रति चादुदादांध्र धर्मदादाङ्गयं नातः ।

एवं जात्वा जान्यानार्थेथ्छस तथा कुरु ॥ 64 ॥

गरणा दानवा: सर्वे जक्ति वा स्थ वा पुनः ।

The pretender Hari, taking the form of a dwarf in his Vāmana incarnation) deceived Bali and took away all his kingdoms. Alas! Still the intelligent good persons reckon the Deva Viṣṇu as the preserver of Religion. What a wonder! Those who are flaterers become victorious in this world; and defeat come to those that speak of Dharma.

O Goddess! You are the Mother of all the worlds; do whatever You like. But You should know that the Demons are all under Your protection; kill or save them as You like.

श्रीदेव्यवाच

सर्वे गछळत पातालं तन वारं यथेवितम् ॥ 65 ॥

कुठ्छं दानवा: सर्वे निर्भया गतान्यवः ।

The Devi said: O Demons! Leaves you all the anger arising from this warfare and go without any fear to Pātāla and live there at your ease and happiness.

काल: ग्रीत्योय युधाधिः कारणं स गुषेज्युषे ॥ 66 ॥

सुनिवंदुपराणां हि सुखं सर्वेत्र सर्वेत्र ।

Better now wait on Time; whether you will get auspicious or inauspicious fruits for your deeds. Know whoever is desireless and unattached, to him happiness is always and everywhere.

त्रैलोक्यस्य च राज्येधिन पे सुखं लोपचेतसामू ॥ 67 ॥

कृते5पि न सुखं पूर्णं स्मयुहाणां फलैपि ।

Whose mind is avaricious, He does not get peace and happiness, even if he acquires the Triloki.

Book IV Chapter XVI

Even, in the golden age, avaricious persons did not get happiness, though they acquired the fruits of their actions.

तस्माचक्यता महीमेतां प्रयांत्वघ महीतलम् ॥ 68 ॥

ममाजां पुरतः कृत्वा सर्वे विगतकल्पया: ।

Therefore you get yourselves freed of your sins and obey My order and leave the earth and go down to the Pātāla.

म्यास उवाच

तच्छुत्वा वचनं देल्यासतेथ्युत्वा रसातलम् ॥ 6 9 ॥

प्राणम्य दानवा: सर्वे नात: शक्लवाभिराक्रातः ।

Vyāsa said: On hearing the Devi's words, the Demons obeyed and bowing at Her feet and preserved by Her, went to Pātāla.

अन्तदीये त्तो देही देवा: स्वभुवनं नतः ॥ 70 ॥

त्यक्त्वा वैरं स्थिता: सर्वे ते तदा देहवदान्वा: ।

Then the Devi disappeared; and the Devas went away to their own homes. Thus the Devas and the Daityas, abandoning their feelings of enmity towards each other, lived in place.

एतदाख्यानमिहलं वः श्रणीति वदत्यथा ॥ 71 ॥

सर्वेदुःखविनिभून: प्रयाति पदमुत्तमम् ।

ड्राँते श्रीमदेवीभाग्रतो महापुराणो चतुर्बंकल्ये

पडवशो5धयाः: 11 15 11

O King! He who hears this fact, gets himself freed from all sorts of calamities and reaches the Highest Peace.

Here ends the Fifteenth Chapter in the Fourth Book of Śrimaddevibhāgavatam, the Mahā Purānam of 18,000 verses, on the truce between the Daityas and Devas and on their departures with peace by Maharsi Veda Vyāsa.

Book IV Chapter XVI

CHAPTER XVI

On the Birth of the Several Avatāras of Viṣṇu

जनमेजय उवाच

भूगुणापान्यनिश्र हेराद्धृतकर्मण: । अवतारतः कर्थे जाताः कस्मिन्मवन्तरे विभो ॥ १ ॥ विस्त्तगघ्रद्ध धर्मेन अवतारकर्थ होरः । पाचनागक्षीं बह्यब्छृतां सर्वसुखावहामू ॥ २ ॥

Janamejaya spoke: O best of Munis! How did Viṣṇu, of wonderful deeds, get his incarnation owing to the curse, cast on Him by Bhṛgu? what were His different incarnations in different Manvantaras respectively? O Thou, well versed in religion! O Brāhmaṇa! Kindly narrate those dis-trostying deeds of Hari in His several incarnations, that are the source of happiness, peace and welfare to all humanity.

व्यास उवाच

शूणु राज्यव्यायी अवतान्त् हरेयां।

यस्मिन्यन्ते जाता युगे यस्मित्रायिधि ॥ 3 ॥

Vyāsa said: O king! Hear, I am narrating to you the incarnations of Śrī Bhagavān Hari which He

had in the several Manvantaras and in the several Yugas respectively.

येन रूपेण यत्कार्यं कृतं नारायणेन वै ।

तत्वयं नृप वध्यामि संक्षेणेत तत्वापून ।। ४ ।।

I will tell you now, in brief, what forms He took and what deeds He did in the various incarnations.

धर्मदैयवालतोरो भृच्छाक्ष्ये मनुसभ्यवे ।

ननताराणे धर्मपुत्री छातारी महीत्ते ॥ ५ ॥

In the Cāksuṣa Manvantara, the Bhagavān Hari took the incarnation of Dharma; and the two sons of Dharma, Nara Nārāyaṇa, were widely celebrated in this world.

अथ वैव्यस्ताखोउस्मिन्द्रतीये तु युगे पुनः ।

दन्तायोदयततोउरंः पुवत्यमामद्धरि: ॥ 6 ॥

Then, in the present Vaivasvata Manvantara, under the reign of Vaivasvata Manu in the second Yuga, Bhagavān Hari incarnated as Dattātreya, in the shape of the son of Atri Rsi.

बहा विष्यानुसथा रुद्रस्योदगी देवसत्तमाः ।

पुत्रविमयान्देवास्तवायोर्धिया वृत्ताः ॥ 7 ॥

Anasūyā the wife of Atri, was desirous to have,

Šrimaddevibhāgavatam

as her sons, the three Devas Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Rudra; and in fulfilment of her desires, the Devas took their births in her womb.

अनसूयाश्रियली वच सतीनापुसा सती ।

यसा सप्राधिता देया: पुत्रल्यमागंस्वयः ॥ 8 ॥

Ansūyā, was foremost amongst the chaste and virtuous women and on her praying, Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Rudra the Trinity at once agreed to become her sons.

ब्रह्मा प्रभृतसोमरूपसृद्धताजेयो हृति: स्वयम् ।

दुर्वीमा रूहरूपोर्सी पुरन्तं ते प्रयेदेर ॥ 9 ॥

Brahmā was born as Soma, Hari was born as Dattātreya and Rudra was born as Durvāsā.

गृसिंहस्यावातास्तु देवकायार्थसिद्धये ।

चतुर्थे तु युगे जातो द्विष्ठाक्पो मनोहरः ॥ 10 ॥

In the fourth Yuga, the Bhagavān assumed the beautiful double form in one, the upper part resembling a lion the lower part a human being to accomplish the noble purpose of the Devas.

हिरण्यकाशिणो: सम्यवधाय भगवान् हरि: ।

वक्र केयं नारसिंहं देववानां विस्मयप्रद् ॥ १ १ ॥

It was to kill Hiranyakaśipu that the Bhagavān Hari assumed this appearance, wonderful even to the Devas.

बलेनिंवमनाधाय ऋषे० तेत्रातुगे तथा ।

चकार कर्व भगवान् वामनं कश्यपानुनेः ॥ १ २ ॥

In the Tretā Yuga, the superior and the best of all the Yugas, the Bhagavān incarnated as Vāmana, (the Dwarf), the son of Maharṣī Kaśyapa, to curb the power of Bali.

छलचित्या मखे भूपं राज्यं तस्य जहार ह ।

वाताले स्वाययामास वलिं वामनस्तध्यूक् ॥ १३ ॥

The Dwarf Hari took away by pretext, the kingdom of Bali, while he was performing a sacrifice and sent him down into the Pātāla, (the lower regions).

युगे चैकानविक्षेण द्वे तात्ये घे भगवान् हरि: ।

जमदर्भिनुक्ती जातो रामो नाम महावलः ॥ १४ ॥

Afterwards, in the nineteenth Yuga, known as the Tretā Yuga, Śrī Bhagavān Hari incarnated as

Paraśurāma, very powerful and the son of Jamadagni Rsi.

क्षत्रियांतकरः श्रीमान्सल्यादी जितोंद्रियः ।

दत्तवामेदिति कृतसां कदयपाय महामने ॥ 15 ॥

He was very beautiful and graceful in his body, truthful and the conqueror of his senses. He

extirpated the Kṣattriya race and gave the whole world over to the high minded Rṣi Kaśyapa.

यो वै परयुगामाखो हेरद्धुतकर्मण: ।

अवतारस्टु राजेन्द्र कवितः यापतगतः ॥ 16 ॥

O king! He is the Paraśurāma, the sin-destroyer, the incarnation of Hari, and the doer of wonderful deeds.

जेतायुगे रपोर्वरी गामो दर्शरायतमतः ।

नत्नारायणांगे ही जातौ भृति महावती ॥ 17 ॥

अष्टाधिंगे युगे शस्ती द्वारपेज्ञनगोरिणी ।

धरायावताराथाँ जातौ कृष्णाज्ञी भृति ॥ 18 ॥

कृतवर्तौ महापुब्दे कुर्वेत्रेजितदारुणम् ।

एवं युगे युगे राजत्रवतगा हरेः किल ॥ 19 ॥

भवन्ति बहवः कामं प्रकृतैतनुपलतः ।

प्रकृतरखितं सर्वे वर्गोतजजगतयम् ॥ 20 ॥

After that the Bhagavān Hari incarnated as Rāma, the son of Daśaratha. Next in the twenty-eighth Dvāpara Yuga, He incarnated as the very powerful Arjuna and Śri Kṛṣna, the Anisās of Nara Nārāyana. To remove the load of the earth, these two were born; and they fought deadly battles in the battlefield of Kurukṣetra. O king! Thus the several incarnations of Hari arose, according to the requirements of Prakṛti. O King! These three worlds are under the control of Prakṛti.

यथेच्छति तदेवेयं भ्रमयत्निजां जगत् ।

पुर्षस्य प्रियार्थी सा रचयत्यखितं जगत् ॥ 21 ॥

Whatever the Prakrti wishes at any time, She can fashion the world in that way. And She does this incessantly in accordance with the Word Divine, the Highest Śakti, to please the Puruṣa, without any cessation.

दूर्वा पुरा हि भगवाझ्मदोतच्चारम् ।

सर्वादि: सर्वांश्रासी दुर्जेय: परमोड्ययः ॥ 22 ॥

Book IV Chapter XVII

निरालम्बो निराकारो नि:स्यूढ़ा परावर: ।

उपधितिस्थिति भाति यच्या: सा प्रकृति: परा ॥ 23 ॥

In days of yore, the most ancient Bhagavān, the Highest, above all the qualities of Māyā, formless, all pervading, difficult to be conceived, without any decay, self-supporting, without any want, created these worlds, moving and unmoving and He manifested Himself as the Trinity, Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Maheśa in the shape of the three qualities Satīva, Rajas and Tamas, and which is called the Highest Prakṛti.

उत्पत्तिकालयोगाता विभा भाति शिवा तत ।

सा विश्व कुन्ते कामं सा पालयति कामाद् ॥ 24 ॥

This all auspicious Prakṛti shines differently according to the differences in time and circumstances. This threefold Prakṛti, the Great Enchantress of the world is creating, preserving the worlds and is destroying them at the end of the Kalpas.

कल्यान्ते संहृत्येव विरूपा विश्वभोहिनि ।

तया युक्तो3सुबद्धृद्धा विष्णु: पालि तयाधिति: ॥ 25 ॥

O King! Whenever there takes place the union with this Prakrti. Brahmā creates, Viṣṇu preserves, and the all-auspicious God Śankara destroys the worlds.

रुद: संहृते कामं तया सभमिलतः शिवः ।

सा चेवोत्याङ्काकुल्यं पुरा वे नुपसतमम् ॥ 26 ॥

It was She That gave birth to Kākutstha, the best of the kings; and to conquer the Dānavas, She placed him at a certain place.

कुत्रचित्स्थाययामास दानवानां जयाय च ।

एवमर्भिश्छ संसारे सुखदुःखात्विताः क्रिकल ।

भवित्न प्राणिनः सर्वे विधितन्त्रनियन्त्रतः ॥ 27 ॥

ड्रिति श्रीमदेवीभागवते महापुराणे चतुर्यकन्थे

षोडशोरध्याः: 11 16 11

O king! Thus all men controlled by the Great Law in this world, enjoy sometimes the pleasures, enjoy sometimes pains and thus exist in the world. Here ends the Sixteenth Chapter in the Fourth Book of Śrimaddevibhāgavatam, the Mahāpurānam of 18,000 verses, by Maharṣī Veda Vyāsa, on the Birth of the several Avatāras of Visnu and their deeds.

Book IV Chapter XVII

CHAPTER XVII

On the Questions asked by Janamejaya

जनमेव जय जवाच

वारांगनास्त्वया छ्याता नरनारायणाओं ।

एकं नारायणं शान्तं कामयाना: स्मातुग: ॥ १ ॥

Janamejaya said: O Muni! You told before that the heavenly prostitutes sent by Indra in the hermitage of Nara Nārāyana became lustful and desired to live with Nārāyana only, whose heart was crlm and quiet.

शपुत्कामस्तदा जातो मुनिनरीरायण्डा ततः ।

निवारितो नरोणा ध्राजा धर्मविद्धा नृप ॥ 2 ॥

At that moment when Nārāyana was about to curse them, his brother Nara desisted him from taking that step.

किं कृतं मुनिना तेन व्यसने समुपस्थिते ।

ताधि: संकल्पितेणना कामाशोधिभूर्णं मुने । 3 ॥

शक्रेनोराविताधिश्र यदुग्रार्थेनाय युनः ।

याचितेन विवाहार्थं किं कृतं तेन जिजाणूना ॥ 4 ॥

Now I ask you what did that triumphant Nārāyaṇa Muni do, in that critical juncture, when he was repeatedly asked by those prostitutes, sent by Indra, to satisfy their lust?

इन्येतच्छोतुपिच्छ्याम घरितं तस्य मोक्षदम् ।

नारायणस्य मे बूहि चित्रस्तेण चित्रात्मह ॥ ५ ॥

O Grand Sire! I am very eager to know the deeds of Nārāyaṇa, leading to one's freedom, Kindly describe in detail and fulfill my wishes.

व्यास उवाच

श्रणु राजन्यक्ष्यामि यथा तस्य महामन्तः ।

धार्मुपत्रस्य धर्मत विस्तरणं बद्यामि ते ॥ ६ ॥

Vyāsa said: Hear, O king! I am describing to you in, detail, what that high souled son of Dharma did.

शप्तुकाममतु सन्त्रयो नेरणाय यदा हरिः ।

वारितो3सौ समाधायस्य मुपनिरारीयणस्तदा ॥ 7 ॥

Srimaddevibhāgavatam

When Nārāyaṇa Hari was ready to curse them, the Rsis Nara, seeing this, consoled him and desisted him.

ग्रान्तकोपस्तदोवाच तासपस्यी महापुनि: ।

सितमुर्षिमितं वाक्यं मभूरं धर्मनन्दन: ॥ 8 ॥

Then the great sage, the ascetic son of Dharma, Nārāyaṇa, leaving aside his anger, began to address them in sweet words with countenance smiling.

अस्मिज्ञमनि चार्वीयः कृतसङ्कूल्यवानहम् ।

आवाय्यं च न कतैव्यः सर्वेधा दारमंत्रहः ॥ ९ ॥

तस्माद्रष्टनु त्रिविदं कृपां कृत्वा मापोपति ।

धर्मान न प्रकृर्वित्त बतभङ्गे परस्य वै ॥ 10 ॥

O Fair women! We have determined to practise asceticism in this life; it does not therefore behave us to accept any wife; therefore shew your kindness unto us and go back to your Heaven. You would better think that those who know what is religion, they never desire to break the vow of another.

शूत्रोरप्रिमसे नृतं स्वाधीभावो रितः म्यूतः ।

कथं क्रौमि सव्यर्थ तदभावे सुतोचना: ॥ 1 1 1 ॥

O beautiful eyed ones! In the sexual pleasure, it is the delightful feeling of passionate joy that is requisite; and we are wanting in those feelings; then how can we effect that union?

कारणेन विना कार्य न भवेदिति निश्रायः ।

कविधि: कवितं शास्ते स्थायीभावो सः किक्त ॥ १२ ॥

No action can come out of no cause; this is all clear. The poets say that the sexual power and pleasure, is the feeling, the mental attitude that corresponds; and that is the only thing, that lasts. And we have no desire for that.

धन्य: सुचासवर्गी: सभायोर्द्ध रातते ।

प्रतिपातं यतो जातो भवतीनामकृतिमम् ॥ १३ ॥

However my limbs are all very graceful, I am very fortunate and blessed in this world, otherwise how can I be the object of your sincere love towards me.

भवतीभि: कृपा कृत्वा रक्षणीय इतं मा ।

भविष्यामि महाभागा: पतितम्यज्ञनमि ॥ १ ४ ॥

You all are very fortunate; therefore do now

shew this mercy unto me “do not break my vow. I pray now that, in a subsequent birth, I may become your husband.

अर्हायिंश शिर्तालाश्यो द्वापरेडस्मिश्रातले ।

देवानां कार्यसिद्ध्याँ प्रभविष्यामि सर्वथा ॥ 5 ॥

तदा भवत्यो महारा: प्राय्य जन्म पृथवमुथक् ।

भूपतीनी सुता भूत्वा पत्नीभावं गाधियव ॥ १ ६ ॥

O large eyed fair women! In the twenty-eighth Dvāpara Yuga, I will certainly incarnate on earth to effect the purpose of the Devas; then you all also would respectively incarnate as the daughters of kings and would also become my wives.

इत्याश्रय हरिसतानु प्रतिश्रुच परिघहम् ।

व्यसज्वस भगवाइमगुढ विमतजरा: ॥ 17 ॥

Nārāyāna thus consented to marry them in some other next birth; and consoling them, made them go back to their Heavens. They also abandoned their mental disquietude and, on reaching back to Heavens, they explained everything to Indra.

एवं विस्वितास्तेन नाता: स्वर्ग तद्धाना: ।

गक्राव कथयामासु: कारणं सकरं पुन: ॥ 18 ॥

आश्रय मघवा तान्यो वृत्तानं तस्य विस्तत् ।

तुषाव तं महात्मानं नारीदूर्वा तयोर्षी: ॥ 19 ॥

Indra heard (from these heavenly women) what the two two Rṣis did and saw before him Urvaśī and other women created by Nārāyaṇa from his thighs, etc. and began to extol the merits of the high souled Nārāyaṇa.

इन्र उवाच

अहो धैयं मुनेः कामं तथैव च तपोबलम् ।

येनेवैध्यः स्वतपसा तापूरूपा: प्रकल्पितः ॥ 20 ॥

Indra said: O! How wonderful is the patience of the Muni? What is the wonderful influence of his Tapas! Oh! He has created, by the sheer force of his Tapas, Urvaśī and these fair women, unrivalled for their beauties, from his thighs.

इति सुत्वा प्रसातमा बथुप सुरारु ततः ।

नातयाणोष प्रभातमा तस्यधिपतो5पबत् ॥ 2 । ॥

The Lord of the Devas thus extolled his merits and became freed from his anxieties. The virtuous

Book IV Chapter XVII

Nārāyaṇa, too, devoted himself to the practice of his Tapasyā.

इन्येततस्वीमाख्यातं मुनेवृत्तातमूढ़तम् ।

नारायणस्य सकर्ते नरस्य च महामुने ॥ 22 ॥

O king! Thus I have described to you, in detail, all the wonderful accounts regarding Nara Nārāyana.

तौ हि कृष्णाजुनी वीरी भूभारहणाय च ।

जातौ तौ भरतश्रेष्ठ भृगोः शणवशाद्धि ॥ 23 ॥

O Superior in the descendants of Bharata! These two Nara and Nārāyana afterwards incarnated themselves, due to Bhīgu's curses the two great heroes Arjuna and Krṣna, to relieve the burden of the earth.

राजोवाच

कृष्णावतारचरितं विस्तरणे वद्रव मे ।

सन्देहो मम चित्रोस्ति त निवारय मानव ॥ २४ ॥

The king said: O respect giving Muni! Now describe in detail the life of the Avatāra Krṣna and dispel my mental doubt.

ययो: पुत्रत्वमापजी हृद्रनन्ती महावली ।

देवकीक्युदौले ती दुःखभाजी कर्ण मुने ॥ 25 ॥

कंसेन निगडे बढ़ी पीक्षिति बहुवलसग्न ।

ययो: पुत्रो हरि: साक्षानपस तीपितोभवत् ॥ 26 ॥

O best of the Munis! Why were Vasudeva and Devaki, who were chosen by the very powerful Hari and Ananta as their parents, doomed to so many miseries and afflictions. Why had these parents to remain for good many years in the prison of Kanisa, who pleased directly by their Tapasyā that Bhagavān Janārdana.

जातोउसी मयुगरातु तु गोकुले स कथं नतः ।

कर्म हृता झारकायां निवासं कृतावाक्यम् ॥ 27 ॥

Why did Kṛṣṇa taking his birth at Mathurā, go to Gokula? Also what was his object to go to Dvārkā, situated in the ocean, when he killed the enemy Karīsa?

पित्रादिवैति हेणं समुद्धे पावनं किल ।

त्यक्त्वा हेणान्तोरनार्य गतावन्म कईं हरि ॥ 28 ॥

Also why did his father, mother adr relatives,

leave their old holy places of residences and go abroad to live in a wretched old country?

कुलं च द्विज्ञापेन कक्षमुसादितं हरे: ।

भारावतारणं कृत्वा वासुदेवः सनातनः ॥ 29 ॥

देहं मुभोच तरसा जगाम च दिवं हरि: ।

पाधिष्ठानां च भारेण व्याकृतासभून्य मेदिति ॥ 30 ॥

ते हता वासुदेवेन पार्थनामिकर्मणा ।

लुंटिता थैरीः पन्यन्ते कवं न पिवातिता ॥ 31 ॥

Why was the Yadu race destroyed by the curse from a Brāhmaṇa! How did Śrī Kṛṣṇa Vāsudeva leave finally His body after He had relieved the burden of the earth and was about to enter into His Heaven? The evildoers of the earth were slain by Kṛṣṇa and Arjuna, of unequalled prowess; but how was it, that those who plundered the wives of Śrī Hari, were not at all punished by Him!

भीभी प्रोषतस्वा काणी वाहीकोंयण याधिवः।

वैराटो५४ विज्णांध्र ध्रष्ट्यान्छा पार्थिवः ॥ ३२ ॥

सोमदानादय: सर्वे निहताः समे नृप ।

तेषापुनीतो भार्यशीरणां न हतः कवम् ॥ ३३ ॥

The great personages Bhīśma, Droṇa, Kāṃa, the king Vālhīka Virāta, Vikāma. Dhṛṣtadyumna, the king Somadatta were destroyed for relieving the burden of the earth; and the plunderers were acquitted! Kindly explain the cause of this.

कृष्णपन्यः कक्षं दुःखं प्रपातः प्रान्तेः परिवत्ताः।

सन्देशोंस् पुनिश्रष्टि चिति ने परिवर्तिते ॥ 34 ॥

How was it that those chaste and virtuous wives of Śrī Kṛṣṇa got into troubles at the latter end of their lives? There has arisen a doubt in my mind on this point.

वसुवेस्वतु धर्मात्या पुत्रु:खेन तापितः ।

त्यकान्यान कयं प्राणानयमृत्रुं जगाम ह ॥ ३५ ॥

Why did the virtuous Vaisudeva leave his mortal coil owing to the death of his sons and why djú he die an unusual death?

पाण्ड्वा धर्मवंभुक्ता: कृष्णे च निततः सदा ।

ते कथं दुःखभोक्तारो हृषयन्मुनिसन्म १३६ ॥

O best of Munis! The Pāṇḍavas were devoted to Kṛṣṇa and they were religious; they had to suffer so many troubles!

Šrimaddevibhāgavatam

ग्रीष्टी च महाभागा कर्थे दुःखस्य भागिनी ।

वेदीमध्याच्च संजाता तक्ष्ययासम्भवा किल ॥ 37 ॥

Why was Draupadī so very unfortunate and she had to suffer so much miseries, and pains, who was born of Lakṣmī from amidst the sacrificial place and from the altar.

सभावां च समानीता रजोदोर्षमन्त्रिता ।

बाला दु:शासनेनाथ केशग्रहणकर्शिता ॥ 38 ॥

पीडिता सिस्युताउष वनमध्यमता सती ।

तथैव कीचेकानपि पीडिता रूतीं भृणम् ॥ 39 ॥

Why did Duhśāsana drag Her by hair while She was in Her menstruation period, in the hall of audience and why was it that Sindhu Rāja Jayadratha, the king of Sindhu, gave Her exceedingly mental troubles?

पुत्रा: पड़ेल तस्यान्तु निहता श्रीभिना गृहे ।

सुभ्रात्रया: सुतो युद्धे बाल एव निपातितः ॥ 40 ॥

Why was it that Her five sons residing in Her house were killed by Asvatthāmā? What was the cause that the son of Subhadrā had to die in the battlefield?

तथा च देवकीपुःा: घट्कं केसेन निपूर्तितः ।

समर्थानि ह्रिणा दैवेन कृतमन्था ॥ 4 1 ॥

Why did the king Karīsa kill the six sons of Devakī; and why was it that Śrī Harī who was capable of averting the Fate did not at all prevent that?

यावतानां तथा ग्रापः प्रभासे नियनं पुनः ।

कृतक्षयस्तथा तीव्रस्तपत्नीचां च लुंठनम् ॥ 42 ॥

What a wonder is this that in the matters of Brāhmaṇa's curse towards the Jādavas, their being killed in the Prabhāsa, the total extermination of the Jadū race and the plundering of His wives, why did He allow Fate to do these great momentous things?

विष्णुना चेध्रोपापि साक्षात्रायायोने च ।

उप्रसेनस्य सेवा वे दासवस्ततं कृता ॥ 4 3 ॥

If He was the all-powerful God and He Himself Nārāyaṇa, that why did He incessantly act like a slave towards Ugrasena.

Note: Ugrasena was the king of Mathurā and father of Kahīsa. He was deposed by his son; but Krṣṇa after having slain Kahīsa restored him to the throne.

सन्देशेयं महाभाग तत्र नारायणे भुनी ।

सर्वजुत्समानत्वं व्यवहार्ते निरन्तम ॥ 4 4 ॥

हर्शगोकादयो भावाः सर्वेधी सद्गुगाः कवम् ।

ईशस्य हरेजांत कवमयन्याय मति: ॥ 4 5 ॥

All these bring doubt in our minds regarding Nārāyaṇa Munī that His deeds are always like those of ordinary persons; why did his pleasures and pains resemble those of ordinary human beings? Were he God, why his actions were not Godly? (i.e. superhuman)

तस्मादित्ततो बृहि कृष्णाय चरितं महत ।

अलीकिकेन हरिणा कृते कर्म महीतले ॥ 46 ॥

Therefore dost Thou describe in detail all the Divine Lilās (playful sports) done by Hari of super human powers in this world.

हता आयुः क्षये देल्या: क्लेणेन महता पुनः ।

कर्वेध्यंशनिः : प्रयिता ह्रिणा मुनिसन्तम ॥ 47 ॥

O Best of Munis! When one's longevity expires, one dies; then I cannot understand what glory was manifested by Hari in killing the Daityas? For Fate Killed them; not Hari.

राक्षमणीहणे नूनं गृहीत्पाटप्र पतायनम् ।

कृतं हि वासुदेवेन चीरबच्चरितं तदा ॥ 48 ॥

Was not the doing of Hari like a thief when he stole away the Lady Rukminī and fled quickly to his own place.

मवुगामण्डलं व्यक्तवा समुद्धे कुलसम्मतम् ।

जरासंधभवातेन द्वारकामानं कृतम् ॥ 49 ॥

What did it mean when he fled to Dvārakā city, and quitted his own highly prosperous town Mathurā simply out of the fear of Jarāsandha?

तदा केनापि न जातो भगवान्हिरीक्षरः ।

किंवित्यकृष्टि मे ब्रह्म-कारणं जजलोपनम् ॥ 50 ॥

Did not any body at that time recognise that he was Śrī Bhagavān Hari? O Respected One! Were he Bhagavān, why did He hide himself in Vraja? Please explain the cause to me.

Book IV Chapter XVIII

एते चान्ये च बह्वः सन्देहा वासवीसुत ।

नायाद्ध महाभ्यास सर्वज्ञोरिस डिजोजन्म १ ५ १ ॥

O Muni! These and many other doubts always exist in my mind; you are the best of the Dvijas and blessed; I pray, dost thou remove these doubts.

गोयस्तबैकः सन्देहो हदयार्त्र निवर्तैत ।

पाङ्गालयाः पञ्धभतैलं लोके किं न जुणीस्थितम् ॥ ५२ ॥

सदारावं प्रमाणं हि प्रवर्दिन मनीिणः ।

पशुप्रमं: कक्षे सैतु समर्थेसि संश्रितः ॥ ५३ ॥

O best of Munis! Another doubt exists and is not dispelled and that is secret. Was not the taking of the five husbands by Pāncālī for herself shameful and despised by the society? The good manners and doings are always considered by the learned as he proofs of virtue. Why did those Pāndavas, then, capable in every respect, do this thing like brutes?

भीभेणापि कृतं किं वा देवक्षोपेण भूतले ।

गोलकों ती सम्यपाङ्य यत्नु वंशस्य रक्षणम् ॥ 54 ॥

And what did Bhisma do living like a Deva in this world? May I ask, was has act of producing two sons by a widow and thus preserving his line of ancestors worthy of his name?

धित्यार्पिनिधा: कामं मुनिधिभ: परितर्पितः ।

येन केनाज्यपायेन पुत्रोस्पत्नलक्षण: ॥ 55 ॥

इति श्रीमद्वीभागतते महापुराणे चतुर्बिकन्क्ये

सप्तद्वी3.शयः ।। 17 ।।

The religious sanctions advocated by the Munis "Procreate sons in any way whatsoever" is simply shameful. Fie to this religious sanction.

Here ends the Seventeenth Chapter in the fourth Book of Śrimaddevibhāgavatam of 18000 verses by Maharṣi Veda Vyasa on the questions asked by Janamcajaya.

Book IV Chapter XVIII

CHAPTER XVIII

On the Devi Earth's Going to the Heavens

व्यास उवाच

शूणु राजन्यव्यायीम कृष्णायच चरितं महत् ।

अततारकारणं चैव देद्याश्रितमद्धुतम् ॥ १ ॥

Vyāsa said: O king! Hear in detail the complete life and the deeds in the in carnation of Śri Kṛṣṇa and also the various wonderful achievements by the Goddess of this universe.

धरैकवा भगत्माना रुदती चातिकर्शिता ।

गोक्पधारिणी दीना शीतागघळितविष्टघम् ॥ 2 ॥

Once on a time, the Earth was very much overburdened by the load of wicked kings and She was therefore very much afraid. She then assumed the appearance of a cow and went to the Devaloka crying and very much dejected.

पृष्ठा शेक्न कि तेउघ वर्तेतं भयमित्यव ।

केन वे पीक्षितास्ति त्वं किं ते दुःखं वसुर्पे ॥ ३ ॥

Indra, the lord of the Devas, asked him, O Vasundhare! What is the cause of your fear now? Who has troubled you? What afflictions are you merged in? Please tell me these.

तच्छुचेला ततोवाच शुणु देवेश मेउखिलम् ।

दुःखें पृष्ठसि यचनं च भाराकात्तास्मि मानद ॥ 4 ॥

On hearing Indra's words, the Earth exclaimed: O Respect giver! When You have asked me, I am explaining to you the cause of all my afflictions and sorrows; at present I am overburdened with too much load.

जरासंधो महापापी माफांधे पतिमां ।

शिशुपाललसा चेधः काशीराजः प्रतपावन्त् ॥ 5 ॥

रुक्मी च बलवाक्मो रन्त्रक महावल: ।

शाल्वः सौभपाति: कूर केशी धेनुकवलकों ॥ 6 ॥

सर्वे धर्मविह्नीव्रा परस्पतिवर्धिति: ।

पाणचारा मदोभस्ता: कालत्काप्रा पार्थिवा: ॥ 7 ॥

तैर्हं पीडिता शक्र भागकानानक्षमा विशो ।

किं कंजीम वयं गच्छामि चिन्ता मे महती चिह्ना ॥ 8 ॥

Now is reigning in the earth Jarāsandha, the king of Magadha, a very very vicious persons. Thus the other Śiśupāla, the lord of the Cedis, the uncontrollable Kāśirāja, Rukmī, the powerful Karīsa, the strong Naraka, the Saubhapati Śāiva, the wicked Keśī, Dhenuka, and Vatsaka all these

Srimaddevibhāgavatam

are now in royal positions. O Lord of the Devas! These kings are all devoid of the least trace of virtue, quarrelsome against each other, infatuated with vanity, and addicted to vicious deeds. These have become kings as if they were personified Yamas, the Lords of Death, and are constantly troubling me. I am now unable to carry their loads; where shall I go now?
