This great thought is constantly ailing me.

पीडिताई वर्गेह ज्यव्हिणा प्रभविष्याना ।

ग्रक जानीहि हरिणा दुःखादुःखतरं गता ॥ 9 ॥

याताङं दुर्भेदैलेन कश्यपस्यात्मजेन वै ।

हताउं हिरण्याक्षेण मगना तस्मिन्महाणर्वे ॥ 10 ॥

तदा सुकररूपेण विद्याना नित्तोउपसरी ।

उद्धताउं वरोहण स्थापिति हि स्थिता कृता ॥ 11 ॥

O Vasava! What to tell! The Bhagavān in His Boar Incarnation is the cause of all these my afflictions; O Indra! These present troubles I am fallen into only through Him; for when the cruel Daitya Hiranyāksa; the son of Kaśyapa stole me away and drowned me in the great ocean, then it was Viṣṇu in his Boar incarnation that killed him and rescued me from the ocean and then kept me in this my stable position.

नोदेशमातले स्वस्था सित्ता नया सुखगाथिति ।

न शताउम्पद्ध देवेश भारं चोबुं तुरातनम् ॥ 12 ॥

Had he not then lifted me up, I would have rested safe in the depths of Rasātala; O Lord of the Devas! Now I am quite unable to bear the load of these vicious persons.

अउे तुः: समापाति ह्राद्विक्षतशा कलिः ।

तदारूं पीक्षिता श्रक्र गतानोरम्याशु सातालन् ॥ 13 ॥

O Surendra! The vicious twenty eight Kali is coming quickly in front. Thinking of His influence, it seems to me that I will be very troubled then and will have to go down to Rasātala.

तस्माचं देवेदेशेण दुःखरूपणीवस्य च ।

पारदो भव भार मे हर पार्दो नमामि ते ॥ १४ ॥

Therefore, O Lord of the Devas! I am bowing down before Your feet, Kindly relieve me of my burden and save me from these endless troubles.

इन खाव

इले किं ते कारोम्प्यत बढ़ाणां शरणां ज्ञ ।

अर्ह ततागमिष्यामि स ते दुःखं हरिष्यति ॥ 15 ॥

Indra said: O Earth! I can not do anything for you. You better go and take refuge of Brahmā. I am also going to Him. He will remove all your troubles.

तच्छूता त्वरिता पृथ्वी ब्रह्मलोकं गता तदा ।

गहानीय पुड़तः प्राप्तः सर्वदेवपुरःसरः ॥ 16 ॥

Hearing Indra's words the Earth hurriedly went to the realm of Brahmā and Indra and all the other Devas followed Her; and all reached the Brahmaloka.

सुरभीमगतां तत्र दृष्टीवाच प्रजापति: ।

महीं जाता महात्राज ध्यानेन समुपस्थितिम् ॥ 17 ॥

कस्मातुवृति कल्याणि किं ते दुःखं वदायुम् ।

पीडिताउपि च केन त्व पाचावारेण भवेद् ॥ 18 ॥

O King! The Grand Father Brahmā saw the Earth coming to him and through the power of meditation, found out the cause of Her coming and said: O Auspicious One! why are you crying? What troubles You have now? What wicked person has given You troubles.

प्रोताच

कलिरायाति दुर्भोर्ग विषयेमि तद्वायादरुम् ।

पाणाचाताः प्रजास्तत्र भविष्यन्ति जनतन्ते ॥ 19 ॥

The Earth said: O Lord of the Earth! The vicious Kali is coming before; under Its influence the subjects will be horribly vicious; therefore I am very much afraid of this Kali.

राजानछ्छा दुराचाराः परस्यरिवीरोधिनः ।

चौराकर्मरतः सर्वे ग्रहसाः पूर्णवैरिणः ॥ 20 ॥

In the beginning of this Kali Yuga, the ancient enemies, the Asuras have now incarnated on this earth as kings. They will be extremely wicked, quarreling against each other, and will be clever in stealing others things. There is no doubt in these.

तान्हत्वा नृपतीभारं हर मेध्ध पितामह ।

पीडितास्तिम महाराज सैन्यभरणे भूभुताम् ॥ 21 ॥

Book IV Chapter XVIII

O Grand Father! Now kill these vicious kings and relieve my burden. O Lord! I am very much troubled by the armies of these kings.

ब्रशोवाच

नाहं शक्तसधा देयि भारावतरणे तव ।

गच्छाव: सर्वनं विशाणेदैवद्वेस्य चक्रिण: ॥ 2 2 2 ॥

Brahmā said: O Goddess! I, too, am unable like Indra to remove your lad. Let us go to that Viṣṇu, the Holder of the disc.

स ते भारपानेदों वै कतियिति जनादनैः ।

पूर्वं मयापि ते कार्य चिन्तितं सुविचारये च। २३ ३ ॥

तत्र गच्छ सुरप्रेक्षि यत्र देवो जनादनैः ।

That Janārdana will remove your burden. I thought of this well before and settled what to do.

व्यास उवाच

इत्युक्चा वेदकर्तोंसी पुरकृत्य सुरांश गाम ॥ 24 ॥

जगाम विणुसन्दर्भ हंसाक्छक्षतुर्भुखः ।

नुहाव वेदवाक्येधि भक्तिप्रवणमानसः ॥ 25 ॥

Vyāsa said: Thus saying, the four faced Brahmā, the Author of the Vedas, rode on His Hamsa Vehicle taking in front the Goddess Earth; and the Devas went to Viṣṇu and began to praise Viṣṇu Janārdana, the Deva of the Devas, with the words of the Vedas with full devotion.

ब्रह्मोवाच

सहसशीर्षत्वमीर सहसाधः सहसघात् ।

चं वेदुपुभः पूर्वे देवदेवः सनातनः ॥ 26 ॥

Brahmā said: Thou hast thousand heads, thousand faces, thousand feet. Thou art the Purusa of the Vedas, Thou art the Deva of the Devas, and Thou art Eternal.

भूतपूर्वे भविष्यच्च वर्तमानं च यद्धिणो ।

अमरत्वं तथा दत्तमसाकं च रमापते ॥ 27 ॥

O Omnipresent! Thou art the Past, Present, and Future! O Lord of Lakṣmī! Thou hast awarded immortality unto us.

एलावामहिता रेउसित को न येति जगत्ये ।

तवं कर्तांप्रयिता हर्तों तवं सर्वगतिरीश्रः ॥ 28 ॥

Thou art the Creator of universe, the Preserver

and the Destroyer; Thou art the One and the Only goal and thou art the God. Every body knows that all these glories belong to Thec.

यात्र उवाच

इतीक्षितः प्रभृविंषुः प्रसजो गकडधज्ञः ।

दर्शनं च ददौ तेभ्यो ब्रहामिष्यो मतलागयः ॥ 29 ॥

Vyāsa said: O king! When Brahmā praised thus, Viṣṇu whose sign was Garuda, was highly pleased and appeared before Brahmā and the other Devas.

पप्रच्छ स्वागतं देदायमत्रवदनो हरि: ।

ततस्वागनने तेषां कारणं च सचितस्तम् ॥ 30 ॥

The Bhagavān asked them about their welfare and enquired in detail into the cause of their arrival there.

तमुवाचाबजी नत्वा धागुःखं च संस्मन्त् ।

भारात्वरणं विद्यो कर्तव्यं ते जनादेन ॥ 31 ॥

Brahmā them bowed down to Him and, remembering the cause of the sorrows of the Goddess Earth, said: O Lord! Thou art now to relieve the burden of the Earth.

भूवि धृत्वाउतरां त्वं द्वारपाने समापते ।

हत्वा दुहाधुगनुखां हर भारं द्वानिधे ॥ 32 ॥

O Thou, Ocean of mercy! When the end of the Dvāpara yuga will come Thou art to incarnate in the world and kill the wicked kings and thus to relieve the burden of the Earth.

विष्णुकाव

नाहं स्वतन्य एवात्र न बहा न शिवतशा ।

नेन्ट्रोसिनं यमस्तद्धा न सुयों वक्रणतशा ॥ 33 ॥

योगमावावशे सर्वीमिंट स्वावरजुकम् ।

ब्रह्मादित्वापयन्ते प्रथितं गुणयुक्तः ॥ 34 ॥

Viṣṇu said: I am not independent in these matters; why I? Brahmā Maheśa, Indra, Agni, Yama, Viśvakarmā, the Sun and Varuna and other Devas, no body is independent. This whole universe, moving and unmoving is existing under the control of the Yoga Māyā; and from Brahmā up to the clot of grass, all are strung into the thread of Her Three qualities.

यथा सा सैच्छया पूर्व कर्तूमिच्छति सुठत ।

तथा कातीत सहिता वयं सर्वैपि तद्गशः ॥ 3 5 ॥

O One of good vows! Whatever that Yoga Māyā, the Supreme Goddess, Who is All will, Whose mouth is inward, Who does good at all times, what she wills She does that at any time. You should all know that we are entirely under Her control.

यद्रहं स्वां स्वतन्त्रो वै विंतानु पिधा किल ।

कृतोउपबं मत्थवपुः कच्छणे या महापर्णे ॥ ३६ ॥

तिर्ययोगिषुकोभोग:काक्लीति:किंसुवं पुनः।

किं पुण्यं किं फलं तत श्रुवयोगिनतासय मे ॥ 37 ॥

You better think that were I independent, what for would I have stayed in the great ocean, incarnating in the Fish and Tortoise Bodies? O Brahmin! What name or pleasure is there in enjoyment in the body of lower animals! What holy merit or any other reward may I expect from being born in the wombs of lower animals?

कोलीवास्य तुसिंहो वा वामनोवासभवं कृतः।

अमदनिमुन्तः कमलास्पधवेयं पितामह ॥ 38 ॥

What is the reason that I assume the body of a Boar? or of a Man-Lion? or of a Dwarf? Why was I born as the son of Jamadagni. Especially why did I, being born of that high-souled Brāhmin Jamadagni, and being the best of the Brāhmins, do the most atrocious act like that of a heartless brute and fill up the lakes with their blood.

नूरुर्सं वा कवं कर्म कृतलावन्मि भूतले ।

क्षनजैसु हृदास्वान्मूरयेवं कवं पुनः ॥ 39 ॥

तत्कवं जमदानेश्ठ पुत्रो भूत्वा द्विजोतभः ।

क्षरियान्हतलानाजी निर्दोयो भर्गेभगनपि ॥ 40 ॥

Alas! I killed the Ksatriyas mercilessly; to say nothing more than that I killed the sons that were then in the wombs. Were I independent, what for I would have done these horrible and cruel deeds! O Lord of the Devas! See again.

रामो भूत्वाथ देवेन्द्र प्राविशड़ंडके वनम् ।

प्रदातिक्षीरवासघ जटावकल्बानपूनः ॥ 4 1 ॥

In my Rāma incarnation I roamed on foot,

helpless and without any provision, in the fearful Dandaka forest unfrequented by any body, wearing clotted hair, bark, rags, like a man who feels no shame, and behaved like a hunter and killed many animals.

असहायो हुपाधयो भीषणे निर्जने बने ।

कुर्वेत्राखेदकं तत्र व्यचरं वियगतप्रः ॥ 42 ॥

न ज्ञातावमूगं हिंमे मायया चिहास्तसदा ।

उठजे जानकीं त्यक्त्वा निर्गतस्तदावनुगः ॥ 43 ॥

लक्षणणोप्र च तां त्यक्त्वा निर्गतो मदवादुगाः।

वारितोप्र यमावत्थं मोहितः प्राकृतैपुर्गेः ॥ 44 ॥

Being under the delusion of Māyā, I could not make out the real nature of the golden deer; consequently leaving Jānaki in the thatched cottage, I went out perusing the deer. Though repeatedly warned by me not to leave the place, Lakṣmana was moved by the qualities of Prakṛti, forsook her and went out on my search.

विश्वरूपं ततः कृत्वा रावणः कयदाकृतः ।

जहार तरसा रक्षो जानकां शोककर्शिताम् ॥ 4 5 ॥

Then the hypocrite Rāvaṇa, the king of the Rāksāsas, under the garb of a beggar; stole away by force the daughter of Janaka, who had become very lean on account of sorrows.

दुखातेन मय तत्र सर्दितं च चने वने ।

सुप्रीयेण च मित्रतं कृतं कार्यवर्गानया ॥ 46 ॥

I was very much distressed owing to the separation from my dear wife, and roamed about weeping sorely in forest and formed friendship with Sugriva, under the influence of the circumstances.

अन्यायेन हतो वाली शापाच्छेव निवारितः ।

सहायान्नानान्कृत्वा लंकायां चलितं पुनः ॥ 47 ॥

It was an act of gross injustice on my part to kill Vālī, the king of the monkeys. I freed him from his curse; afterwards, aided by the monkeys, I had to go to Lanka.

बद्धोर्भहं नापयाणीछ लक्ष्मणघ मानुनजः ।

विसंजी पत्तिते दृष्टवा वानरा विस्मयं नातः ॥ 48 ॥

When my younger Lakṣmana and myself were both enchained under the chain of the serpents,

Book IV Chapter XVIII

Någapåsa, and were senseless, the monkeys all were astonished.

गऊऊने तद्ध़प्रान्य मोचिती धातरी किल ।

चिन्ता मे महती जाता देई किं का कार्थस्थिति ॥ 49 ॥

Then Garuda came and freed us the two brothers, from those Någapåsås! I considered then what adverse inauspicious circumstances Fate sometimes ordians on our lot.

हतं राज्यं बने वासो मृत: तातः प्रिया हृता ।

युद्धं कटं द्वतार्वेममरे किं वा कार्विथिति ॥ 50 ॥

I lost my kingdom, lived in the forests, my father died, Jānakī was stolen and I had to suffer extreme troubles in very deadly battles; I could not know what worse fate still awaited for us?

प्रथम् तु महादुःखमार्जस्य वनाश्रयम् ।

राजपुत्राश्रावतर्येव धनहीनश्र निर्भितः ॥ 51 ॥

O Suras! What more calamity can you expect to befall any person than—that I was from the very first deprived of my kingdoms and wealth, and had to go to the forest with the princess Sitā dwelling in and taking shelter in a dense forest!

वार्टिकार्जि विरा मे न दत्ता वन्निर्भिये ।

पदातिरसहायोर्द्ध नधनीत्रश्र सिनीर । II 52 II

At the time of my going to the forest my father did not give a single penny; penniless and helpless I had to get out of Ayodhyā on foot.

वतूर्दैशि वर्षिणि नितानि च तदा मदा ।

क्षात्रं धर्म परित्यज्य व्याधयुत्त्या महावने । ५३ ॥

I was compelled to leave my Kṣattriya Dharma and take up the avocation of hunter and thus to spend fourteen years in forest.

दैवादुढे जयः प्राप्तो नित्ञोरसौ महासुरः ।

ज्ञानीता च पुनः सीता प्राप्ताप्रोथया माया तथा ॥ ५४ ॥

After that, under the benign influence of Fate, I was able to kill that Asura Rāvaṇa and got the victory in the battle and was able to bring back dear Sītā to Ayodhyā.

वर्षोरि कतिचित्रा सुखं संसारस्म्यवम् ।

प्राप्ति राज्यं च सम्पूर्णं कोसलाप्रधितिहत ॥ 55 ॥

There I succeeded in becoming the ruler of the

kingdom Kośala with its subjects and got the full kingdom and enjoyed for a few years the pleasures of the world.

पुरैव वर्तमानेन प्राप्ताराजेन वै तदा ।

लेकापवादपीतिन तत्का सीता वने मया ॥ 56 ॥

कान्ताविरहर्ने दुःखं पुनः प्राप्तं दुगासदम् ।

पातालं सा गता पश्धाद्वी तिभत्या धातकाना ॥ 57 ॥

The stealing away of Sītā took place at the first outset; next I got my kingdom; then the subjects began to circulated the bad name regarding Jānakī; and I being afraid of that, deported her into exile in the forest. At that time I had to suffer again extreme pain and agony due to the serparation from my wife. Then the daughter of the Goddess Earth penetrated into the Earth and got down to the Patāla.

एवं रायावततोर्षि दुःखं प्राप्तं निरितमम् ।

परतन्त्रणे मे नूनं स्वतन्तः को भवेत्तदा ॥ 58 ॥

O Devas! When I had to depend on Fate and to suffer so many troubles incessantly, where else can you dare to say that an independent man exists.

पश्राकालवशामुपाव: स्वर्गों में भातृभिनः सह ।

पततन्यस्य का वातां वतन्या विबुघेने वे ॥ ५९ ॥

Afterwards under the influence of Time, I had to go to Heaven with my brother. Let all this point to what it may, the intelligent learned people can say what an amount of mishaps takes place to one who is dependent!

पतन्तोउस्यहं नूनं पच्योगेने निशामय ।

तथा त्वमपि रूक्ष सर्वे चान्ये सुरोतमाः ॥ 60 ॥

डीत श्रीमदेश्वीभागते महापुराणे

चतुर्विकन्येउधारशोअयाः ।। 18 ॥ 11

O One born from the Lotus! You hear my word; I am in every way dependent; why I? Rudra, You and all those Suras are fully dependent.

Here ends the Eighteenth Chapter of

the Fourth Book of the Mahā Purānam

Śrimaddevithāgāvatam of 18,000

verses by Maharṣi Veda Vyāsa

on the Devi Earth's going to

the Heavens.

CHAPTER XIX

On Chanting the Hunns to the Devi

ཕམ་ཟམས:

སྟོལྟུལ་ཀྱི་སྟོལ་ཀྱི་སྟོལ་ཀྱི་སྟོལ་ཀྱི་སྟོལ་ཀྲི་སྟོལ་ཀྱི་སྟོལ་ཀྱི་སྟོལ་ཀྱི་སྟའི་སྟའི་སྟའི་སྟའི་སྟའི་སྟའི་སྟའི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་ཁྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་བྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་གྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་ཀྱི་སྟལ་

We, too, are fascinated by that Māyā; and hence we also, being blinded by that, do not at all remember. That Highest Eternal Purusa, calm and quiet, the World-Teacher, the Highest Self, of the nature of Pure Existence, Intelligence and Bliss.

अर्ह विद्यारूई बढ़ा शिवोड़मिति मोहिताः ।

न जानीमो वयं धातः परं वस्तु सनातनम् ॥ 3 ॥

O Brahma! I am Visnu, I am Brahmā, I am Rudra, thus our I-ness Aharikāra has blinded our eyesight; and we are made unable to recognise That Eternal Highest Self.

यन्यामाघौहित्राई सदा वर्ते परात्मनः ।

परवान् दारभाङ्गती माधिकस्य यथा यरे ॥ 4 ॥

As the wooden dolls dance according to the will of the player, the magician, I also am similarly fascinated by the Māyā and am thus incessantly rolling about like a dependent man.

भवताउपि तथा दूषा विभूतितस्य चाढ़ुता ।

करणादी भवयुक्तेन मयापि च सुधाणें ॥ 5 ॥

मिण्हाप्रेय 8 मन्त्राविद्ये रासमण्डले ।

समाजे तत्र सा दृष्टा श्रुता न वचसाधि च ॥ 6 ॥

O Brahma! In the beginning of the Kalpa, Maheśvara, You and I saw the wonderful unspeakable form and glory of that Highest Self at the time of Rāsamandala in the Māni Dvipa where there was the Mandāra Tree and the Devas assembled. Then I also saw that wonderful thing a second time in the Sudh\( ^{2} \):nava ocean of nectar,

and the most wonderful of it is this: that until we were able to See that Form, we did not hear anything of Her before!

तस्माता परमां गर्फिंक सर्वेनवघ सुरा: शिवगम् ।

सर्वकामप्रदो मायामाठी शर्फिंक परावमाः ॥ ७ ॥

Therefore, O Devas! To-day do you all remember that Prime Force, the Highest Self, that all beneficent Force that yields all good and auspicious things; That Very Force will fulfill now all your desires.

व्यास उवाच

इतुक्ता हरिणा देना बढ़ाठा भुवनेश्वीम् ।

सस्मर्नसा देहीं योगमायांं सनातनीम् ॥ ८ ॥

Vyāsa said: O king! No sooner the Bhagavān Hari addressed thus, Brahmā and the other Devas at once mentally began to meditate on that Eternal Yoga Māyā, the Goddess of the Universe.

स्मृतमात्रा तदा देही प्रत्यक्षं दर्जनं दही ।

पाणाङ्गशरापीतिप्रा देहो जगराणा ।

इद्धया प्रमुदिता देहान्तुडुवर्ता सुर्दर्गना ॥ 9 ॥

Being thus meditated, at once appeared before their eyesight the Devi, the Goddess of the Universe, whose colours were like the blood red Java flower, holding in two of Her hands noose, hook, or goad, while the third hand indicated favour and the fourth hand bade all discard every sort of fear.

देवा ऊचुः

कर्णनाघाढ्या तन्त्रविस्कृतीयां विषावसोः ।

तथा जगद्धतेस्या निर्मतं तां नत्ता बयम् ॥ 10 ॥

As the web comes out of a spider of fire and sparks come out, so this whole Universe comes out of that Goddess. We all bow down before Her with our humble hearts, full of devotion.

यन्मायाशक्तिसंकल्पं जानसर्वे चराचम् ।

तां चितं धुवनाधीशां सरमाः कर्णाणिधा ॥ 11 ॥

Book IV Chapter XIX

We all salute to that Goddess of the Universe, Bhuvanesvari, under Whose Māyīc force this whole Universe, moving and unmoving, is recreated; Who is All-Intelligence and the Ocean of Mercy.

यद्धानाद्वोत्थितिर्वज्ञानाद्ववनाशनम् ।

संविद्धुपं च तं दौंदी स्मारमः सा प्रधोद्यात् ॥ 12 ॥

This world appears real to him who is unable to realise Her Real Essence; and the world drops off as unreal no sooner he realises Her Presence. We all meditate on that All Intelligent Goddess and we all pray to Her that She may grant unto us more strength to meditate Her and Her alone so.

मातलस्थे च विवाईं चर्यशक्न्चे च धीमाहि ।

तनो देवी प्रचोदयात् ॥ १३ ॥

We all want to know Mahā Lakṣmī, we all meditate on the Omnipotent; May the Goddess grant unto us strength to meditate on Her so.

भारन्तनैः स्म भुवनानिरंतरं प्रसीद

गं नो विधिके कुरु कार्यमिद्ध द्वयरं ।

भारं हरव विवित्रव सुरारिवर्ग महा

महेश्वर सतां कुरु गं भवानि ॥ 14 ॥

O Thou, the Remover of the world's afflictions! Best pleased unto us; O Thou, kind hearted! Do this work for us and promote our good. O Thou, Lord of the Earth! Dost Thou relieve the burden of the earth by killing these Asuras and bring on our welfare.

यद्वेबुबालिध दयसे न सुरान्कदाचिक्ति ने

क्षमा रणमुपुडे सिरशरे: ग्रहरुम् ।

एतचवैय गदितं ननु यक्षल्पं

धूला तृणं दह इतागपदाधिक्षणैः ॥ 1 5 ॥

O Thou, Lotus-Eyed! If Thou dost not show Thy mercy towards the gods, they will never be able to strike their enemies with their weapons in the battlefield. O Goddess! Thou didst verify the truth of this before, when Thou didst assume the appearance of a Yakṣa and utter the following sentences “O Fire! You burn this blade of grass” etc. (in the Kena Talavakāra Upaniṣad).

कंस: कुजोर्श यवनेन्सुतश केर्शी

वाईरूशो बकबकीखशाल्यमुख्या: 1

येउ-ये तथा नृपतयो भूथि सन्ति तांस्वं

हत्वा हरस्व जगतो भरमाशु मातः ॥ 16 ॥

O Mother! Kañsa, Bhauma, Kālayavana, Keśī, Jarāsandha, the son of Brhadratha, Vaka, Pūtanā, Khara, Śalva and other vicious kings respectively are dwelling on the earth. Dost Thou better kill them and relieve the burden of the Earth.

वे विद्यान्ना न निहाना: किल शक्रेशण

वे वा विश्रहा जलजाशिक पुरन्देशे।।

ते ते पुरुखं सुरुखकंरं सुमीक्षमाणाः।

संख्ये शर्यिविन्हता निजलौतया ते ॥ 17 ॥

O Lotus-Eyed! Thou hadst killed those Asuras without any effort that were not slain by Viṣṇu or Maheśvara and they ended their lives, while they were beholding Thy pleasing countenance.

शर्फिकं विना हरित्वगुखा: सुराङ

नैवेक्षा विचितन्तु तत देवदेवेल ।

किं धारणाविरहितः प्रपूर्णवन्तो

धर्तु धरां च रजनीशकलावतंसे ॥ 18 ॥

O Goddess! Holding Moon on Her forehead! These Hari, Hara, Brahmā and the other gods are unable to move a step without the help of Force; nothing to speak any further, the thousand headed King of Serpents is unable to hold this earth even for a moment, were he not provided with the Śakti of upholding this world.

इन्र उवाच

वाचा विना विधिरलं भवतीह विध्ध

कर्तृ हरि: किमु स्मारहितोध पातुम् ।

संहर्तुभीग उभ्योजितून ईशः किं ते

ताभिरेव सहिता: प्रभव: प्रजेशः ॥ १९ ॥

Indra said: O Bhagavati! Would Brahmā be ever able to create this world without the aid of Sarasvatī! Would Viṣṇu, the Deva of the Devas, be ever able to preserve it without the aid of Laksmī or would Mahesvara be ever able to destroy this world without the help of His Umā; no, never! These Devas, the Trinity, are able to take their

Šrimaddevibhāgavatam

respective parts in the keeping up of the world simply because they are united with the forces, incarnate in Sarasvatī, etc., who are again parts of Thee.

विष्णुर्वाच

कर्तु प्रभुने ईहिणो न कदाचनाहं

नाथीश्रस्तव कतारहितक्षिलोक्या: ।

कर्तु प्रभुचमनयोउत्र तथा विहर्तु त्वं

वै समस्तविषयेक्षिरे भासि नूनम् ॥ 20 ॥

Visṇu said: O Vimale! Were we deprived of Thy Force, the Creator would never have been able to create the world, I would never have been able to preserve it and Maheśvara would never have been able to destroy Therefore O Devi! Thou alone art reigning as the Supreme Goddess amidst all the glories in the creation, preservation, and destruction of this Universe.

व्यास उवाच

एवं सुत्वा तदा देवी तानाह विबुधेश्वान् ।

कि तत्कार्यं वदल्वच कंपिभ विगतलयतः ॥ 21 ॥

असाध्यमपि लोकेश्मितलकोमीय सुरीमतान् ।

शंसन्तु भवतां दुःखं धरायाङ्य सुरोतमाः ॥ 22 ॥

Vyāsa said: O king! Thus praised by the Gods, the Goddess then addressed them: O Devas! Let all anxities leave you all; I will do what the Devas have desired, even if be very difficult to do in this world; now explain what is troubling the Goddess Earth.

देवा कचुः

वसुधैवं भयाकान्ता सम्पात विबुधान्यति ।

रूदती वेयपाना च पीडिता दृष्यपुर्जेः ॥ 23 ॥

The Devas replied: The wicked kings are troubling very much this Earth. She cannot any longer bear their burden; therefore She came crying unto us.

भागपहर्णं चायया: कतिथं घुवनेश्वी ।

देवानामीमितं कार्यमेतदेवापुना शिशे ॥ 24 ॥

O Bhuvaneśvari! Now it is Your duty to remove this load of earth. O Auspicious One! Know this, the desire of the gods.

घातितसु पुरा मातस्वया महिषरूपभृत ।

दानवोर्गतिवलाकान्तसत्सहायांश्छीदिगः ॥ 25 ॥

तथा शुभ्यो निशुभ्यश रत्कौजास्तधापरः ।

चणदमुपडी महावीर्याँ तवेय धूपतोचनः ॥ 26 ॥

दुर्मुखो दुःसह्छैव कारलश्रातीलयैवान् ।

अन्ये च बहवः क्रारास्तव्यैव च निपातितः ॥ 27 ॥

O Mother! In days of yore, You have killed the most powerful Dānava named Mahiṣa, attended by lacs and lacs of Dānavas and Daityas; What to speak! You have slain Śumbha, Niśumbha, Raktabīja, the very powerful Canda Mungda, Dhūmralocana, Durmukha, Duhsaha, the strong Karāla and many other cruel Dānavas.

तथैव च सुरार्थि जहि सर्वान्हीशान्न ।

“ भारं हर धान्याश्र उदुरं दुषुधुजाम् ॥”

Now, similarly kill the wicked kings, the enemies of the Gods and save the Earth from their heavy loads.

व्यास उवाच

इतुक्ता सा तदा देवे दोवानाहीयका शिवा ॥ 28 ॥

समूहस्यासितापाङ्गी मेघमाधीराय शिता ।

Vyāsa said: Thus addressed by the Devas, the Most Auspicious One, the Devi, whose colour was black and who looked askance, said smiling in a tone, grave and deep, like that of the rumbling of a raincloud.

श्रीदेख्याच

मयंदे चिन्तितं पूर्वमैशालत्रणं सुरा: ॥ 29 ॥

भारावतरणं चैव वथा स्यादुपुर्णपुर्णम् ।

O Suras! I have already thought over how the burden of those wicked kings, the parts incarnate of Asuras, can be removed.

मया सर्वे निर्तेल्या दैत्येणा ये महीभुजाः ॥ 30 ॥

मागधाडा महाभागा: स्वशक्त्या मंदतेजसः।

I will, by My own power, rob them of their powers, of that highly prosperous and affluent Jārāsandha, the king of Magadha, and others, the parts incarnate of the wicked Asuras, that are now shining on the Earth.

Book IV Chapter XIX

भविद्वारि वंरैगीरवतीयं धरातले ॥ 31 ॥

मच्छफिन्नेः कतव्यं भारावतरणं सुरा: ।

O Devas! You would all better go down and incarnate yourselves as part incarnations, impregnated with My power, and help thus in the removal of the load of this earth.

क्यपयो भारया सार्य दिविनानां प्रजापनि: ॥ 32 ॥

यादवानां कुले पूर्वं भविताने उनककुदुपि: ।

The Deva Prajāpati Maharṣi Kaśyapa would first of all go with his his wife and incarnate as Ānaka Dundubhi Vasudeva, in the race of Yadu.

तथैव भृगुगायाड़े भगवाल्विष्णुव्यय: ॥ 33 ॥

अंगेन भविता तत्र वसुदेवसुत्तो हरि: ।

The Imperishable Bhagavān Viṣṇu will also incarnate as part, owing to the curse of Bhṛgu, as the son of Vasudeva.

त्वारुई प्रभविष्यामि यग्रोतायां च गोकुले ।। 34 ॥

कार्य सर्वे कार्यमिम सुणां सुरसन्तमाः ।

O Devas! I will be born, then, in the womb of Yaśodā, in Gokula and will accomplish all the ends of the Gods.

कारागारे गत विष्णु प्रापयिष्यामि गोकुले ॥ 35 ॥

शेपं च देवक्रीगभित्रापयिष्यामि रेहिणीम् ।

I Will send Viṣṇu from the prison to the Gokula; also I will transfer Ananta from the womb of Devaki to the womb of Rohin?

मच्छकरणोघचिती ती च कततीं दुरसंक्षयम् ॥ 36 ॥

दुरानां भूषुजां कामं द्वापराने सुनिश्छितम् ।

They two will grow, day by day, by My Force and at the end of the Dvāpara Yuga, will no doubt kill the wicked kings.

इंत्रणो5यज्ञनः साक्षात्क्रियित वलक्षायम् ॥ 37 ॥

धर्मारी3पि महाराजो भाविष्यित युधिष्ठि: ।

वाय्वेगो भीमसेन्नाशिक्यंगी च यमावपि ॥ 38 ॥

वसोरोंगे5य गोगेयः क्रियिति वलक्षायम् ।

Arjuna too, the part incarnate of Indra, will destroy the armies of those kings. Yudhishira, the part incarnate of Dharma, Bhimseña, that of of Vāyu, Nakula and Sahadeva the incarnate of the two Aśvins, and Bhismā, the incarnate of Vasu as

the son of the Gangā, will take their respective births and exhaust their armies.

जजत् च भवन्तोड्य धाग भवतु सुस्धिता ॥ 39 ॥

भागवतलर्ग नूनं कारिणीम सुरोत्तमाः ।

O Devas! Now be calm and quiet and go. Let the Earth be also calm and quiet; be confident that I will certainly remove the load of this earth.

कृत्वा निमिणासांतान्यराजलगड़र नं संशया:। ४० ॥

कृकक्षेत्र कारिष्यामि क्षत्रियाणां च संक्षयम् ।

I will make the above mentioned Gods as my instruments merely and I will destroy, by My own force, the Ksatryas in the battlefield of Kurukşetra.

असुर्येधां मतिसनृष्णा ममताधिमाता सुहा ॥ 41 ॥

जिगीषा मदनो मोहो दोर्भेनैश्वन्ति याद्वाः ।

Malice, jealousy, foul intention, desire, attachment, vanity, covetuousness, desire for victory, lust, and fascination are the vices that will destroy the Yádavas.

बाहाणस्य च शापेन वंशाणो भविष्यति ॥ 42 ॥

भगवानपि शापेन त्वक्ष्यतैतक्लेवम् ।

The race of Yadu will end owing to the curse of a Brāhmaṇa. The Bhagavān also will leave His mortal coil due to a curse.

भवन्तोर्पि निजांगीश सहाया: शार्ड्धिन्वनः ॥ 4 3 ॥

प्रभन्तु सनारीका मधुरायां च गोकुले ।

Now go and incarnate in your respective parts, with your wives, in Gokula and Mathurā, and be helpers in the work of the Lord.

व्यास उवाच

इन्युक्चलांदईये देही योगायामा परातमः ॥ 44 ॥

सधारा वे सुरा: सर्वे जनयु: स्वान्यालयानि च ।

धराशि सुविधा ज्ञान तस्य बावनेने तीचिति ॥ 45 ॥

Vyāsa said: Saying thus, the Devi the Goddess of the Universe, the Māyā incarnate of the Highest Self disappeared. The Devas and the Earth went to their respective places. O king Janamejaya! the Goddess Earth was pleased with the Devi's words, and being quiet, remained surrounded with various medicinal plants and creepers.

<table><tr><td>अोषधीवीरूघोपेतात बभूव जनमेव 1प्रजाश्र सुस्तिने जाता द्विजाश्रामुसंहोदयम्।सन्तुहा मुनयः सर्वे बभूवुर्धर्मततपरा: ॥ 46 ॥इति श्रीमद्वीभागवते महापुराणे चतुर्वकन्न्येएकॉनिवीरोस्थाय: ॥ 19 ॥</td></tr></table>

Then the subjects felt peace and pleasure, the Brāhmaṇas also grew stronger in happiness and

porrseity, and the Munis became glad and began to perform their religious acts with fresh zeal and alacrity.

Here ends the Nineteenth Chapter in the Fourth Book of Śrimaddevibhāgavatam of 18,000 verses, the Mahā Purānam, on chanting the hymns to the Devi by Maharsi Veda Vyāsa.

- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -

Šrimaddevibhāgavatam

CHAPTER XX

On Devaki's Marriage

व्यास उवाच:

शृणु भारत वक्ष्यामि भारावतरणं तथा ।

कुरक्षेत्र प्रभासे च श्रपितं योगमायया ॥ १ ॥

यदुवंशे समुचितिथ्योगरिततेजसः ।

भूगृगाप्यतपेन महामायावलेन च ॥ 2 ॥

Vyāsa said: O Bharata! I now narrate to you about the relief of the burden of the Earth, the destruction by the Yoga Māyā of the armies at Kuruksetra and Prabhāsa, the two sacred places, and about the birth, due to the curse of Bhru, of the Bhagavān Hari, of unparalleled prowess, under the influence of Mahāmāyā, in the race of Yadu. Now hear.

ऐक्षितिपासमुत्तारिनितिमिति मे पति: ।

मायया विहित्तो योगो विष्णोजेन्म धरातले ॥ 3 ॥

Viṣnu's taking birth in the world was effected under the circumstances caused by Yoga Māyā, to relieve the burden of the Earth; this is my opinion.

Note: Prabhāsa is a well-known place of pilgrimage near Dvārakā, in Gujarat.

एकं विश्रं नृप देवी सा ब्रह्माविष्युरापि ।

नर्तेबलिनं माया विगुणा न पराधिकमु ॥ 4 ॥

O king! When the Goddess Mahāmāyā, the Incarnate of the three qualities can make Brahmā, Viṣṇu and the other Devas dance incessantly as their Internal Controller, then what wonder, that She would appear charming to the other Jivas and make them dance incessantly, as woodon dolls.

गर्भवासोद्रदं वुःखं विष्णुस्नायुसंगुतम् ।

विष्योरापवितं सव्यरयया विगतलीलाया ॥ 5 ॥

All the troubles incurred in remaining in the womb, amidst the urine, excreta and tissues, She had brought to bear finally on Viṣṇu by Her ever famous Līlā (Divine Sport).

पुरा रायावततेर्धि निर्जरा वानसा: कृता: ।

विवितं ते यथा विद्युर्जु:ख्यापोगे मोहिताः ॥ 6 ॥

In days of yore, in Rāma incarnation, She, That Supreme Goddess made the Gods become monkeys and you know very well already, what an amount of trouble Bhagavān Viṣṇu had to undergo by the hard iron chain of bondage, “This is mine, this is I”, etc., imposed by Māyā.

अर्ह मेमिति पाशेन सुवृद्धेन नाराधि ।

योगिनो मुक्त्मंगाष मुक्रिकामा मुप्रहक्ष: ॥ 7 ॥

This Yogis who want final liberation and who have abandoned all their attachments and who want devotion, they worship the Supreme Goddess of the Universe, the Incarnate of Auspiciousness.

तामेव सुप्रपासने देर्वि विश्रेष्ठश्री शिवगम् ।

तद्धक्तिलेणलेशांश लेणलेशलवांगकम् ॥ 8 ॥

O king! Who will not serve Her? A trace of devotion towards Whom effects at once the salvation of the Jivas.

लब्धा सुनी घोबजंतुलां न सेवेत को जनः ।

भुवनेशीलेव वक्ने द्वातिति भुवनजयम् ॥ 9 ॥

If any human being utters simply the name "Bhuvaneśvari" (the Goddess of Universe) She gives him the three worlds; and if any one cries and utters for help "Save me," then the Goddess of the Universe, being unable to find anything that

Book IV Chapter XX

She can repay him as a fit recompense for his Utterance, becomes Herself indebted to that man. मां पातीत्यय चचसो देवाभावादुपगाधिता ।

विद्यार्थिवीत तस्या ऋे रूपे जानीहि पार्थिव ॥ 10 ॥

O King! Know Vidya (knowledge) and Avidyā (non-knowledge, spiritual ignorance, Iowner knowledge) Her two forms; Vidya gives liberation; Avidyā causes bondage.

विद्याभू मुचते जन्तुबंधते प्रविद्धया पुनः ।

ब्रहा विद्युघ हरूघ सर्वे तस्य वाजपानुः ॥ १ १ ॥

Brahmā, Viṣṇu Rudra, all these and their Avatāras are Her instruments and are under Her subjection, are if they as all fastened by a cord.

अवतारा: सर्वे एवं यन्थिता इव दामधि: ।

कदाचिच्च सुखं घुंफे वैकुण्डे श्रीसागरे ॥ 12 ॥

कदाचिलुकृते युद्धं दानवैर्विवतनरै: ।

हरि: कदाचिद्धान्यै वित्तान्यकरोत्ति च ॥ 13 ॥

कदाचिच्च तपस्तीत्र तीर्थे चरित सुमत ।

कदाचिच्छ्यने शेते योगानिद्रामुगाधिनः ॥ 14 ॥

The Bhagavān Hari sometimes resides in Vaikunthā, sometimes resides in the sea of milk and enjoys pleasures, sometimes fights with the powerful Dānavas, sometimes performs extensive sacrificial ceremonies sometimes performs severe asceticism and sometimes takes to deep sleep under the guidance of Yoga Māyā. Thus He never becomes free and independent.

न स्वतनः क्वाधिषच भगवान्यमुसूनः।

तथा बढ़ा तथा रूस्तलधेन्द्रो वरणो यमः ॥ 15 ॥

कुवेरीउनी सीन्द्र च हवाउन्ये सुरसनाः ।

मुन्यः सनकाध्यां ससिद्धायतलयाये ॥ 16 ॥

सर्वेप्रवारणगा नित्यं पशुतालीव नस्य च ।

O King! And like Viṣṇu, Brahmā, Rudra, the other Gods Indra, Varuna, Yama, Kubera, Agni, the Sun, Moon and other celestial gods, the Sanaka and other Munis, Vasisītha and other Rṣis, all are incessantly controlled by the Supreme Goddess, as if they are the dolls in the hands of a playing magician.

नसि प्रोता यथा गावो विवर्तिन वशानुपाः ॥ १ ७ ॥

तथैव देवता: सर्वा: कालपाशनियन्त्रिता: ।

All the Devas are controlled by the noose of Time, just as oxen are brought under control by men, by strings passed within their noses.

हर्षगोकारवो भावा निर्जित-ज्ञालसादय: ॥ १ ८ ॥

सर्वेणा सर्वेदा राजन्देहिनां देहर्शिंशतः ।

O King! Pleasure, pain, sleep lassitude, idleness, and other passions and feelings are always found inherent in every embodied being.

अभाग निर्जान: प्रोक्ता देवाशु प्रत्याकारकः ॥ १९ ॥

अभिप्रानताश्यतीनन्तेनुतगा:बवित् ।

उपतिर्धितिनागाख्या भावा वेशं नितन्तम् ॥ २० ॥

अभारसंत कंबं घाच्या निर्जीराक्य कंपु नः ।

The Devas are written down by authors in their books as not subject to death or decay; this statement is merely nominal and not real; for whoever is always subject to birth, growth, decay and death how can we call him immortal or beyond old age and death?

कंधे दुः:खाधिपूर्णा वा जायन्ते विद्योतमाः ॥ 21 ॥

कंधे देखा शक्त्वा व्यसने क्रीडनं कंधपू ।

Why do the Devas get into sorrows, and troubles? How can they be called gods? How can one enjoy when one is involved in a trouble?

क्षणातुवस्तिजाश्र इयुवतेर्जित्य संशय: ॥ 22 ॥

जलजानां च कीटानां गमकानां तथा पुन: ।

उपमा न कर्व चैघामायुधोजने मरा: स्मृता: ॥ 23 ॥

In this world, it is seen mosquitoes and other insects born from water die after a very short time; so, like these, the Devas at the expiry of their ordained life-period, die too. Then why not the Devas be treated like those insects? Why not shall we term them, "Mortal"?

ततो वर्षविश्रधारि शतावर्षायुपस्था ।

मनुष्या ह्रामरा देवास्तस्तमावृ हब्दा पर: सून्तः ॥ 24 ॥

रहस्तथा तथा विष्णु क्रमगक्ष भवन्ति हि ।

नश्यन्ति क्रमगक्षेव वर्द्धने चोत्तीसेनम् ॥ 25 ॥

Some human beings live one year; some other live upto one hundred years the Devas live longer than men; the life of the Prajāpati Brahmā exceeds those of the Devas; Rudra lives longer than

Šrimaddevibhāgavatam

Brahmā; and Viṣṇu lives longer than Rudra. All these are thus subject by turns to birth growth, and decay.

नूनं देह्वता नाणो मृतस्योरपितेच च ।

चक्रवद्यमणे राजनसर्वेणां नात्र संशयः ॥ 26 ॥

Those who are born, they die; those that die are again born. Thus O King! In this world all the Jivas, no doubt, move round and round like a wheel.

मोहजालावृतो जन्तुभूयते न कदाचन ।

मायायां विद्यानायां मोहजालां न नर्यति ॥ 27 ॥

The Jivas are deluded by the network of Moha (charms) and thus deluded. They can never gain final release. So long as Māyā exists in them, their delusion is not destroyed.

उत्स्युकाल उत्पत्ति: सर्वेषां नृप जायते ।

तथेव नागः करणाने ब्रह्मादीनां यथाकामम् ॥ 28 ॥

O King! At the time of creation Brahmā and all other things came into existence, in due order, and these will duly dissolve at the time of the Great Dissolution (the Pralaya)?

निमितं यस्तु यत्रागे स भावतितं तं नृप ।

नान्यथा नद्ववैकृतं विधिभान निर्मितं तु यत् ॥ 29 ॥

Whatever is the cause of destruction to any body here, that destroys the body in question. According to the Will Powers of the Supreme Goddess, whatever is created by Brahmā, no none can undo that. Know this as perfectly certain.

जन्माम्युजरायाशिपु:खं वा सुखमेव वा ।

ततथैव घनेत्कामं नान्यथे हविनियाँः ॥ ३० ॥

Know this the predestined law that the birth, death, old age, diseases, pain or pleasure overtake all the Jivas according to the perscribed order of Nature; never these laws fail to operate in their actions.

सर्वेधां सुखदी देवी प्रत्यक्षी शिशिभास्करो ।

न गयालित तयोः पीड़ा क्वचिन्छौरिसम्पया ॥ ३ १ ॥

See! The Devas that we see with our physical eyes, the Sun and Moon yield pleasure to all; still they suffer always troubles at the hands of their enemies (i.e. Rāhu and Ketu, i.e. there always take

place regularly solar and lunar eclipses, when they are in the ascending and descending nodes). This suffering is never removed.

भास्करस्य सुतो मन्दः क्षयी चन्दः कलंकवान् ।

पश्य राजन्विधः सूत्रं दूर्वार्च महतामपि ॥ ३ ३ ॥

The son of the Sun (Saturn) is always injurious to people; hence it is termed “Manda” (bad); the Moon was attacked with consumption and bears stain on his body (spots in the Moon disc). What to speak of ordinary men? The great men even are subject to the prescribed laws of Fate and Nature.

वेदकर्ता जगद्धतां बुद्धिद्रनु चतुर्मुखः ।

सोप्रियकिलवतांग्रापोनृष्यापुर्जीसरवतीन् ॥ 33 ॥

The Creator of the world, the four faced Brahmā is the author of the Vedas and awarder of Intelligence. He, too, on seeing Sarasvatī, his own daughter, was afflicted with passion.

शिवस्थापि मृता भार्यी सती रूपधा केलवम् ।

सोउभवदुःखसंततः कामार्तेऊ जानितां ॥ 34 ॥

When Sati, the wife of Śiva, left off Her mortal coil, Mahādeva, though He could remove the sorrows of all, was very much moved with passion and greatly afflicted.

कामानिन्दधदोहसु कालिंचां पत्ति: शिवः ।

साउपि रथामज्ञान जाता तरिद्वाधयगघुच ॥ 35 ॥

Then, being burnt very much as it were by the fire of passion, he threw himself down into the water of the river Kālindi; and the water of that river became black-coloured, as if burnt by the burning fire of the Sorrows of Śiva.

कामातों रयमाणसु ननः सोर्पि घुणोर्वम् ।

गतः प्राप्तोर्ध घपुणा शपः कामातुरो घुणम् ॥ ३६ ॥

पतत्वदीय ते लिङ़ं निर्ज्ञेजि घुणं किल ।

पयी चामुतवापी च दानवैर्पिभितं गुदे ॥ ३७ ॥

O King! When Mahādeva, being infatuated with Kāma, went into the forest of Bhrgu and becoming naked, began to copulate, the ascetic Bhrgu, seeing him in that state, exclaimed “O You are very shameless” and cursed Him thus: Let your penis drop off just now. Mahādeva, then to satisfy his

Book IV Chapter XX

thirst for passion, began to drink the water of the lake Amrta Vāpi, dug by the Dānavas.

इन्ट्रीपि च वृषो भूत्वा वाह्नत्वां गतः क्षितौ ।

आहायय वसंतोकस्य विषाणोर विवेकिनः ॥ 38 ॥

सर्वेन्त्रयं नांत कुत्र प्रघुगफितः कुतो गता ।

यदेम्पुगविज्ञानं न ज्ञातं हरिणा किल ॥ 39 ॥

Indra, too, the Lord of the Devas, turned into a bull and carried Viṣṇu on his back on the face of the earth. What to speak where the omniscience and omnipotence disappeared of Bhagavān Viṣṇu, Who is the First of all created beings and possessed of reason and discrimination? Oh! What a great wonder, that He could not know about the golden deer?

राजन्यायावलं पश्य रामो हि काममोहितः ।

रामो विरहसंतनो करोद शुभागतुः ॥ 40 ॥

योप्रच्छत्याद्वामूढः वयं गता जनकामजा ।

भक्षिता वा हता केन रुदयुचन्तरं ततः ॥ 4 1 ॥

Judge, O King! for yourself, the great power of Māyā, that even Rāma Candra was deluded by passion, and very much grieved for the separation from his dear wife Sītā, and wept much for Her. Greatly deluded, he began to cry aloud and ask the trees “where has gone My Sītā, the daughter of Janaka? Is she devoured by the rapacious animals? or whether is she stolen by some mischievous person?

लक्ष्मणाई मरिधामि कान्ताविरहूःश्रितः ।

त्वं चापि मम दुःखेन मरिष्यसि बनेउनुः ॥ 4 2 ॥

आवयोर्मर्ग जाला माता मम मार्थिति ।

गुरुभोउध्वितुःखातृः कर्थ जीवितुमहिति ॥ 4 3 ॥

O Brother Lakṣmaṇa! I am being consumed by the fire of separation from my beloved; I will commit suicide now; and you too will die due to the separation from me; our mother, too, would die when they will hear of our deaths; Śatrughna, too, will be very much afflicted at our death and will not hold his life.

सुभिमा जीवितं जहाग्यत्रयसनकर्जिता ।

पूर्णकामास्थ बैकैयी भवेत्युसमप्तिता ॥ 44 ॥

The mother Sumitrā, too, will destroy her life, being afflicted with her son's demise; and then Bharata's and his mother's desires will certainly be fulfilled.

हा सीते ब्क नगात्रसि त्वं मां बिहायु मसानुगा ।

एहोहि मुगावगशक्षि मां जीवय कुशोदरि ॥ 45 ॥

O Sita! I am very much moved by your separation; where have you gone, leaving me here! O dear-eyed, O one of thin waist! come; give life unto me!

किं करोमि वक गच्छामि वद्धीर्न च जीवितम् ।

समाशास्य दीनं मां प्रियं जनकनन्दिनि ॥ 46 ॥

What shall I do? Where shall I go? My life is entirely dependent on you, O daughter of Janaka! I am your darling? Now I am very much depressed owing to your separation. Please come and speak to me words of hope and courage.

एवं विलपता तेन रोपाणमिततेजस ।

वने वने च भ्रमता नैहिता जनकात्मजा ॥ 47 ॥

Though Rāma, of unequalled prowess, roamed about weeping from forest to forest, yet he could not fine out Sitā.

शरणय: सर्वलोकानां राम: कमललोचन: ।

गरणां वानराणां स गतो मायाशिमेहितः ॥ 48 ॥

सहायान् वानरान्कावा बकवभ बकणालयम् ।

जघान रावणं वीरं कृम्भकर्ण महोदरम् ॥ 49 ॥

He, who is the Refuge of all the worlds, the lotus-eyed Rāma, got entangled into the delusion of Māyā and had to take refuge Himself under the monkeys, and with their help, constructed the bridge across the ocean, crossed the ocean and was thus able to

kill the valiant warriors Kumbhakarna and Rāvaṇa.

आनीच च ततः सीतां गमो दिव्यमकात्यम् ।

सर्वैज्ञोप्रि हृतां मत्वा रावणेन दुर्गतमना ॥ ५० ॥

Then Rāma got back His Sītā before him but suspecting, since she had been stolen by the vicious Rāvaṇa, made her take an oath, though it is to be remembered that Rāma was all-knowing.

किं क्वीमि महाराज योगमायावलं महत् ।

यया विश्वामिदं सर्वे श्रामितं ध्रमते किल ॥ 51 ॥

Śrimaddevibhagavatam

O King! The power of Yoga Māyā is very great; what shall I speak of Her great power? This whole cosmos is always urged into activity by Her and thus goes rolling on and on incessantly.

एवं नानावतारोउर विष्णु: शापवर्श गत: ।

क्रांति विविधाश्रौ द्वैवाधानि: सदैव हि ॥ 5 2 ॥

Thus, in various incarnations, Bhagavān Viṣṇu was always under the influence of previous curse and also under the control of Destiny and had to do various functions incessantly.

त्वाहं क्रविध्यापि कृष्णयासपि विवेहितम् ।

प्रभवं मानुषे लोके देवकार्यांशसिद्धये ॥ ५३ ॥

O King! Now I will speak to you about the birth of Śrī Krṣna in the world for serving the purpose of gods and will narrate His Lilā.

कालिन्दीपुलिने रथे हासी-मधुवर्ने पुरा ।

लवणो मधुपुस्तु तत्रासीद्वावो वली ॥ 54 ॥

In days of yore, on the delightful banks of the river Kálindi, there was a place, called Madhuvana, where lived a powerful Daitya named Lavana, the son of Madhu.

द्विजानां दुःखदः पाथी बरादोन नार्थितः ।

नित्तोउसौ महाभाग लक्ष्मणास्यानुजेन वे ॥ ५५ ॥

शुक्रनेनाथ संग्रामे ते नित्रत्य मदोलकन्दम् ।

वासिता मयुगा नामी पुगी परमगोभना ॥ ५६ ॥

That wicked Demon was exceedingly arrogant, on getting a boon, and he used to give an enormous amount of trouble to the Dvias. Śatrughna, the younger of Lakṣmana, killed that uncontrollable Daitya and built a very beautiful city there and named it Mathurā.

स तन पुक्रारक्षी ई पुत्री शज्ञिपूर्वनः ।

निदेश्य राज्य मतिमाकाले प्रप्त दिवं गतः ॥ 57 ॥

The intelligent Sátrunha, the destroyer of enemies, installed his two lotus-eyed sons in that kingdom and, when his end came, went to Heaven.

सूर्यवैश्रश्वे तां तु यावादा: प्रतिवेषिति ।

मवूगं मुक्तिडां राजन व्यातितनाय: पुरा ॥ 58 ॥

Afterwards on the decline of the Solar race, the

Yādavas, born of the race of Yayāti, occupied that Mathurā city, giving salvation to all.

शूरसेनाधिधः शूरस्ताथभूमेदिर्वीपति: ।

माधुराळ्यूरसेनांशे बुधुने विषयातृष्य ॥ 59 ॥

O King! There reigned in Mathurā city one Yādava king, a valiant warrior, named Śūrasena; and he enjoyed all the pleasures of Mathurā.

तत्रोप्यः कश्यपोशः शापाच्छ वक्षाम्य वै ।

वसुदेवो5तिविख्यातः शूर्येनसुतस्तदा ॥ 60 ॥

Under the curse of Varuna, Vasudeva took his birth as the son of the renowned Sūrasena, as the part incarnate of Kaśyapa.

वैश्यवृत्तिरतः सो5प्न्युते विलिति माधवः ।

उउसेनो बमुवाच केसस्तत्यामजो महान् ॥ 61 ॥

He took up the profession of a Vaiśya and engaged himself in agriculture. And on the death of his father, the prosperous and wealthy Ugrasena became the King of Mathurā. The powerful Karīsa was the son of Ugrasena.

अदितिवेदकी जाता देवकस्य सुता तदा ।

शापाई वक्णस्याथ कक्यपानुगता किल ॥ 62 ॥

On the other hand, the King Devaka had a daughter born to him, named Devakī, the part incarnate of Aditi. She under the the curse of Varuna, followed Kaśyapa.

दत्ता सा वायुर्देवाय देवकेन महात्मना ।

विद्यार्थे रचिते तत्र वागभूग्राने तथा ॥ 63 ॥

The high souled King Devaka performed the marriage ceremony of his daughter Devaki with Vasudeva.

कंस कंस महाभाग देवकीगर्भसभ्यवः ।

अर्धास्तु सुतः श्रीमांस्व हन्ता भयिष्यति ॥ 64 ॥

When this marriage ceremony was over, a voice was heard from Heaven, saying: O furtunate Karīsa! The eighth son of this Devakī will take away your life.

तखूला वचनं कंसो विमितोउभूम्हाबलः ।

देववाचं तु तां मत्वा सत्यां चित्तमावम सः ॥ 65 ॥

The powerful Karīsa, hearing that voice from Heaven, was surprised and took it to be true and became very anxious.

Book IV Chapter XX

किं करोमीति संचिन्य विमर्गमकरोत्त्व ।

नित्तैनां न मे मृत्युभवेद्रैव सत्म्य ॥ 6 6 ॥

उपायो नान्यथा चास्मिकार्य मृत्युभयावहे ।

इयं चितृष्वसा पूज्या कर्थ हन्मीचिन्तन्त्य ॥ 67 ॥

Kañsa began to argue in his mind. Once he thought “I would to-day destroy her; then my death won’t take place; for I can’t see any other way of escape from this difficulty; again he thought, “she is my sister, daughter of my paternal uncle and therefore fit to be worshipped; how can I kill her!”

पूर्वविंशरायामास मरणं मेउस्वहो स्सा ।

पापेनापि प्रकर्तेमा देहरूषा विष्णित्धा ॥ 68 ॥

Lastly, he came to the final conclusion, thus “she is the cause of my death, though she is my sister,

fit to be worshipped; to kill her will not lead me to sin; for it is enjoyed by the wise: Do even a sin to avert one's own death."

प्रायश्धितेन पापस्य शृत्रद्धर्षिति सर्वथा ।

प्राणास्त्रा प्रकर्तेमा बुधैरधेनसा तथा ॥ 6 9 ॥

The sins can be remedied always by penances. Therefore to save one's life, by committing even a sinful act, ought to be done by the wise.

विधिन्य मनसा कंसः खड्यामादाय सत्यतः ।

जग्राह तां वरारोहां केशेष्वाकृष्य पापकृत् ॥ ७ ॥

कोशालखद्यापुङ्क्य हृतकामो दुराशयः ।

पश्यतां सर्वलोकानां नवोबां तां चकर्ष है ॥ ७ ॥ ॥

The vicious Kañsha thus arguing, holding the scabbard in his hand, drew from it the sword and dragged and caught hold of the newly married handsome woman by her to kill her before the presence of the public.

हन्यमानां च तां दृष्ट्वा हाहाकारो महानभूत ।

वसुदेवानुगा वीरा युद्धायोशकामुर्भिकः ॥ ७ 72 ॥

A cry of universal consternation and distress arose on all sides, seeing Kārīsa thus ready to kill Devaki; then the warriors, under Vasudeva, at once raised their bows and arrows, ready to fight.

भुड़ मुडौत प्रोबुस्ते ते तदादुत्साहास: ।

कृपया मोचयामासुरैदककी देवमातम् ॥ 7 3 ॥

These wonderfully valorous warriors loudly

exclaimed to Kāṃsa, “Leave Devakī at once” “Leave Devakī at once” and then they were finally able out of their mercy to release the Devamātā Devakī, from the hold of the vicious Kāṃsa.

तद्धुद्रमाभवद्धोरं वीगाणां च परस्यम् ।

वसुदेवहायानां केसेन च महात्मानां ॥ 74 ॥

वर्तमाने तथा युद्धे दारूणे लोमहरणे ।

कंसं निवारयामासुरुंद्धा चे यदुस्तमाः ॥ 75 ॥

पितृब्बसेयं ते वीर पूजनीया च बालिणा ।

न हन्तव्य त्वया वीर विवाहात्सवर्माने ॥ 76 ॥

Deadly battles ensued then between the powerful Karīsa and those valorous on Vasudeva's side.

Seeing the exceedingly terrible battle, the old Yādavas asked Karīsa to desist from such a battle and advised him thus. This Devakī is your sister; you ought to pay her respect. Did you not consider even for a moment that she is as yet a girl. O Hero! You ought not to kill her at the time of this joyous marriage ceremony.

श्रीहत्या दुःसहा वीर कांतिनी पापकृतमा ।

भृतभाषितमाजेण न कर्तेल्या विज्ञानता ॥ 77 ॥

O Valiant Warrior! The murder of a woman is intolerable! Destroyer of fame, and most heinous crime! You should also consider that learned persons ought not to commit such dastardly acts as the murdering of females, depending simply on a voice from heaven, a very ordinary thing!

अन्तर्हिंते केनापि गरुणा तव चास्य वा ।

उद्दितिंत कुतो न स्याढ्रानबंकरी विषो ॥ 78 ॥

It may be that some of your Vasudeva's enemy has uttered that harmful word, hiding himself from your sight. No reason can be shewn contrary to this.

यशसते विधाताय वसुदेवगृहस्य च ।

अरिण रचिता वाणी गुणमायाविदा नृप ॥ 79 ॥

We are of opinion that to ruin your name and to destroy the house of Vasudeva, some magician, expert in black magic, your enemy has framed this voice from Heaven.

विशेषेय विरस्वं भृत्वा भूतभूयितभायया ।

यशोमूर्तविधातार्थमुपायस्वरिणा कृतः ॥ 80 ॥

Šrimuddevibhāgavatum

O king! You are a hero; why do you fear the words of a devil. We firmly believe, there is no doubt, that this is done by your malicious enemy to ruin your name.

विद्युबसा न हतल्या विवाहसमये पुनः ।

भवितच्यं महाराज भवेच्च कथमन्यथा ॥ 8 1 ॥

O king! What is destined to take place, will take place; no one can stand against it otherwise. Therefore, at this marriage festivity, you ought never to kill this your respected sister.

एवं तैर्वीध्यमानोउसी निवृत्तो नाभवटादा ।

तदा तं वसुदेवोउपि नीतिज्ञः प्रत्यभावत ॥ 82 ॥

कॉस सत्यं ब्रवीयघु सत्यायारं जगतयम् ।

दायामि देवकीपुगन्त्यज्ञांसव सर्वेश: ॥ 83 ॥

O King Janamejaya! Though made to understand thus by the old wise Yādavas, the king Karīsa did not desist from his purpose; Śrī Vasudeva, versed in morals, told him “Karīsa! These three worlds are established on Truth. I say on Truth that I will hand over to you all my sons, born of the womb of Devakī, no sooner they are born.

जातं जातं सुतं तुध्यं न दायायाम यदि प्रभो ।

कृभपोपाक तदा घोरे पतंतु मम पूर्वज्ञः ॥ 84 ॥

And if I do not deliver to you all those sons, no sooner they are born, then all my forefathers will fall down into the hell called Kumbhipāka.

शून्याध शवर्न सर्व पौराय वे पुरःस्थितः ।

कचूले त्विततः कंसं साधु साधु पुनः पुनः ॥ 85 ॥

न मिया भाषते क्वापि वसुदेवो महाना: ।

कैंग मुंघ महाभाग स्तीह्यापातकं तथा ॥ 86 ॥

The descendants of Puru, that were present there, hearing his truthful words, praised him repeatedly and told Karīsa “Vasudeva is a high minded personage; he is surely not to tell a lie. Therefore, O Thou, blessed one! Now leave Devakī and be free from committing the murder of a woman.

एवं प्रविधितः कंसो यदुकूर्दैहानमिभः ।

क्रोधे तथस्त्वा स्थितस्तत्र सत्यवाक्यामोदितः ।

O king! Thus made to understand by the aged high minded Yādavas, the king Karīsa accepted the truthful words of Vasudeva and abandoned his anger.

ततो दुनुभयो नेवुर्वादिजाणि च सत्यम्: ॥ 87 ॥

जयग्वदस्तु सर्वेभामुपवस्तत्र संसदि ॥

Then the Dundubhis and other sounding instruments were sounded; and their sounds filled the place; and all repeatedly uttered jai, jai.

प्रसाध कंसं प्रतिमोच्य देर्वकाँ

महायशा: श्रासुतस्तदानीम् ।

जगाम गेहे स्वजानानुवृतो

नवोदया वीतभयस्तरी ॥ 88 ॥

इति श्रीमद्दैभीभगवते महापुराणे वर्जृकंकन्धे विशोभयाय ॥ 20 ॥

Then the famous Vasudeva, the son of Sūrasena, thus pleased the king Karīsa and freed Devaki; and, surrounded by his relatives, he went quickly without any fear to his own house, accompanied by Devaki.

Here ends the Twentieth Chapter in the Four Book of

Śrīmaddevībhāgavatam, the Mahā Purānam, of

18,000 verses, by Maharṣi Veda Vyāsa, on Devaki's marriage.

Šrimuddevibhāgavatum

CHAPTER XXI

On the Killing of the Sons of Devaki

व्यास उवाच

अथ काते तु सम्पाठे देवकी चैदक्षिणी ।

गर्भ द्वारा विधिवसुदयेतेन सज्ञा ॥ १ ॥

Vyāsa said: O King! Thus, in due course, Devaki, the goddess incarnate, being united according to rules with Vasudeva, became pregnant.

पूर्णर्ष द्वगभे मासे सुभुचे सुनभुत्तमम् ।

रूपावयवसम्पत्रे देवकी प्रथमं यदा ॥ 2 ॥

तदार3.6 वसुदेवस्ती सत्यावब्यानुगोपिति: ।

भात्विताच्च महाभागो देवकी देवमातरम् ॥ 3 ॥

वरोक समयं मे तं वं जानासि स्वसुतापणे ।

मोचिता तं महाभागो रणथवेन मया तदा ॥ 4 ॥

When full ten months were over, a good-looking

Book IV Chapter XXI

and beautiful child (male) was first born to Devakī. Then the good-natured Vasudeva remembered his promise, and also what is ordained by Heaven; and he spoke to Devakī, the part incarnate of Aditi “O fair-looking! You know that I saved your life at your marriage by swearing on oath to deliver all your fresh-born children to Karīsa. Now has come the time to hand over your child to Karīsa.”

इन पुरं सुकैगानते दाख्यामि भातुभूने ।

'खले कंसे विनाशार्थ दैवे कि व कार्त्विति ।'

विविचकर्माणं पाको दुर्जीय हकृततमविः ॥ 5 ॥

O good-haired woman! Now I will hand your this son to Karīsa. Know Karīsa is very cruel and wicked. I cannot say what step he will take urged on by Fate, to kill your child. O Sweet One! We have no hands in the matter. The effects of Karma are exceedingly puzzling. Ordinary persons cannot know them.

सर्वेधां कित जीवानां कालपाणानुवर्तीनान् ।

भोक्त्यं स्वकृतं कर्मं रुषं वा यदि वातुगम् ।

प्रात्प्रा सर्वैवैयाज जीवस्य विधिभिनिम्न ॥ 6 ॥

All persons are subsect to Time, the Destroyer, and enjoy the merits or demerits of their deeds. The effects of past Karma are fashioned by the Creator; knowing this, allow me to take away your child.

देवक्याच

स्वामिन्यूँ कृतं कर्म भोक्त्यं सर्वथा नृभि: ॥ 7 ॥

तीर्थेल्पोषिवर्दीवि कि न याति क्षयं ही तत ।

Devaki said: O Lord! Certainly men have to suffer fully the effects of their past Karmas. But can that not be upset by residing in holy places, practising penances and asceticism or by making generous gifts?

तिलितो धर्मशास्त्रे पायश्रितविधिनू प १ ८ १।

यूर्वार्तिज्ञानां पाणानां विनाणाय महामर्भिः ।

ब्रहाहा हेमहारी न सुपायो गृतल्पतः ॥ 9 ॥

द्वारणाद्धते चौणें गुद्धि याति यतस्ततः ।

The high minded Maharjis have fixed rules and penances for destroying the sins of past deeds;

twelve years' vow to observe penances can purify one from sine e. g., Brāhimicide, stealing gold, drinking or stealing the wife of one's preceptor and many others.

मन्यात्रिपियोंहिंट्र प्रयाश्रतं चियानतः ॥ १ ० ॥

तथा कृत्वा नर: पाणमुच्येत वा न वाउनच ।

विगतिच्चानते किं मुनवस्त्चर्विंगतः ॥ १ १ ॥

याज्ञल्क्यादय सर्वे प्रंभाश्रप्रवर्तकाः ।

O Sinless one! Will not any one be freed from their sinful effects, if they observe practices and penances as are ordained by Manu or other Munis? If you don't accept penances to be sufficiently purificatory, do you mean to say, then, that what the Maharsis, secers, Yajñavalkya and other promulgators of religious doctrines have uttered, they did so, as an act of dire falsehood and villainy?

भवित्यं भवत्ये यद्यं निशयः प्रभो ॥ 12 ॥

आयुर्वैद् स प्रियेये मन्वादास्तवाश्रस्ताः ।

O My husband! “What is in the womb of Fate will surely come to pass,” if this be taken as granted, then the whole Ayurveda (medicinal books) and Mantra vādas, the science and recitation of mantras or sacred formulae turn out utterly fruitless and false!

उद्यमन्तु वृथा सर्वमेवं चेईविनिर्मितम् ॥ 13 ॥

भवित्यं भवत्येव प्रवृत्तिस्तु निरर्थिका ।

अनिर्योमादिक व्यर्थ निवर्ते स्वर्गमाधानम् ॥ 14 ॥

यदा तत प्रमाणं हि वृथैव परिभावितम् ।

वितर्थे तत्प्रमाणे तु प्रभोच्छे: कृतो न हि ॥ 15 ॥

उद्यो च कृते सिंहिटुः प्रवर्धक्षेणे साध्यते ।

तत्सादप्र प्रकर्तेल्यः प्रपंचश्रितकल्पित्यः ॥ 16 ॥

यथायं वालकः क्षेमं प्राप्नोति मम पुक्रकः ।

If all the actions are under the control of Fate, then no effect can come out of any efforts; so all effort are reduced to no effect. If what is ordained by Heaven is to come to pass then what is the use of taking recourse to any action and Agniṣtoma sacrifices etc., that are declared to lead to Heavens. Judge! If Heaven or Fate you consider all-in-all, then the whole Vedas, the revelations from God's

Śrimaddevībhāgavatam

mouth turn out false; if the Vedas be false, then there is no reason why the whole Dharma will not be destroyed.

Note: Fate is here denounced.

मिया यदि प्रकर्तेयं वचनं शुभमिच्छता ॥ 17 ॥

न तत्र दूषणं किंचित्यबटिनं मनोषिणः ।

Now when it is seen that effects do come out whenever any exertion is made, then you ought to think out carefully and find out some means to avert danger. Therefore do you judge and find out a good way of preserving the life of this new born baby.

The learned people say that to tell a lie is not a sin, if you can there by save a life, and have honest motive for the welfare of all.

Note: Here is a diplomatic statement!

སྭུམ་ཆེན་ཆེན་

निरुभाय महापभो सत्यमेतदुब्बीम ते ॥ १ १ १ ॥

Vasudeva said: O blessed one! I now tell you what is truth and the matters connected with truth.

उपम: खलु कतिथः फलं दैवबयानुगम् ।

त्रिविधानीह कर्माणि संसारोउर पुराविदः ॥ १९ ॥

प्रवदनीते जीवानां पुराणोधामायेषु च ।

सँखितानि च जीणानि प्राक्याति सुमध्यमे ॥ २० ॥

वर्तमानाति वामोक शिवधानीह देहिनाम् ।

Effort, application and manifestation of energy are certainly the duties of man; but their effects are all under the Great Destiny or Fate. The Pundits knowing the ancient lore say that there are three kinds of Karma mentioned in the Purāṇas and Āgamas: First, the Sañcita Karma (done in past births); the Prārabdha Karma, the Karma already done; and the Vartamāna Karma (Karma in hand).

शुभागुभानि कर्माणि बीजभूतानि यानि च ॥ 21 ॥

बहुजन्ममुच्यानि काले निर्घ्ति सर्वथा ।

The Karma, auspicious and inauspicious, done in many previous lives and preserved in seed forms, remains always inherent in a human soul. Urged on by this Karma, the Jivas quitting their previous bodies, enjoy Heaven or Hell as effects of these, their own acts.

पूर्वदेंहं परित्यज्ञ जीवः कर्मवशानुगः ॥ 22 ॥

खर्गा वा नरकं वार्थि प्राप्नोति स्वकृतेन वै ।

दिख्ये देहं च सम्याय यातनादेहमर्थजम् ॥ 23 ॥

According to their good or bad works, the Jivas acquire the higher happy body and enjoy various pleasures in the Heaven, or they take up very painful vicious bodies and suffer various pains in hell.

भूनक्ति विविधान्योगान्ययं वा नरकेशवा ।

भोगान्ते च यदोत्पत्ते: समयस्तस्य जायते ॥ 24 ॥

लिंगवद्रेल सहितं जायते जीवसंजित्मा ।

तैद्व सझितेभ्यछ कर्मभ्यः कर्मिभः पुनः ॥ 25 ॥

At the expiry of the above period in Heaven or Hell, when there comes the time of his assuming another body, the Jiva becomes conscious of the subtle body (Línga Deha) and takes his birth again. When the Liūga Deha comes into existence, the part of the Karma done in various previous births that are ripe and ready to yield their fruits, gets attached to the Jiva by God (or Destiny).

योजयवेय तं कालं कांगिण प्राकृतिन च ।

देहनानेन भाव्यानि शुभानि चाजुपानि च ॥ 26 ॥

Therefore the collective effect of Karma done in previous births always exists in a Jiva's body. O Fair-eyed One!—The effects of Prārabhda Karma, ripened and ready to yield their fruits must have to be experienced by a Jiva, whether happy or unhappy.

प्राख्यान च जीवेन भोक्त्यानि सुलोचने ।

प्रावश्छितेन नग्रयिति वर्तमानाति भामिनी ॥ 27 ॥

O beautiful young woman! Penances, performed according to rules, destroy the effects of Karmas that are in hand and are weak, (i.e. not yet accumulated strongly as to remain in seed forms.)

सच्छितानि तर्वेषागु यवार्थ विंहितेन च ।

प्रायक्रमंणां भोगासंख्यो नान्यथा भवेदे ॥ 28 ॥

तेनायं ते कुमारो बे देवः कंसाय सर्वथा ।

The Prārabdha Karma, those acts out of all the previous acts done in previous births are fully mature and ready to yield their fruits) cannot be

Book IV Chapter XXI

averted; their effects must have to be experienced and then they can die away; they cannot be expiated by penances or any other remedial measures. Therefore you most hand over unconditionally your new born babe unto the hands of Karisa.

न विद्यावचनं मेप्रति लोकर्निदाधिपूत्थितम् ॥ 29 ॥

अतिवेअप्रिस्तु संसारो धर्मसोर महात्मनाम् ।

दैवाधीनं हि सर्वेषां मरणं जनन्त तथा ॥ 30 ॥

तस्माछ्कोक न कत्वेयो दैहिनां हि तिरायंकः ।

O Goddess! I have never done any blameable action, nor have I told any lic. Therefore do you fulfill your truth and hand over your baby. O Devaki! Dharma is the only thing permanent and real in this fleeting world. Even the births and deaths of high souled persons are subject to the great Destiny. Therefore the Jivas ought not to be sorry when there is no help for it.

सत्यं व्यस्य गतं कान्ते व्यथा तस्यैव जीवितम् । ३ १ ॥

इह लोकों गतो यमात्यरलोकः कृतस्ततः ।

O dear one! What shall I say to you! Know this much that his life is spent in vain who is lost to Truth. O beautiful one! Whose this life is destroyed, what can he expect in the life to come!

आगो दौहि सुतं सुषु कंसाय प्रद्याम्यहम् ॥ ३ २ ॥

सत्यसंस्तगहाँवि शुभमयो भविष्यति ।

Therefore, O Goddess! Give me your baby and I will hand it over to Karīsa. If we can observe this truth, we will meet with ample rewards afterwards; there is no doubt in it.

कत्वील सुकृतं पुंभि: सुखे दुःखे सति प्रिय ॥ ३३ ॥

“सत्यसंक्षणाहिंहि शुभायेव भविष्यति ।”

Where there are pains and pleasures for the Jivas, there it is highly incumbent on us to do good and meritorious deeds. If we can act according to Truth, we will certainly get good fruits.

व्यास उवाच

इत्युक्तवित कान्ते सा देवकी शोकसंयुता ।

द्वौ पुत्र प्रसूतं च वेभयाना मनस्विनी ॥ 34 ॥

Vyāsa said: Thus addressed by Vasudeva, the

husband of Devaki, who was very much grieved and intelligent, gave over the newly born baby, her whole body trembling, to the hands of Vasudeva.

वसुचेदोर्पि धर्माना आदाय स्वसुतं शिशुम् ।

जगाम कंससदं मार्ग लोकेशिशुतः ॥ 35 ॥

The virtuous Vasudeva took that baby and went out to the Kamsa's palace. On the way, the people, seeing him thus, were very much astonished and began to praise him.

लोका कचुः

पर्यन्तु बसुदेवं घो लोका एवं मनस्विनम् ।

स्ववाक्यमनुक्छैव बालमादाय यात्यसी ॥ 36 ॥

मृत्यवे दातुकामोउढा सत्यवागनसूयक: ।

सफलं जीवितं चाख्य धार्मं पर्यवृते चाळूतम् ॥ 37 ॥

यः पुरं याति कंसाय दातुं कालात्मयेपि हि ।

The copla said: “O people! See how Vasudeva is sensible to keep his words! He is taking his son to hand over to Karīsa. This truthful and highsouled man, free from malice, is going to give up his son to the hands of Karīsa who is the Death Personified. See his wonderful patience; this man’s life is really high, noble and true.

व्यास उवाच

इति संन्यामानन्तु प्राप्तः कंसालयं वृप ॥ 38 ॥

ददावस्यैः कुमारं तं जातामात्रामानुम् ।

Vyāsa said: O King! Vasudeva, thus praised, reached; at last the Kamsa's palace and handed over his newly-born son to Kāṃsa.

कंसोप्र विस्तयं प्राणो दूद्वा धीरं महात्मनः ॥ 39 ॥

गृहीता बालकं प्राह सिमन्तुर्पिंद्ये वचः ।

धान्यस्तं श्रुपुत्राठ जातः पुत्रसमर्पणात् ॥ 40 ॥

मम भूतुर्नं चार्यं वै निरा प्रोक्त्नु चादभः ।

न हृत्वो यथा कामं बालोयं यातु ले गृह्म ॥ 41 ॥

अधमन्तु प्रदात्यस्त्वया पुत्रो महामते ।

The King Kañsa, too, was very much astonished to see this wonderful patience of Vasudeva. Then he held aloft the child and laughed and said: "O son of Śārasena: You have been blessed to-day by giving me your son just now. But the voice from

Srimaddevibhagavatam

Heaven said that your eighth son will be the cause of my death; this your first son is not my cause of death. Therefore I will not kill this baby; you can take your baby back to your home.

O High-minded One! Let me have your eighth son brought here, when he will be born; I hope you will positively do it."

इत्युक्चा बसुदेवाय ददाव्या खल: शिशुम् ॥ 42 ॥

गच्छत्वयं गृहं बालः क्षेमं व्याहतवायूः ।

The cruel and wicked Karīsa returned the child and said: “Let this child go back safely to his home.”

तमादाय तत्न शीरितंगमा स्वगृह मुदा ॥ 43 ॥

कंसोप्री सचिवानाहं वृथा किं धातये शिशुम् ।

अध्यमादेवकीपुत्रामम मृत्युस्वहातः ॥ 44 ॥

अतः किं प्रवमं बालं द्वता पार्यं कौरामदम् ।

When the King Karisa said thus, Vasudeva, the son of Śūrasena gladly took his child back and came home. Then the King Karisa told his ministers: that the Heavenly voice told that the eighth son would be the cause of his death; and so there was no necessity to kill that child. There was no need to incur sin by killing the first child.

सभा साधिदिति तेयुक्वना संशिता मन्सिकसना:॥ 4 5 ॥

विसर्जितासु कंसेन जम्पुले स्वगृहान्यति ।

The ministers, hearing the king Kamsa's those words, began to praise him very much and exclaimed repeatedly "Well done" "Well done." They went away to their respective ho.nes, when ordered to do so by Kanisa.

गतेषु तेषु सम्पातो नारदो मुनिसत्तम: ॥ 4 6 ॥

अभ्युद्यानार्ययाद्वादि चकारोरमुस्तत्वा ।

पगच्छ कुशलं राजा ततागमनकारणम् ॥ 47 ॥

नारदस्ते तदोवाच सिमतपूर्विमेदे वचः ।

कंस कंस महाभाग गतोईं हेमपर्वतम् ॥ 48 ॥

तत्र बह्रादायो देवा मन्त्र चक्रः समाहिताः ।

देवक्यां बसुदेवस्य भावांयां सुरास्तमः ॥ 49 ॥

Now Nārada, the best of the Munis, arrived to Kaṇīsa. The king Kaṇīsa, the son of Ugrasena, stood up at once and offered him water to wash his

mouth and with green grass and rice worshipped him dejectively and enquired of his wife—He then asked the Muni about the cause of his untimely arrival there. The Maharṣi Nārada then smilingly and with sweet words repeatedly uttered “Kaṇisa,” “Kaṇisa” and then said, O blessed one! I went perchance to Sumeru Mountain. There Brahmā and other gods formed an assembly and were thus thinking out plans that Viṣṇu, the Supreme God, would take His birth in the womb of Devakī, the wife of Vasudeva to kill Kaṇisa.

वराधरै तव शिष्यूघ जनं चात्र कतिष्यति ।

तत्कथे न हतः पुत्रत्वया नीति विज्ञाना ॥ ५० ॥

Now I ask you, you are very expert as a politician; then why have you not killed the son of Vasudeva?

कंस उवाच

अष्टमं च हनिष्येड़ं मृत्युं मे देवभाषितम् ।

Karīsa said: "I will kill the eighth son according to the Heavenly Voice".

नात्र उवाच

न जानासि नृपश्रेष्ठ राजनीति शृभाशुभाम् ॥ ५ १ ॥

माघावर्न च देवानां न त्वे वैत्स वद्यामि किम् ।

Nārada said: O King! Now I understand that you do not understand any thing of politics, leading to auspicious or inauspicious results; especially when you are quite ignorant of the Māyā of the Devas, then what shall I say to you!

रिपुरलोप्रि गृहेणा नोरेश्वः शुभमिच्छता ॥ 52 ॥

सम्पेलनक्रियायां तु सर्वे ते ह्रहणाः स्मृतः ।

मूर्खल्वमरिसंलायाः कृतोर्धे जानता लवा ॥ 53 ॥

The truth is this: the warriors, looking after their own welfare, never overlook the weakest of their foes. What have you understood when the Heavenly Voice uttered: “the eighth son.” It means the children counted from the first and then finished upto eighth; it may mean first, second, third or upto eighth. Never forego your enemies: then why have you desisted in killing your enemy when you got

Book IV Chapter XXII

that enemy in your possession. Nothing is shewn of you in this act save dire foolishness, and ignorance.

इत्युक्वताउगु गतः श्रीमात्रायरे देवदर्जनः ।

गतेउव नारद केसः समाहूयाय बालकम् ।

पाणाणे पोथथामास सुरुखं प्राप च मन्त्यधीः ॥ ५४ ॥

इति शीमद्दौभाणवने महापुराणो चतुर्बिक्क्ये

एकानोबिंशोउथावः ॥ 21 ॥

Thus saying, the Maharṣī Nārada vanished quickly. Kaṃsa, of little understanding brought back the son of Vasudeva and killed him by dashing him against a stone and was relieved.

Note: This human body is a microcosm; the universe is the macrocosm. God resides in the centre and controls the two. In this human body also live the Devas and the Danavas. The left half of the body, the Idā side, is the seat of the Devas. The right half, the Pingālā side, is the seat of the Dānavas. In this body war is always going on between the Devas and Dānavas. Sometimes the Devas get victory; sometimes the Dānavas win. God is in the centre, the heart and controls the two.

Here ends the Twenty first Chapter of Śrimaddevībhāgavatam, the Mahā Purānam of 18,000 verses composed by Veda Vyāsa, on the killing of the sons of Devakī.

Book IV Chapter XXII

CHAPTER XXII

On the Part Incarnations of the Several Devas

जनमेजय उवाच

किं कृतं पातकं तेन वालकेन पितामह ।

वञ्जातमाजो निहस्तलया तेने तुदातमना ॥ १ ॥

Janamejaya asked: O grand father! What bad act did that child commit, that no sooner he was born than he was killed by Karīsa?

नारदोर्धि मुनिश्रेष्ठो ज्ञानवाभ्यांतलयः ।

कमेबेबिध्धि पार्थ कृतावन्बहावितमः ॥ २ ॥

Especially, Maharṣi Nārada is the the best amongst the Munis and foremost amongst the Brahmavids (Knowers of Brahma), always doing virtuous acts, and learned; why did he become the agent in this very sinful act?

कर्ता कार्यिता पापे तुल्यपापी स्मृती बुधीः ।

स कर्वं प्रेर्यायाम युनि: कर्म खलं तदा ॥ 3 ॥

Pundits declare that the doers and stimulators of any evil deed both are equally responsible; then how is it that Nārada, being the best of the Munis, instigated the wicked Kaṇisa to do this evil act!

संशयोर्धरं महान्पेत्र बृहि सर्वे सविसतम् ।

येन कर्मविषयकेन वालको तिथनं गतः ॥ ४ ॥

I am very much in doubt on this point. Kindly describe, in detail, the act that the child did as the result of which he had to meet with this fate of being killed.

ध्यास उवाच

नारदः कौतुकेश्री सर्वेत्रा कलहप्रियः ।

देवकावार्थीमागव सर्वेत्रच्छकार ह १। ५।।

Vyāsa said: the Devarṣi Nārada is always fond of seeing quarrels brought about amongst parties; he always likes thus to see the fun. Here specially to serve the gods' purpose he went to Kaṇīsa and incited him to such an act.

न चिष्याधायणे बुद्धिरुपैतस्य कदाचन ।

सत्यवक्ता सूरणा स कतवेय निततः शुचिः ॥ ६ ॥

Really he never intends to speak a lie; he is always truth speaking, pure hearted, and always ready to serve the gods.

एवं पद्ध बालकानेने जाता जाता निपातिताः ।

पद्ध नष्णा शापयोगेन सम्भय मरणं गताः ॥ ७ ॥

Thus the six sons were born to Devaki; and Karīsa, too, killed those six sons consecutively as they were born. These six sons named Sāgarbha, were killed just after their births, owing to their having been previously cursed.

श्रणु राजप्रवक्ष्यामि तेषां शापस्य काराणम् ।

स्वायंभुघैन्ते पुत्रा मौदोः घणमहावलः ॥ ८ ॥

O King! Hear why they were cursed before. In the reign of Sväyānbhūva Manu, were born to Urṇā, the wife of Maharṣi Marīci, the six powerful sons, all of a virtuous disposition.

Šrimaddevibhāgavatam

क्रणार्यों चैव भार्यायमासन्यर्मविचघणाः ।

बहाणं जहसुर्विध्य सुतां यथितमुचुतम् ॥ 9 ॥

शगाण तांस्तदा बहा दैत्ययोर्न विशलंच्यः ।

कालनैमिसुता जातासे चड्णभर्ग विशणरं ॥ 10 ॥

अवतारे परे ते तु हिरण्यक्रिपयोः सुता: ।

जातस्ते ज्ञानसंयुक्ताः पूर्वशापभयावृष्य ॥ 11 ॥

Once, on an occasion, the Prajāpati Brahmā, on seeing his daughter, became passionate, and was ready to hold sexual intercourse with her. At this, those six sons laughed at him. Brahmā cursed them saying “You all go quickly and take your births in the wombs of the asuras”. Therefore those six sons became the sons of Kālanemi in their first birth. At their second birth, they became the sons of Hiranyakasipu. This second time they had the fear of curse in their minds and therefore were born endowed with knowledge.

तमिमुक्समनि शनताछ तयडुकुः समाहिताः ।

तेनां प्रीते5पवद्बाद्धा घड्गाभाणां वरान्द्यै ॥ १ २ ॥

In this birth they became peaceful and, collecting all their energies, they began to practise austerities. Brahmā was pleased at this and asked the Sāgdarpha to take boons.

ब्रहोवाच

शला यूयं मया पूर्वं क्रोधयुक्तेन पुजकाः ।

तुऊसि यो महाभागा बुबैतु वाक्तिजं वरम् ॥ 13 ॥

Brahmā said: O my sons! I was, very angry to you before and cursed you; now I am very much pleased with you; ask boons from me that you all desire.

व्यास उवाच

ते तु शुत्वा वचस्त्यः ब्रह्णा: प्रीतमानसाः ।

ब्रहाणामबुद्बन्कामं सर्वे कार्यार्थतत्परा: ॥ १४ ॥

गर्भा ऊनुः

पितामहाशु तुढो5स देहि नो वाज्छतं वरम् ।

अवध्या देहतैः सर्वेमानवेश्ठ महोरैः ॥ १५ ॥

गव्यसिंस्द्वपतिभिवर्धो मापूरित्यामह ।

Vyāsa said: Hearing Brahmā's words, they were very glad and becoming very anxious to secure their

object of desire, said: O our grand sire! To-day thou art pleased unto us; now favour us with our desired boons. That we may be invulnerable to all the Devas, human beings, the big serpents the Gandharvas, and the Lord of Siddhas, (semi-divine beings supposed to be of great purity and holiness and said to be particularly characterised by eight supernatural faculties called Siddhis).

व्यास उवाच

तानुवाच तते ब्रहा सर्वेमेत्ब्रविष्यति ॥ १ १ ॥

गछन्तु वो महाप्यागाः सत्यमेव न संगयः ।

Vyāsa said: Brahmā told them “what you have asked, you would certainly get; O blessed ones! better go now; my words will be found to be literally true. No doubt in this.

दत्वा वरं तते बह्मा मुविताने तदा भवन् ॥ 17 ॥

हिरण्यकसिंगपुः: कृष्टस्तानुवाच कृष्टज्ञः ।

यस्माड्रिक्राय मां पुत्रास्तोषिते बै पितामहः ॥ 18 ॥

वरेण प्रार्थितो:त्यर्थं बलन्तवो नतो:भवन् ।

युषामिषहिंपितः स्नेहस्ततो युषामेल्याजाग्यहम् ॥ 19 ॥

यूयं रज्जेत् पाताले पडूभणां विश्रुता भृति

Granting them boons, Brahmā went away; they then became very glad. O best of Kurus! Hiranya-kāśipu began to think “my sons now have pleased the Grandsire Brahmā and are now regardless of me” and got very angry and said: You all are become very proud on account of receiving boons; and since you have ceased your good feelings towards me I also henceforth cut off my connection with you. Now better go to Pātāla; you will be known in this world as Sadgarbha.

पताले निद्राविष्टारितसहुत् बहुवल्सग्न् ॥ 2011

ततस्तु देवकीर्णर्षे वर्षे वर्षे पुनः पुनः ।

पिता च: कालेनेभेतुत तत्र कंको भविष्यति ॥ 21 ॥

स एव जातमाज्ञान् वो वधिष्यति सुदारुणः ।

At present you would be always involved in deep sleep and remain in Pātāla for many years; and when you will be born one after another in the womb of Devakī, then your father Kālanemi of previous birth will be born as Kanisa; and he would

Book IV Chapter XXII

be cruel hearted and surely kill you all, no sooner you be born.

व्यास उवाच

एवं शप्तस्तदा तेन नर्षें जातान्यून: पुनः ॥ 22 ॥

जंघान देवकीपुत्रान्यद्वारभांउछापनोदितः ।

Vyāsa said: Thus because they were cursed, they took their birth repeatedly and Karīsa, too, being urged on by the same curse, killed those sons of Devakī, the Śadgarbha, no sooner they were born.

शोशोः सदमास्तत्र देवकीभर्मन्सितः ॥ २३ ॥

विश्वसितश भयंोर्सती योगेन योगमायया ।

नतीशे गौहिणीपार्थ कृत्वा संकर्णी बलात् ॥ २४ ॥

पत्नतः पड्यो मापि लोकख्यालितं गतस्तत ।

In the seventh womb of Devakī, Ananta made his appearance. The featus in the womb was attracted by Yoga māyā and placed in the womb of Rohinī. But there was the rumour that there was miscarriage in the womb of Devakī in the fifth month; and this became known to the public.

कंसोरोपि ज्ञातलास्तर वेदकांगर्भपतान्न ॥ 25 ॥

मूंद प्राप स दुहात्मा शुत्वा वाता सुखावाहाम् ।

Kainsa came to know that there had been miscarriage. That wicked soul became exceedingly glad to hear this gladdening news.

अष्टमे देवकीर्णर्भ भगवान्साल्यतं पति: ॥ 26 ॥

उसास देवकायर्ध भागवततगाय च ।

And about this time the Bhagavān, the protector of the devotees appeared in the eighth womb of Devaki to serve the purpose of the gods and to relieve the load of the Earth.

राजोवाच

वसुदेव: कथयपांगः शेणांशस्थ तदारभवत् ॥ 27 ॥

हरेरांस्वथा प्रोक्तो भवता मुनिसनम ।

अन्य च वैउगा देवनां यत्र जातानस्तु तान्य वद ॥ 28 ॥

भारावतरणार्थे वै श्रितः प्रार्थन्यान्नय ।

The King said: O best of Munis! “You have described the part incarnations of (1) Kaśyapa as Vasudeva and (2) of Bhagavān Hari to relieve the burden of the Goddess Earth as prayed by Her; and (3) of Ananta Deva; but you have not described

the part incarnations of the other Devas. How the other Devas incarnated as their parts on this earth, kindly describe them now."

व्यास उवाच

सुराणामसुराणां च वे येउसा भृवि विश्रुतः ॥ 29 ॥

तानई संग्रवंश्यामि संबंधेपणं श्रणुष्य तान् ।

Vyāsa said: The part incarnations of Suras and Asuras on this earth, and their names I am now saying to you in brief; hear.

वसुरुदेवः कर्णवपोशे ठेवकी च तवास्टिति: ॥ 30 ॥

बलदेवस्वन्तंगो वर्तमानेयु तेषु च ।

योउसौ धर्मसुतः श्रीमात्रायण इति श्रुतः ॥ 31 ॥

तत्यांगो वासुदेवसु: विद्यामने युनी तदा ।

नरस्त्यानुजी यस्तु तत्यांगोउर्जन एव च ॥ 32 ॥

Vasudeva was the part incarnation of Káśyapa, Devakī was of Aditi, Baladeva, of Ananta; Vāsudeva Śri Krṣṇa, of Śrimān Nārāyaṇa; the son of Dharma existing even at that time in his physical body; Arjuna, of Nara, the younger brother of Nārāyaṇa.

युधिष्ठिरनु धर्मशील धार्थशेणी धामु इन्छुत ।

अधिश्यंशी ततः प्रोन्ती माठीयुगे महावृत्ति ॥ 33 ॥

Yudhiştihra was part incarnate of Dharma, Bhimasena, of Vāyu, the powerful twins of Mādri, Nakula and Sahadeva, of Asvinīkumāras?

सूर्याश: कर्ण आख्यातो धर्माशिवुः स्युतः ।

ग्रोगो बृह्मयनेरांशस्तमुत् शिवांगजः ॥ 34 ॥

The valiant hero Karna, born of Kunti, was part incarnate of the Sun, and the high minded Vidura, the knower of the Supreme Essence, was incarnate of Yama, the king Dharmarāja. Drona, the Ācārya of the Kurus and the Pāṇḍavas was the part incarnate of Bhaspati; and his son Asvāthamā was part incarnate of Rudra Deva.

सपूत्र: श्रोन्तुः प्रोक्तो गंगा भायां मता बुधे: ।

देवकन्तु समाख्यातो गन्यवंपतिरामे ॥ 35 ॥

Śantanu was the part incarnate of the Ocean; his wife, of the river Ganges in human form. It is stated in the Purāṇas that the king Devaka was part incarnate of the Lord of Gandharvas.

वसुभीष्मो विराटसु महज्ञा इति स्युतः ।

अर्षिष्य सुत्तो हौमो धृतगाषुः प्रकीर्तिनः ॥ ३६ ॥

महज्ञः कृपः प्रोफः: कृतवमां तथापर: ।

दुर्गंधनः कलेशंशः शकुनिं विद्ध द्वापरम् ॥ ३७ ॥

सोमपुत्र: सुवर्चिखः: सोमप्रकृतदाहतः ।

पावकांगीः पृष्टयुणः शिखडी राक्षसस्तथा ॥ ३८ ॥

सनत्कुमारस्थांगस्तु प्रयुन्तः परिक्षितिः ।

दूघदो वकणास्यांगे त्रीयदी च रासंगजा ॥ ३९ ॥

त्रीयदीतनया: पछ्र विश्वेश्वांगना: स्मृता ।

कुन्ति: सिद्धिद्वितिमरी मतिणाधिरारजना ॥ 40 ॥

कृष्णान्य-संस्था सर्वे देववारांगना: स्मृता ।

राजान्ध तथा सर्वे असुरा: शकनीदिता: ॥ 41 ॥

The Grand-father of the Kauravas, the foremost of the heroes, Bhīsma Deva was the incarnate of Vasu; Virāta, the Lord of Matsya was the part incarnate of Maruts; Dhṛtarāstra, of the Daitya Harīsa, the son of Arīsta Nemi; Krpa and Krta Varmā, of Maruts; Duryodhana, of Kali and Śakuni, of Dvāpara; Suvarcākhya Somapraru, of the son of the Moon; Dhṛṣtadyumna was part incarnate of Fire and Śikhandī of Rāksasa; Pradyumna was part incarnate of Sanatkumāra; the king Drupada was part incarnate of Varuna; Draupadī, of Lakṣmī; Draupadī's five sons, of Viśve-Devas; Kuntī was incarnate of Siddhi; Mādri, of Dhṛti; Gāndhāri, of Mati; the wives of Śri Kṛṣṇa were the heavenly public women; thus all the Devas came as their part incarnations, urged on by Indra.

हिरण्यकशिपोरेश: शिशुपाल उद्राहत: ।

वियवितर्जसाम्ब: शल्य: प्रब्राह द्रवपि ॥ 42 ॥

कालनैमित्रला कंसः केशी हवशिशास्त्रला ।

अग्रिष्टे बलिपुसन्त कुकृची गोकुले हतः ॥ 43 ॥

Amongst the Asuras, Śiśupāla was the incarnate of Hiranyakaśipu; Jarāsandha, of Vipracitti, Śalya, of Prahlāda; Karīsa, of Kālanemi and Keśī, of Haya Sīrā. The Asura named Arīṣṭa of the form of a cow that was killed by Krṣṇa was the son of Bali.

अनुहादो ध्युकेतुर्भगतानोय आक्सलः ।

लम्बः प्रणंवः संजातः खरोःसीधेनुको5भवत्। 44 ॥

Dhṛṣṭaketu was part incarnate of Anuhrādhā, Bhagadatta, of Bāskala; Pralamba, of Lamba; Dhenuka, of Khara.

वाराख्छ क्रिरोष्ठ धैल्पो परमदारुणी ।

मल्ली तावेव संजाती ख्याती चाण्यपुधिको ॥ 4 5 ॥

Cānūra and Muṣṭika, the two athletes, were part incarnates of Vārāha, and Kiśora, the two dreadful Daityas.

दितिपुजसत्धास्टिषो गजः कुकल्याधिधः ।

बलिपुजी बकी खाता बकस्तदनुः म्यूत: ॥ 46 ॥

यमो क्रतरला कामः क्रोधश्रवे चतुर्धकः ।

तेघामंगैस्तु संजातो इंगणपुत्रे महावलः ॥ 47 ॥

Kuvalaya, the elephant of Kañsha, was part incarnate of Arīṣṭa, the son of Diti. Vaki was the daughter of Bali, Vaka was her younger. The powerful son of Droṇa, Āsvatthāmā, though known as the part incarnate of Rudra, was really born of the four parts of Yama, Rudra, Cupidity and Anger.

अंगवतरणे पूर्वैतैयता राक्षसात्सा ।

जाता: सर्वे सुरांगास्ते ब्रितिभागतवताणे ॥ 48 ॥

पूतेषां कथितं राजनंशानतरणं नृप ।

सुराणां चासुराणां च पुराणेपु प्रकर्पितेन् ॥ 49 ॥

The Daityas and Rāksasas that were born to relieve the heavy burden of the Earth were all incarnates of Asuras. O king! I have thus narrated to you in order the incarnations of the Suras and Asuras, as they are stated duly in the Purāṇas.

यदा बढ़ायदो देवा: प्रार्थनार्थ हरि गतः ।

हरिणा स तदा दर्ती केशी खलु सितासिति ॥ 50 ॥

श्यामवर्णैततः कृष्ण: श्रोतः संकर्पणस्तथा ।

भारावतरणार्थ ती जातो देवांशमम्बी ॥ 51 ॥

When Brahma and the other Devas went to Visnu and prayed to Him, then Hari gave to Brahma one hair of a black colour and one hair of a white colour. The Bhagavān Sī Krṣna was born of that black hair and Sankarṣana Baladeva was born of the white hair. They were both the incarnations of Visnu.

Note. Here the black is the younger and the stronger; and they also represent the polarities. The Jivas are points of those hairs.

Book IV Chapter XXIII

<table><tr><td>अंशावतरणं चैतच्छूणोत भक्तिभावतः ।</td></tr><tr><td>सर्वेभपातिविमुक्तो मोते स्वजनैवृतः ॥ 52 ॥</td></tr><tr><td>इति श्रीमदैभागवते महापुराणो चतुर्बैकन्वे</td></tr><tr><td>द्वाविंशो5.धाया: ॥ 22 ॥</td></tr></table>

He who hears with devotion the story of these part incarnations, becomes freed of all sins and

passes away his time merrily, surrounded by his circle of friends; there is no doubt in this.

Thus ends the Twenty second Chapter in Fourth Book of Śrimaddevibhāgavatam, the Mahā Purānam, of 18,000 verses by Maharṣī Veda Vyāsa on the part

incarnations of the several Devas.

Book IV Chapter XXIII

CHAPTER XXIII

On the Birth of Śri Kṛṣṇa

व्यास उवाच

हतेयु घटसु घुपेयु देवकवा औग्रमेसिना ।

समये पत्ति गर्भे वचनारावरस्य च ॥ १ ॥

अधमस्य च गर्भस्य रक्षणार्थमतिन्रतः ।

प्रयत्नकारोज्ञा मरणं स्वं विवित्यन्त्र ॥ २ ॥

Vyāsa said: The six sons of Devaki being thus killed by Kānsa and the seventh womb being miscarried, Kānsa began, when Devaki was pregnant for the eighth time, to think of the cause of his own death, as advised by Nārada and watched carefully, day and night, so that this time there would be born a son and there be no miscarriage.

समये देवकीर्णपं प्रवेशमकेतोद्धरि: ।

अंगेन वसुदेवे तु समागन्य यथाकमम् ॥ 3 ॥

On the other hand the Bhagavān Hari came down and rested on the body of Vasudeva as part incarnate and then entered into the womb of Devaki, in due order.

तदेवं योगाया च यगोदायां यधेष्छया ।
